### [1] [On the X-ray emission from massive star clusters and their evolving superbubbles II. Detailed analytics and observational effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/0903.1678)
*G. A. Añorve-Zeferino,G. Tenorio-Tagle,S. Silich*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文构建了超星团（super star cluster）与星际介质（ISM）相互作用的综合性X射线图景，通过解析模型比较并合并超风（superwind）与风吹气泡（wind-blown bubble）的X射线辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为全面刻画超星团与星际介质之间的相互作用，需要将超风驱动的X射线辐射与风吹气泡的辐射进行对比与整合，从而获得统一的X射线图景。

Method: 提出快速多方超风（polytropic superwind）的流体力学与X射线光度解析模型，作为系列论文Paper I结果的扩展；多方特性可参数化辐射冷却等多种效应。同时推导适用于所有考虑热蒸发（thermal evaporation）气泡模型的X射线性质，并解析计算各组分的表面亮度（surface brightness）与加权温度（weighted temperature）。模型显式依赖金属丰度（metallicity）并采用氢与金属分开贡献的真实X射线发射率（emissivity）。

Result: 获得了对金属丰度有显式依赖、并允许发射率作为流体力学变量一般函数的X射线模型，可分别给出表面亮度与加权温度的解析预期值，形成整合超风与气泡的X射线图景。

Conclusion: 论文以模型与观测数据的比较作为收尾，用于检验上述解析X射线图景的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we present a comprehensive X-ray picture of the interaction between a super star cluster and the ISM. In order to do that, we compare and combine the X-ray emission from the superwind driven by the cluster with the emission from the wind-blown bubble. Detailed analytical models for the hydrodynamics and X-ray luminosity of fast polytropic superwinds are presented. The superwind X-ray luminosity models are an extension of the results obtained in Paper I of this series. Here, the superwind polytropic character allows to parameterize a wide variety of effects, for instance, radiative cooling. Additionally, X-ray properties that are valid for all bubble models taking thermal evaporation into account are derived. The final X-ray picture is obtained by calculating analytically the expected surface brightness and weighted temperature of each component. All of our X-ray models have an explicit dependence on metallicity and admit general emissivities as functions of the hydrodynamical variables. We consider a realistic X-ray emissivity that separates the contributions from hydrogen and metals. The paper ends with a comparison of the models with observational data.

</details>


### [2] [A note on oscillons: non-negligible metric fluctuations during preheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21578)
*Daniel del-Corral,Paolo Gondolo,K. Sravan Kumar,João Marto*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文检验了暴胀后预热（preheating）与振荡子（oscillon）形成的主流格点模拟方案（固定FLRW背景）与广义相对论（General Relativity）的自洽性，发现其忽略的度规扰动在预热阶段不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流格点方法在空间均匀的FLRW背景上演化非均匀暴胀子场，膨胀由体积平均能量密度驱动，且忽略度规扰动。作者旨在检验这一近似在广义相对论下的有效性。

Method: 利用线性化爱因斯坦约束方程（linearized Einstein constraints），推导ADM哈密顿约束（ADM Hamiltonian constraint）的固有体积平均，并与格点模拟所用的Friedmann方程对比；同时对Starobinsky与α-attractor模型进行数值验证。

Result: 慢滚暴胀中Bardeen势（Bardeen potential）的压制在预热阶段消失：一旦第一慢滚参数达到量级1，度规与暴胀子涨落进入同一微扰阶；忽略度规涨落会漏掉标量场方程的主导阶贡献。精确的平均约束还额外包含空间曲率、局部膨胀方差、剪切及度规对局域能量密度的修正。数值上，度规贡献在放大阶段可与标量场贡献相当。

Conclusion: 这些结果直接影响振荡子形成的标准格点描述，说明固定FLRW方案不自洽，并支持将数值相对论（numerical relativity）作为非线性预热问题的自洽框架。

Abstract: The dominant lattice approach to post-inflationary preheating and oscillon formation evolves an inhomogeneous inflaton field on a spatially homogeneous Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker background, whose expansion is sourced by a volume-averaged energy density, while metric perturbations are neglected. We examine the consistency of this approximation with General Relativity. Using the linearized Einstein constraints, we show that the suppression of the Bardeen potential, characteristic of slow-roll inflation, disappears during preheating: once the first slow-roll parameter becomes of order unity, the metric and inflaton fluctuations enter at the same perturbative order. We further show that the scalar-field equation evolved in the fixed-FLRW prescription omits leading-order contributions generated by metric fluctuations. We then derive the proper-volume average of the ADM Hamiltonian constraint and show that it does not reduce to the Friedmann equation used in lattice simulations: the exact averaged constraint contains additional contributions from the spatial curvature, the variance of the local expansion, the shear, and metric corrections to the local energy density. Finally, we illustrate numerically, for Starobinsky and $α$-attractor models, that the metric contribution becomes comparable to the scalar-field contribution during the amplification stage. These results directly affect the standard lattice description of oscillon formation and motivate numerical relativity as a consistent framework for the nonlinear preheating problem.

</details>

### [3] [Metallic Néel order stabilized by coupling between inequivalent Hubbard layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20922)
*Yi-Ming Wu,Jessica Yiqi Pan,Hong Yao,Steven A. Kivelson*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用无限制Hartree-Fock近似研究两层不等价掺杂方形晶格Hubbard模型的基态相图，发现强层间耦合可破坏条纹序并产生具有空穴口袋的重构费米面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受多层（n≥3层）铜氧化物超导体近期ARPES实验启发，探究层间耦合在多层铜氧化物中可能带来的新物理。

Method: 采用无限制Hartree-Fock近似（unrestricted Hartree-Fock approximation），研究两个耦合且掺杂不等价的方形晶格Hubbard层。

Result: 在解耦层极限下，轻空穴掺杂基态通常为非公度自旋条纹态（incommensurate spin-stripe state）；当层间耦合足够强时，条纹序相对于公度Néel有序金属（commensurate Néel-ordered metal）被破坏，所得态表现出以(±π/2,±π/2)为中心的空穴口袋（hole pockets）重构费米面，与实验观测特征相似。

Conclusion: 结果表明多层铜氧化物中层间耦合可产生定性上全新的物理。

Abstract: Inspired by recent ARPES studies on multilayer ($n\geq3$ layers) cuprate superconductors, we use the unrestricted Hartree-Fock approximation to explore the ground-state phase diagram of two coupled, inequivalently doped square-lattice Hubbard layers. In the decoupled-layer limit, the lightly hole-doped ground state is typically an incommensurate spin-stripe state. However, with sufficiently strong interlayer coupling, stripe order is destabilized relative to a commensurate Néel-ordered metal. The resulting state exhibits a reconstructed Fermi surface with hole pockets centered at $(\pmπ/2,\pmπ/2)$ that are similar in character to those seen in experiments. Our results illustrate the qualitatively new physics that can arise from interlayer coupling in multilayer cuprates.

</details>


### [4] [Smectic Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20932)
*Grayson R. Frazier,Erez Berg*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在二维 Rashba 超导体（Rashba superconductor）与易面铁磁序（easy-plane ferromagnetic order）相邻的体系中，配对凝聚体的软非幺正涨落（soft nonunitary fluctuations）可作为超导 U(1) 相位的涌现规范场，压制相位相干并驱动系统进入“近晶型”（smectic）超导态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于阐明超导序与易面铁磁序相互交织（intertwined orders）时相位相干（phase coherence）如何被重塑，并关联到过渡金属硫族化合物衬底（transition-metal dichalcogenide substrate）邻近的菱面体石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）体系。

Method: 方法上通过识别自旋极化经 Lifshitz 不变量（Lifshitz invariant）与超电流的线性耦合，将其视为超导 U(1) 相位的涌现规范场（emergent gauge field），并对软磁涨落积分以重整化有效超流刚度（effective superfluid stiffness）。

Result: 结果显示，积分掉软磁涨落后系统进入具有有限动量螺旋相位织构（finite-momentum helical phase texture）的近晶型超导态，其长波刚度在一个方向上消失，使涡旋能量代价不再对数发散，从而在任意有限温度下破坏准长程超导序。弱晶格各向异性（lattice anisotropy）钉扎铁磁序并截断长波近晶行为，恢复有限有效刚度；而在磁序起始附近，增强的磁化率（magnetic susceptibility）导致任意弱磁电耦合下出现涡旋解禁闭（vortex-deconfined）区。

Conclusion: 结论指出，交织的超导序与易面铁磁序可重塑相位相干，该机制为相关材料平台中的相位行为提供了理解框架。

Abstract: We identify a mechanism in which soft nonunitary fluctuations of the pair condensate can suppress phase coherence in a two-dimensional Rashba superconductor proximate to easy-plane ferromagnetic order. This spin polarization couples linearly to the supercurrent through a Lifshitz invariant, acting as an emergent gauge field for the superconducting $\mathrm{U}(1)$ phase. Integrating out soft magnetic fluctuations renormalizes the effective superfluid stiffness and drives the system into a "smectic" superconducting state, characterized by a finite-momentum helical phase texture and a long-wavelength stiffness that vanishes in one direction. Consequently, vortices no longer have a logarithmically divergent energy cost, and the quasi-long-range superconducting order is destroyed at any finite temperature. Weak lattice anisotropy pins the ferromagnetic order, cutting off the smectic-like behaviour at long wavelengths and thereby restoring a finite effective stiffness. Nonetheless, near the onset of magnetic order, the enhanced magnetic susceptibility leads to a vortex-deconfined regime for arbitrarily weak magnetoelectric coupling. The results provide insight into how intertwined superconducting and easy-plane ferromagnetic orders can reshape phase coherence, with relevance to rhombohedral graphene proximitized by a transition-metal dichalcogenide substrate.

</details>


### [5] [Digital-analog concept for superconducting perceptron-like neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21613)
*Andrey E. Schegolev,Vsevolod I. Ruzhickiy,Georgy I. Gubochkin,Alexander S. Ionin,Ivan A. Nazhestkin,Mikhail Y. Fominskii,Lyudmila V. Filippenko,Igor I. Soloviev,Maxim V. Tereshonok,Nikolay V. Klenov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种混合数字-模拟（digital-analog）架构，将数字单通量量子（SFQ）通信与紧凑模拟非线性处理相结合，用于实现超导人工神经网络。作者设计并表征了基于Σ-神经元（Σ-neuron）的数模数（DAD）转换器，并通过电路级与系统级仿真及MNIST分类实验验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导人工神经网络需要兼顾数字通信与高效非线性处理，而混合数字-模拟架构是实现该目标的一条有前景的路径。研究旨在验证以Σ-神经元为核心的DAD转换器作为感知机类神经网络基本功能块的可行性。

Method: 通过电路级仿真考察SFQ脉冲序列编码的输入值如何转换为模拟电平、经Σ-神经元非线性变换后再映射回脉冲输出；实际制备并表征了重新设计的Σ-神经元，提取其类S形（sigmoid-like）传输特性并纳入系统级仿真，同时分析DAC、神经元与ADC模块之间的工作范围匹配要求。

Result: 实测Σ-神经元呈现适合激活函数实现的类S形传输特性；基于该实测特性与数学S形激活分别构建两种感知机网络，在MNIST手写数字数据集上分别达到97.0%与91.9%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果界定了DAC、神经元与ADC各功能块的工作范围匹配条件，支持该接口作为具有数字输入输出的感知机类超导神经网络构建单元的可行性；实测神经元特性引入的性能差异表明器件真实参数对系统精度存在影响。

Abstract: A promising route to superconducting artificial neural networks is a hybrid digital-Analog architecture that combines digital single-flux-quantum (SFQ) communication with compact Analog nonlinear processing. The study focused on the dynamic conversion of a discrete signal passing through a digital-to-Analog-to-digital (DAD) converter, in which the role of the Analog cell was performed by a $Σ$-neuron with a nonlinear transfer function -- the basic cell of perceptron-like neural networks. Furthermore, the DAD converter, the elementary functional block of the hybrid architecture, combines a digital-to-analog converter (DAC) and an Analog-to-digital converter (ADC), and re-encodes the Analog $Σ$-neuron waveforms as an SFQ pulse sequence. Circuit-level simulations demonstrate how input values encoded by SFQ pulse trains are converted into analog signal levels, transformed by the $Σ$-neuron, and mapped back to pulse-based outputs. As a key experimental step, we fabricated and characterised a redesigned $Σ$-neuron and measured a sigmoid-like transfer characteristic suitable for activation-function implementation. The extracted response was incorporated into system-level simulations to assess the influence of realistic device parameters on the conversion process. We delineate the operating-range matching requirements for the DAC, neuron, and ADC blocks, supporting the feasibility of the proposed interface as a building block for perceptron-like superconducting neural networks with digital inputs and outputs. Finally, we developed two perceptron networks, one using a mathematical sigmoid activation and the other the measured $Σ$-neuron transfer characteristic, which reached classification accuracies of $97.0\%$ and $91.9\%$, respectively, on the MNIST handwritten digit dataset.

</details>


### [6] [Collective excitation-mediated transport in nanoscale Josephson junctions that exhibit quantum confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21725)
*Zhengyuan Liu,Sebastian Scherb,Werner M. J. van Weerdenburg,Daniel Wegner,Nadine Hauptmann,Alexander A. Khajetoorians*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文研究库仑阻塞（Coulomb blockade）区纳米约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）叠层中的局域隧穿输运，发现由结内禀集体激发模式（collective excitation mode）显著增强准粒子（quasiparticle）输运，并表征其能量与库仑能隙的层依赖调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子限域（quantum confinement）可强烈改变约瑟夫森结的输运行为，但库仑阻塞区中兼具强垂直量子限域的纳米结叠层内隧穿机制尚待厘清。

Method: 在库仑阻塞区对纳米尺度约瑟夫森结叠层进行局域隧穿输运测量，其中金属层表现出强垂直量子限域；量化集体模式能量与库仑能隙，并施加机械运动与外加磁场等扰动考察其响应。

Result: 准粒子输运被一种结内禀、局域于孤立金属覆盖层的集体激发模式显著增强；集体模式能量与库仑能隙均呈现强层依赖调制，与底层量子阱态（quantum well states）的调制一致；该集体激发对机械运动与外加磁场等扰动有响应。

Conclusion: 该集体模式对费米能级（Fermi level）附近的准粒子敏感，可能作为超导态（superconducting state）的间接探针。

Abstract: Quantum confinement can strongly modify transport through Josephson junctions. Here, we study local tunneling transport through nanoscale Josephson junction stacks in the Coulomb blockade regime, where the metallic layers exhibit strong vertical quantum confinement. We find that quasiparticle transport is strongly enhanced by a collective excitation mode intrinsic to the junction and localized in the isolated metallic overlayer. We quantify both the collective-mode energy and the Coulomb gap and show that both exhibit strong layer-dependent modulation, consistent with the modulation of the underlying quantum well states. We further investigate how the collective excitation responds to various perturbations, including mechanical motion and an applied magnetic field. Our results suggest that this collective mode is sensitive to quasiparticles near the Fermi level and may therefore provide an indirect probe of the superconducting state.

</details>


### [7] [Universal Dzyaloshinski-Moriya interaction dictates pairing in unconventional superconductor families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21731)
*Baishun Yang,Yida Chu,Xuelei Sui,Haiqing Lin,Shijie Hu,Bing Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究指出，局域反演对称性破缺产生的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用（DMI）是铜氧化物、铁基与镍基超导体的共同要素，可统一解释非共线磁性与超导行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共线反铁磁自旋涨落范式（collinear-antiferromagnetic spin-fluctuation paradigm）长期主导非常规超导研究，但无法调和铜氧化物、铁基与镍基超导体中普遍观测到的非共线自旋现象，因而需要新的统一图像。

Method: 采用大规模第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与无偏大尺度密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）模拟，系统考察 DMI 在这些超导家族中的作用及其与掺杂的耦合。

Result: DMI 可统一解释母体化合物中的非公度序（incommensurate orders）、自旋波能隙（spin-wave gaps）与非共线自旋结构；空穴掺杂下强 DMI 驱动自旋涡旋与 π 相移空穴条纹合并，形成涡旋-空穴杂化条纹相，稳定电荷序并支持而非抑制超导；电子掺杂下涡旋则钉扎空穴并抑制长程超导。

Conclusion: DMI 被确立为非共线磁性与超导之间的统一纽带，空穴-条纹-涡旋耦合被识别为微观配对引擎，对主流配对机制提出挑战，并提供可实验检验的材料优化路线图。

Abstract: The collinear-antiferromagnetic spin-fluctuation paradigm has long guided unconventional superconductivity research, yet fails to reconcile the noncollinear spin phenomena observed across cuprates, iron-based superconductors, and nickelates. Using extensive first-principles calculations and unbiased large-scale DMRG simulations, we show that Dzyaloshinski-Moriya interaction (DMI)-arising from local inversion-symmetry breaking-is a common ingredient across these families. This DMI unifies hallmark observations in parent compounds-incommensurate orders, spin-wave gaps, and noncollinear textures. Under hole doping, strong DMI drives spin vortices to merge with pi-shifted hole stripes, forming hybrid vortex-hole stripe phases. These phases stabilize charge order while supporting, not suppressing, superconductivity. By contrast, under electron doping, these vortices pin holes and suppress long-range superconductivity. Our results establish DMI as a unifying link between noncollinear magnetism and superconductivity, identifying hole-strip-vortex coupling as a microscopic pairing engine. Given that DMI is common across major superconductor families, these findings challenge the prevailing pairing mechanism and offer an experimentally testable roadmap for materials optimization.

</details>


### [8] [Topological properties and gap structure of the paired state in twisted TMD bilayers within a two-band effective model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21825)
*Palash Saha,Michał Zegrodnik*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于扭角过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）双层的平带拓扑超导实验现象，采用有效两带 Kane-Mele 类模型并结合哈特里-福克（Hartree-Fock）平均场处理库仑排斥与格点间配对相互作用，分析实空间配对下的超导能隙对称性及其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验显示，扭角过渡金属硫族化合物（twisted transition metal dichalcogenide, TMD）双层平带拓扑体系中可涌现超导电性，促使开展相应的理论研究以刻画该体系非平庸能带拓扑对超导态的影响。

Method: 采用有效两带 Kane-Mele 类模型（Kane-Mele-like model）描述体系非平庸能带拓扑，并引入库仑排斥（Coulomb repulsion）与格点间配对相互作用（inter-site pairing interactions），在哈特里-福克平均场（Hartree-Fock mean-field）层面进行处理；在实空间配对（real-space pairing）假设下分析超导能隙对称性。

*Francesco Geroni,Roberto Paoli,Riccardo Massidda,Giacomo Tommei*

TL;DR: 面向单夜甚短弧（very short arc, VSA）初轨确定（initial orbit determination, IOD）难题，本文比较两种直接求解测距问题的神经网络（neural network, NN）模型：纯数据驱动多层感知机（multi-layer perceptron, MLP）与引入物理约束损失（physics-informed loss）的版本，并用“动力学可容许性”而非仅误差来评估其输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法（如高斯法、拉普拉斯法）要求角观测覆盖轨道的足够比例，在仅有单夜观测时病态甚至完全失效，而这对大多数新发现的近地天体（near-Earth object, NEO）是常态。因此需要能直接从甚短弧（VSA）恢复轨道状态向量的方法。

Method: 两种模型均以单条 VSA 的三组带时标角观测 $(t_i,\alpha_i,\delta_i)$（$i=1,2,3$）为输入，预测地心距离与距离变化率 $(\rho,\dot\rho)$ 以补全轨道状态向量；其一为纯数据驱动目标训练的 MLP，其二加入物理约束损失。二者在目标不相交划分下，基于 NEODyS-2 星表中 $39\,031$ 个真实 NEO 的 $568\,127$ 条 VSA 样本训练。

Result: 在 $56\,773$ 条弧的留出测试集上，物理约束模型将 $85.1\%$ 的预测置于可容许区域（admissible region），数据驱动基线为 $76.5\%$；而高斯法与拉普拉斯法仅分别对 $45.0\%$ 与 $47.3\%$ 的弧给出解，且其中约十分之九收敛到退化根。按自行（proper motion）与真实距离分层分析显示，物理约束目标并非全面占优：它以更长的误差尾部换取可容许性，并在运行上最相关的快速、近距离弧段上具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 两模型按构造对每条弧均有输出，故评估重点由“是否有解”转向“估计是否动力学可容许”。结果表明物理约束损失提升可容许比例并显著改善快速近距弧段表现，但需在误差分布与可容许性之间作出权衡。

Abstract: Initial Orbit Determination (IOD) from Very Short Arcs (VSAs) remains one of the most challenging open problems in asteroid surveillance and celestial mechanics. Classical methods require angular observations spanning a sufficient fraction of the orbit, and become ill-conditioned or fail outright when only a single observing night is available, as is the case for most newly discovered Near-Earth Objects (NEOs). We present a comparative analysis of two Neural Network (NN) models that attack the ranging problem directly: both ingest a triplet of time-tagged angular measurements $(t_i,α_i,δ_i)$, $i=1,2,3$, from a single VSA and predict the geocentric range and range-rate $(ρ,\dotρ)$, thus completing the orbital state vector. The first is a Multi-Layer Perceptron trained on a purely data-driven objective; the second augments it with a physics-informed loss. Both are trained, under an object-disjoint partition, on a sample of the $568\,127$ VSAs of $39\,031$ real NEOs available in the NEODyS-2 catalogue. Each model returns an estimate on every arc by construction, so we assess instead whether that estimate is dynamically admissible: on a held-out test set of $56\,773$ arcs the physics-informed model places $85.1\%$ of its predictions inside the admissible region, against $76.5\%$ for the data-driven baseline, whereas Gauss's and Laplace's methods return a solution on only $45.0\%$ and $47.3\%$ of the same arcs and collapse onto the degenerate root in about nine of those cases out of ten. A stratified analysis over proper motion and true range shows that the physics-informed objective is not uniformly superior to the baseline: it trades a longer error tail for admissibility and for a marked advantage on the fast, nearby arcs that are operationally the most relevant.

</details>

As a concrete realization, we consider a gauged U(1) ${B-L}$ extension of the Standard Model. The phenomenology of the framework is essentially controlled by two free parameters: the U(1) ${B-L}$ gauge coupling $g_{BL}$ and the $Z^\prime$ boson mass $m_{Z^\prime}$. For a fixed $m_{Z^\prime}$, the observed dark matter abundance requires the dark matter mass to be near the $Z^\prime$ resonance, $m_{\rm DM} \sim m_{Z^\prime}/2$, and sets a lower bound on $g_{BL}$. Complementarily, LHC searches for a $Z^\prime$ resonance set an upper bound on $g_{BL}$. The parameter space allowed by the dark matter abundance and LHC constraints can account for the recent LZ event. The synergy between future $Z^\prime$ resonance searches at the High-Luminosity LHC and the LZ experiment may provide a test of this framework.

Method: 采用 on-shell 方法（on-shell methods），为每个算符 $\mathcal{O}$ 赋予权重 $(w,\bar w)=(\ell-h,\ell+h)$，其中 $\ell$ 与 $h$ 分别表示其最小构型的粒子数与总螺旋度（total helicity）。在缺少非全纯 Yukawa 耦合（non-holomorphic Yukawa couplings）的前提下，分析 $L$ 圈阶算符混合 $\mathcal{O}_j\to\mathcal{O}_i$ 的消失条件。

Abstract: The manuscript provides a description of a theoretical analysis of a non-Newtonian Prandtl fluid subject to peristaltic flow through an inclined asymmetric channel. The model also includes thermodynamic aspects such as heat diffusion (the Soret effect) and viscous dissipation resulting from wall-fluid slip conditions, which may help optimize medical devices such as lab-on-a-chip systems and dialysis machines. In this study, the concentration of a generic chemical, temperature, and fluid velocity are taken into account through mass, heat, and momentum balances, respectively. The solution is approximated using numerical techniques suitable for long wavelengths (low frequency) and low Reynolds numbers. The study also discusses trapping phenomena, which are crucial from a clinical point of view. The developed insights can improve the understanding of physiological flows in the gastrointestinal tract and blood vessels. By understanding how the fluid moves and how particles are trapped, these insights may contribute to the design of improved medical pumps and artificial organs. Graphical visualizations are provided for the fluid velocity profile, temperature distribution, and concentration of a generic chemical. Furthermore, the numerical results are validated through comparison with a closed-form solution from a benchmark problem.

Abstract: Photon-to-Digital Converters (PDCs) are photosensors with single photon resolution and large dynamic range that digitise the output of individual single-photon avalanche diodes directly on-chip, offering potential advantages with respect to analogue Silicon Photo-Multipliers (SiPMs). In this work, we assess the PDC technology from the perspective of particle physics, using early, low-coverage PDC prototypes for proof-of-concept studies in calorimetry and tracking. Under simple, but extrapolable, detector conditions, we qualitatively compare PDCs with SiPM-based systems to illustrate their potential in terms of detector performance and readout. As the first application of this technology to particle physics instrumentation, we place particular emphasis on the user perspective, reporting on the practical experience of operating PDCs, from setup and calibration to data handling, and highlighting the relative ease of integration compared to conventional SiPM readout. ### [22] [Bootstrapping Classical Systems with Quenched Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20927)
*Minjae Cho,Yaprak Önder,Eslam Khalaf,Michael G. Scheer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一种 bootstrap（自举）方法，用于推导经典统计系统（classical statistical systems）中淬火无序（quenched disorder）下无序平均可观测量的界，并将其表述为线性规划与半正定规划问题，在随机格点稀释 Ising 模型与无序接触过程中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含淬火无序的经典统计系统，推导无序平均可观测量（disorder-averaged observables）的严格界存在困难。该文旨在引入 bootstrap 框架，为这类系统提供系统性的界推导手段。

Method: 方法包含三个核心要素：概率测度系综的正定性（positivity of ensembles of probability measures）、无序平均运动方程（disorder-averaged equations of motion），以及随机耦合变量的显式期望值（explicit expectation values of random coupling variables）。三者共同构成无序平均空间上的线性规划（linear programming）问题；进一步利用双复本 Gram 矩阵（two-replica Gram matrix）的正定性，可构造半正定规划（semidefinite programming）问题以给出双复本关联函数（two-replica correlators）的界。

Result: 以二维随机格点稀释 Ising 模型（two-dimensional random site-diluted Ising model）和一维无序接触过程（one-dimensional disordered contact process）为示例进行验证。对无序接触过程，bootstrap 界呈现出一段包含多个拐点（kinks）的区域，作者将其与 Griffiths 区（Griffiths region）相关联，其有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析给出的典型关联长度指数（typical correlation length exponent）与已知值在数值上吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该 bootstrap 方法可为由淬火无序主导的经典统计系统提供无序平均可观测量的界，并能通过线性规划与半正定规划形式分别处理单复本与双复本量。数值示例表明该方法能够刻画与 Griffiths 区相关的行为，并给出与已知结果相符的关联长度指数。

Abstract: We introduce a bootstrap method for deriving bounds on disorder-averaged observables in classical statistical systems with quenched disorder. The three main ingredients of the bootstrap are the positivity of ensembles of probability measures, disorder-averaged equations of motion, and explicit expectation values of random coupling variables. Together, these ingredients lead to a linear programming problem over the space of disorder averages. By further employing the positivity of the two-replica Gram matrix, we also formulate a semidefinite programming problem that produces bounds on two-replica correlators. We demonstrate the method using two examples: the two-dimensional random site-diluted Ising model and the one-dimensional disordered contact process. For the latter example, the bootstrap bounds exhibit a region of multiple kinks that we relate to the Griffiths region, whose finite-size scaling analysis yields a typical correlation length exponent in good numerical agreement with the known value.

</details>


### [23] [Arctic Curves and a Gapped Gas Phase in a Two-Band Free-Fermion Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21004)
*Charles Jordan,Dimitri M. Gangardt,Alexander G. Abanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究交错自由费米子链（staggered free-fermion chain）中畴壁态（domain-wall state）的虚时间演化（imaginary-time evolution）并施加返回自身约束，得到由锋利边界（arctic curves）分隔的冻结相、液相与气相。研究确定了两条 arctic 曲线、热力学返回振幅（return amplitude）及完整等时关联核（equal-time correlation kernel），并给出精确的矩阵 Wiener-Hopf 分解（matrix Wiener-Hopf factorization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画受返回约束的虚时间演化所生成的时空剖面结构，特别是谱隙（spectral gap）如何导致不可压缩的半满气相及其内层 arctic 边界。该问题与双周期二聚体模型（doubly periodic dimer models）中的冻结-液-气结构存在对应关系，需在连续时间自由费米子框架下建立对应。

Method: 方法上，将问题约化为椭圆谱曲线（elliptic spectral curve）上的标量 Riemann-Hilbert 问题，从而获得同一分块 Toeplitz 算子（block-Toeplitz operator）的精确矩阵 Wiener-Hopf 分解。返回振幅与关联核分别由该算子的行列式与逆控制；arctic 边界则被刻画为自由准粒子轨迹（free quasiparticle trajectories）的焦散线（caustics）。

Result: 结果显示，液相区域关联呈代数衰减（algebraic decay），气相区域呈指数衰减（exponential decay）。热力学返回振幅的对数由一个带显式谱隙依赖的二次项与一个有界的周期 theta 函数修正组成。研究同时给出了两条 arctic 曲线（外层冻结-液边界与内层气相边界）及完整等时关联核的精确表达。

Conclusion: 结论是，所得到的冻结-液-气结构构成双周期二聚体模型中相应结构的连续时间自由费米子对应物。精确矩阵 Wiener-Hopf 分解为返回振幅与关联核提供了统一描述框架。

Abstract: We study the imaginary-time evolution of a domain-wall state constrained to return to itself in a staggered free-fermion chain. The resulting space-time profile separates into frozen, liquid, and gas phases divided by sharp boundaries known as arctic curves. The spectral gap produces an incompressible half-filled gas phase bounded by an inner arctic curve, in addition to the outer frozen-liquid boundary. We determine both arctic curves, the thermodynamic return amplitude, and the complete equal-time correlation kernel. Correlations decay algebraically in the liquid regions and exponentially in the gas, while both arctic boundaries arise as caustics of free quasiparticle trajectories. The return amplitude and correlation kernel are respectively controlled by the determinant and inverse of the same block-Toeplitz operator. We obtain an exact matrix Wiener-Hopf factorization of this operator by reducing the problem to a scalar Riemann-Hilbert problem on an elliptic spectral curve. The factorization also yields the exact thermodynamic return amplitude: its logarithm consists of a quadratic term with explicit gap dependence and a bounded periodic theta-function correction. The resulting frozen-liquid-gas structure is a continuous-time free-fermion counterpart of that found in doubly periodic dimer models.

</details>


### [24] [Functional Renormalization for Random Matrix Theory: The relational background field method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21031)

TL;DR: 本文针对随机矩阵模型（random matrix models）提出一种基于部分矩阵值中间场（partial matrix-valued intermediate field）的重整化群（renormalization group, RG）构造方法，在大N极限下保持原模型的U(N)/O(N)对称性，并给出与标准双标度指数（double-scaling exponent）精确吻合的临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散引力模型（discrete gravity models）的可靠RG流构造，同时保持其底层对称群（通常为U(N)或O(N)），仍是一个未解问题。对随机矩阵模型而言，该对称性与编码二维量子欧氏时空（two-dimensional quantum Euclidean spacetime）随机几何的相互作用内在关联，因此构建保对称性的RG尤为关键。

Result: 所得红外理论（infrared theory）由三维非局域欧氏场论（three-dimensional non-local Euclidean field theory）描述，具有非平凡的类Wilson-Fisher不动点（Wilson-Fisher-like fixed point）及单一相关方向（single relevant direction）。其相应临界指数（critical exponent）与标准双标度指数精确匹配。

Abstract: The construction of a reliable renormalization group (RG) flow for discrete gravity models that preserves their underlying symmetry group, typically $U(N)$ or $O(N)$, remains an open problem. For random matrix models, which are the focus of this paper, this symmetry is intrinsically tied to the interactions encoding the random geometry of two-dimensional quantum Euclidean spacetime. We develop a novel approach based on the introduction of a partial matrix-valued intermediate field. In the large-$N$ limit, measure concentration strongly suppresses fluctuations of its singular values, allowing it to play the role of a self-consistent background field. This provides the basis for a relational RG in which the notion of scale is dynamically induced by the effective Gaussian measure in the basis where the intermediate field is diagonal. Our construction preserves the symmetry of the original model and admits a well-defined continuum limit. We show that the resulting infrared theory is described by a three-dimensional non-local Euclidean field theory with a non-trivial Wilson-Fisher-like fixed point and a single relevant direction. Remarkably, the associated critical exponent exactly matches the standard double-scaling exponent. We finally discuss extensions of the background-field approach to other discrete gravity models, such as random tensor models, and to different symmetry groups, as well as connections with more formal RG frameworks and information geometry.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of rigid polycrystalline structures in atomistic ionic particle systems as low-energy configurations. The interaction between particles of opposite charge is modeled by hard spheres that interact when they are tangential. The interaction between particles of same charge is modeled as a hard repulsion that forces a minimal distance between them. The atomistic energy is frame invariant, and no underlying reference lattice is assumed on the ionic configurations. The asymptotic behavior of configurations with finite surface energy scaling is identified by means of $Γ$-convergence. The related continuum theory is described by piecewise constant fields that encode the local orientation of the configuration. The limiting energy is local and concentrates at grain boundaries, which correspond to the boundaries of the regions where the underlying configuration has a constant orientation. The limiting energy density is anisotropic and depends on the relative misorientation of the two grains, their translation misfit, and the normal to their interface. Furthermore, we perform a fine analysis of surface energies for solid-solid and solid-vacuum phase transitions and determine energetically favorable orientation mismatches. This relies on a structure result for our grain boundaries, which shows that, due to the rigid setup, interpolating layers near the grain interface are energetically not favorable.

</details>

Abstract: We study the first-passage time statistics of an active Ornstein--Uhlenbeck particle on a finite one-dimensional interval with an absorbing boundary at one end and a reflecting boundary at the other, subject to stochastic resetting. In the absence of resetting, activity can either increase or decrease the mean first-passage time, depending on the relation between the persistence time and the diffusive time to absorption. </details>

Abstract: The Lorenz system is the canonical low-order model of convective instability, yet its dissipative and driving terms have never been checked against, nor constructed from, an explicit thermodynamic bookkeeping. We derive a modification that satisfies the local-detailed-balance condition for macroscopic relaxation toward nonequilibrium steady states, thereby identifying the thermodynamic force, entropy-production rate and frenesy of the resulting flow. The resulting model produces a transition from a quiescent fixed point to a robust, large-amplitude relaxation oscillation, closely analogous to recharge-discharge oscillator paradigms used for the El Nino-Southern Oscillation.   <summary>Details</summary>

Our accurate numerical results are attributed to the efficiency of the irreversible algorithm, whose advantage over the reversible algorithm is even more pronounced for the self-avoiding trail model than for the self-avoiding walk model.

### [31] [Multi-Resolution Wire-Fencing for Efficient Path Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21897)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大体系（如生物分子模拟）中，受存储限制或序参量计算代价所限，帧保存频率较低，导致可用于射击（shooting）的采样点数量严重不足，极端情况下仅剩单点，重复射击同一构型会降低采样效率。

Method: 作者在前沿的线栅移动（wire-fencing move）基础上引入多分辨率变体：对选定的子轨迹（subtrajectories）以高于已存储轨迹的时间分辨率进行传播。该精细化仅作用于蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo, MC）移动本身，不改变生成路径的结构与存储方式。

Abstract: Path sampling methods enable the computation of thermodynamic and kinetic properties through Monte Carlo (MC) moves that generate trajectories from short forward and backward molecular dynamics (MD) segments. Recently, the wire-fencing move was developed to achieve near-unity acceptance while also rapidly decorrelating successive paths, two properties that are usually in conflict in conventional MC schemes. However, in large systems, such as biomolecular simulations, the frame-saving frequency is often kept low to reduce storage requirements. Likewise, when evaluating the order parameter is expensive, frames are saved less frequently to reduce the associated cost. In either situation, this can severely limit the number of available shooting points, in extreme situations leaving only a single point, typically the one with the highest order parameter value, accessible for shooting. Repeated shootings may then originate from the same configuration, reducing sampling efficiency. Here, we introduce a multi-resolution variant of the wire-fencing move in which selected subtrajectories are propagated at higher temporal resolution than the stored trajectories. This refinement affects only the MC move and does not alter the structure or storage of the generated paths, but enhances the diffusion of shooting points along the trajectory and thereby improves sampling efficiency. The approach is demonstrated on two model systems and a realistic protein--ligand unbinding process, with the latter showing an estimated efficiency improvement of more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [32] [Universal Eigenvector Statistics of Non-Hermitian Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22079)

We find analytically, solutions for stars with any radius and mass, parametrized by $m$ and $N_s$, the total number of fermions. We briefly discuss classical stability of the Newtonian solution, and its relation to Newtonian polytropic fluids.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We show that the logotropic dark fluid, proposed as a single-fluid unification of dark matter and dark energy, is exactly dissipative unimodular gravity with pressureless matter and the logarithmic energy diffusion function $Q(a)=3A\ln a+Q_{c}$. The correspondence is exact at the level of the homogeneous background, rather than asymptotic, and introduces no parameters beyond those already present in either description. It provides a dynamical reinterpretation of the logotropic temperature $A$, whose origin was left open in the original proposal: up to the expansion rate, $A$ is the rate at which energy is diffused from matter into the geometric sector, $\dot Q=3AH$. The adiabatic sound speed of the effective fluid is $c_{s}^{2}=A/ρ$, where $ρ$ is the unimodular matter energy density, and reaches the speed of light at $a_{s}=a_{M}/2^{1/3}$; the matter energy density then vanishes and changes sign at $a_{M}$, which is precisely the scale factor at which the model is known to become phantom. Since $ρ$ coincides with the enthalpy density $ε+P$ of the logotropic fluid, the phantom branch is the branch on which the matter sector carries negative energy density and on which the apparent horizon entropy decreases. Finally, we show that a one-parameter saturating diffusion law removes these late-time pathologies while preserving the low-redshift phenomenology.

</details>

Abstract: A novel reinterpretation on the effective one-body (EOB) mapping of compact-body binary (CBB) coalescence was previously considered. Instead of the conventional ``marble in a funnel" description of EOB, CBB coalescence is modeled as the concurrent rotation and contraction of a hollow shell with a constant reduced mass measure. Merger is achieved, therefore, when the shell radius reaches the total mass radius. While past works analyzed the energetics sector of the model and the radiated gravitational waves (GWs), this work considers the geometric waveform modeling aspect. More specifically, we mature the mass-shell waveform analysis initially discussed with toy-model conditions by imposing necessary non-conservative mechanics, leading to the analytical expression of usable mass-shell modeled GW-forms. This is to set the foundation for eventual computational endeavors with the analytical waveforms presented here, enabling a comparison against current and state-of-the-art models.

</details>

We find that the migrated remnant remains close to uniform rotation after the transition, and we trace its quasi-radial pulsations through complementary diagnostics involving the central density, the rotation profile, and the axisymmetric gravitational-wave channel. For $k_2=1/2,1,2$, these limit results have applications to radial parts of Brownian motions with drift on the $M\times N$-dimensional matrices over $\mathbb R,\mathbb C$, and the quaternions for $M\to\infty$. We also discuss these limits in the Dunkl case $N=1$.

*Frédéric Ouimet,Donald Richards*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在回应 Li 所提出的两个全正性问题，从而厘清一维共形 bootstrap 中正性结构的精确边界。该问题关系到该正性结构在何种参数范围内成立。

Conclusion: 结论确定了一维共形 bootstrap 中正性结构的精确适用范围：共形块核的阈值严格为 1/2，且在 Δ<1/2 处正性不可避免地失效，无法通过限制 z 的下界来补救。

Abstract: In this paper, we completely resolve the two total positivity problems raised by Li [J. High Energy Phys., 2023(7):Paper No. 167, 44 pp., 2023], thereby determining the precise scope of this positivity structure in the one-dimensional conformal bootstrap. First, the Gauss hypergeometric kernel $\mathcal{F}(Δ,z) = {}_2F_1(Δ,Δ;2Δ;z)$ is proved to be strictly totally positive of order infinity for $Δ> 0$ and $z \in (0,1)$. Second, the sharp lower $Δ$-parameter threshold for the associated one-dimensional $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ conformal block $G_Δ(z) = z^Δ\mathcal{F}(Δ,z)$ is shown to be $1/2$: the conformal-block kernel $G_Δ(z)$ is strictly totally positive of order infinity for $Δ\geq 1/2$. For every $τ\in (0,1/2)$, there exists a strictly negative odd-order minor of $G_Δ(z)$ such that all its $Δ$-values are in $(τ,1/2)$ and all its $z$-values can be chosen arbitrarily close to one. Consequently, no restriction $z > z_0$ with $z_0 < 1$ can restore total positivity of order infinity over all $Δ> 0$.

</details>

*Nicola Soave,Gianmaria Verzini,Lorenzo Villata*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在质量约束变分框架下研究乘积空间 R^N × G 上的驻定聚焦非线性薛定谔方程，建立泛函框架并刻画基态存在性、临界指数行为与维数交叉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所研究的乘积空间是欧氏空间与紧度量图的混合结构，其所有面为 (N+1) 维并沿余维一的界面粘合，能量空间为真正的 Sobolev 空间，且图的度量与拓扑同时进入变分问题，此类混合结构此前缺乏系统分析。

Method: 建立泛函框架：给出 H^1(R^N × G) 的两种等价描述，引入两个变量上的部分重排（partial rearrangements）及相应 Pólya–Szegő 不等式，证明局部化形式的 Gagliardo–Nirenberg 不等式并与 R^{N+1} 及半空间的最优常数作比较；随后在质量约束下分析变分问题。

Result: 当 2<p<2_*:=2+4/(N+1) 时对任意质量存在基态；在临界指数 p=2_* 时，基态存在于介于欧氏临界质量之半与全值之间的、依赖于图的阈值之下，该阈值在图上存在圈覆盖时取全值、存在终端边时恰为半值，且两种情况阈值均为尖锐；当 2_*<p<2+4/N 时全局极小元不存在，但在进一步的质量阈值之下证明了局部极小元的存在性并给出显式下界。

Conclusion: 论文刻画了维数交叉：在临界质量以下极小元不依赖于图变量，并以 G 上 Kirchhoff 拉普拉斯算子的首个非零特征值刻画使半平凡解成为局部极小元的阈值。

Abstract: We study the stationary focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation on the product $\mathbb{R}^N \times \mathcal{G}$ of the Euclidean space with a compact metric graph, in the mass-constrained variational setting. Such a product is a hybrid structure of a new type: all its faces are $(N+1)$-dimensional and are glued along interfaces of codimension one, so that the energy space is a genuine Sobolev space, while both the metric and the topology of the graph enter the variational problem. We first develop the functional framework, giving two equivalent descriptions of $H^1(\mathbb{R}^N \times \mathcal{G})$, introducing partial rearrangements in each of the two variables together with the corresponding Pólya--Szegő inequalities, and proving Gagliardo--Nirenberg inequalities in a localized form, with a comparison of the optimal constants with those of $\mathbb{R}^{N+1}$ and of the half-space. We then study the mass-constrained problem. Ground states exist for every mass when $2<p<2_*:=2+4/(N+1)$. At the critical exponent $p=2_*$, they exist below a graph-dependent threshold lying between one half of the Euclidean critical mass and the full Euclidean critical mass. The latter value is attained when the graph admits a cycle covering, whereas the threshold is exactly halved in the presence of a terminal edge. In both cases, the threshold is sharp. For $2_*<p<2+4/N$ global minimizers do not exist, but we prove the existence of local minimizers below a further mass threshold, for which we give an explicit lower bound. Finally, we describe the dimensional crossover: below a critical mass the minimizers do not depend on the graph variable, and we characterize the threshold below which the semi-trivial solution is a local minimizer in terms of the first nonzero eigenvalue of the Kirchhoff Laplacian on $\mathcal{G}$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 由于磁 Wigner 方程含有关于空间变量的一阶导数，所得结果为 $L^2$ 空间中的紧性（compactness），但不获得正则性（regularity）增益。

Conclusion: 本文将 Golse 与 Möller（Commun. Math. Sci., 2026）的 $L^2$ 平均引理推广到电磁场情形；在广义相空间变量下应用速度平均，可在 $L^2$ 中取得紧性，而受方程中一阶空间导数所限，无法得到正则性提升。

Abstract: We discuss the application of $L^2$-based velocity averaging to the Wigner kinetic equation governing the quantum evolution of the Wigner transform of a density operator of a particle subjected to an external electromagnetic field. This extends the $L^2$-based averaging lemma from [F. Golse, J. Möller: Commun. Math. Sci. (2026)] to electromagnetic fields. We make use of the magnetic or gauge-invariant Wigner transform due to Stratonovich [Sov. Phys. D., (1)414-418, 1956] which differs from the usual definition by a phase factor involving the circulation of the magnetic vector potential along a line segment. The magnetic Wigner transform obeys the Wigner equation in the generalized phase space variables $(x,v=ξ-A(t,x))$, which is a more natural setting for the application of velocity averaging. Since the magnetic Wigner equation contains a first order derivative in the spatial variable, we obtain compactness in $L^2$ but no gain in regularity.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: $Gamma$-极限由周期与分数阶指数两参数的相对标度完全决定，临界对数标度下权重显式依赖于两个渐近过程的相对标度。

Abstract: We study the simultaneous asymptotic behaviour of homogenization and the Bourgain--Brezis--Mironescu limit for quadratic fractional energies with periodic coefficients. The functionals depend on two small parameters: the period of the coefficients and the fractional exponent approaching the local limit. We prove that the $Γ$-limit is completely determined by the relative scaling of these parameters. In the two extreme regimes, one recovers the expected separation of scales, yielding either the homogenized Dirichlet energy or the energy with averaged coefficients. At the critical logarithmic scaling, however, the two mechanisms coexist, and the limit is a convex combination of the homogenized and averaged energies, with weights depending explicitly on the relative scaling of the two asymptotic processes. The proof combines the homogenization analysis of the short-range interactions with a blow-up argument showing that long-range interactions are simply averaged using a Riemann--Lebesgue-type argument.

</details>


### [53] [2D Torus defocusing NLSE with a potential and random initial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21969)
*Nicolas Lindstrom*

Main category: math.AP

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bourgain 曾就二维散焦非线性薛定谔方程（defocusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation）的随机初始数据给出相关结果，本文旨在将该结果推广至带位势（potential）V(x) 的情形。

Method: 考虑二维环面（2D Torus, T²）上带位势 V(x) ∈ H^{2⁺}(T²) 的非线性立方散焦薛定谔方程，初值 u₀ 取以 (−Δ+V)^{-1} 为协方差（covariance）的中心高斯随机变量（centered gaussian variable），并分析截断方程（truncated equation）解的性质。

Conclusion: 结果将 Bourgain 关于二维散焦非线性薛定谔方程不变测度（invariant measures）的工作推广到带 H^{2⁺} 位势的情形。

Abstract: We consider the non-linear cubic defocusing Schrödinger equation on the 2D Torus ($T^2$) with a potential $V(x)\in H^{2^+}(T^2)$. The initial data, $u_0$,is given by a centered gaussian variable with covariance $(-Δ+V)^{-1}$, and thus is almost surely in $H^{0^-}(T^2)$. We show that it is almost surely well-possed for all time in the weak sense, and that $u-e^{it(Δ-V)}u_0$ is in $H^{s}(T^2)$ for some $s>0$, and that the solution is the limit of the solutions to the truncated equation. This result is a generalization of the results first given by Bourgain in Invariant Measures for the {2D}-Defocusing Nonlinear {Schr{ö}dinger} Equation.

</details>

### [54] [Analytic results for thermal correlation enhancement after a trap quench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21128)
*Giorgio Li,Paola Ruggiero,Stefano Scopa*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文基于量子广义流体力学（Quantum Generalized Hydrodynamics）的有限温度推广，解析研究了热一维玻色气体在陷阱淬火（trap quench）后密度关联（density correlations）的非平衡演化，重点讨论 Tonks–Girardeau 极限，并用精确数值结果标定其温度适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热一维玻色气体在非平衡条件下密度关联如何演化尚缺乏解析描述，尤其需要明确温度在动力学过程中的作用及其有效范围，因此本文针对陷阱淬火后的演化展开研究。

Method: 发展量子广义流体力学（Quantum Generalized Hydrodynamics）的有限温度扩展，聚焦 Tonks–Girardeau 极限，并将解析预言与双阱到谐振阱淬火以及陷阱释放（trap release）两种情形的精确数值结果进行对照。

Result: 解析结果重现了膨胀阶段关联的动力学增强，即从热抑制关联到幂律体关联（power-law bulk correlations）的转变；对陷阱释放，固定体点对应的共形距离（conformal distance）以 1/t 衰减，关联逐渐进入与温度无关的普适 ~1/x² 区间。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在流体力学长时间（hydrodynamic long-time）区域，体密度关联趋向零温平衡形式，且与初始温度无关，揭示了该普适行为在有限温度下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium evolution of density correlations in a thermal one-dimensional Bose gas following a trap quench. We focus on the Tonks--Girardeau limit and develop an analytical description based on a finite-temperature extension of Quantum Generalized Hydrodynamics. We benchmark our predictions against exact numerics for a quench from a double well to a harmonic trap and for a trap release, and assess their temperature range of validity. Our results capture the dynamical enhancement of correlations during the expansion stages, namely the crossover from thermally suppressed to power-law bulk correlations. For the trap release, the conformal distance associated with fixed bulk points decreases as $1/t$, so the correlations progressively probe a universal regime~$\sim1/x^2$ that is independent of temperature. We thus show that, in the hydrodynamic long-time regime, the bulk density correlations approach their zero-temperature equilibrium form independently of the initial temperature.

</details>


### [55] [Efficient MPO Construction for Long-Range Hamiltonians with Periodic Boundary Conditions: Application to Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21290)
*Xin Wang,Bo Xiong*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种矩阵乘积算符（matrix product operator, MPO）构造方法，通过引入额外传播通道，将周期边界条件与有限程耦合直接嵌入开边界 MPO 中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子多体动力学的张量网络（tensor network）模拟中，MPO 是基础组件；如何在开边界 MPO 中有效表示周期边界条件与有限程耦合，是待解决的构造问题。

Method: 采用引入额外传播通道（propagation channels）的 MPO 构造，将周期边界条件（periodic boundary conditions）与有限程耦合（finite-range couplings）嵌入开边界 MPO，并在时间依赖变分原理（time-dependent variational principle, TDVP）框架下模拟自旋-1/2 链的淬火动力学（quench dynamics）。

Result: 以四阶龙格-库塔法（fourth-order Runge-Kutta method）作为数值基准进行对比，单体和两体可观测量（single-body and two-body observables）均表现出极好的一致性（excellent agreement）。

Conclusion: 该方法为周期有限程系统（periodic finite-range systems）中的多体动力学张量网络模拟提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Matrix product operator (MPO) serves as a fundamental component in tensor network simulations of quantum many-body dynamics. We employ an MPO construction that introduces additional propagation channels to embed both periodic boundary conditions and finite-range couplings directly into an open boundary MPO. We apply this construction within the time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) framework to simulate quench dynamics in a spin-1/2 chain with finite-range interactions, and benchmark the results numerically against the fourth-order Runge-Kutta method, finding excellent agreement for both single-body and two-body observables. The approach offers a practical route for tensor network simulations of many-body dynamics in periodic finite-range systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 色散效应（dispersion effects）的引入对密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）正确描述非共价相互作用（non-covalent interactions）至关重要。XDM（exchange-hole dipole moment）模型结合了阻尼成对渐近表达式的简洁性与理论化的色散系数计算，被认为是分子晶体描述中最准确的色散修正之一，但存在两个主要缺陷：依赖实验测定的自由原子极化率（free-atom polarizabilities），以及因使用 Hirshfeld 划分而在大原子部分电荷体系（如碱金属阳离子、卤素阴离子）中表现不佳。

Method: 提出 neXDM，即 XDM 的非经验变体：采用 Kirkwood 极化率公式消除实验参数，使 neXDM 成为纯 meta-GGA 色散泛函；并借鉴 Bučko 等人在类似 TS（Tkatchenko–Scheffler）方法上的工作，以迭代 Hirshfeld 划分（iterative Hirshfeld partitioning）替代原 Hirshfeld 划分。

Result: neXDM 在标准分子与晶体基准集上的表现与 XDM 相当，并大幅改善了对离子体系的建模。在 X23 集的分子晶体晶格能（molecular crystal lattice energies）上，neXDM 取得 0.700 kcal/mol 的成绩，创下色散修正广义梯度近似（generalized-gradient approximation, GGA）泛函的新纪录。

Conclusion: neXDM 在保持 XDM 精度的同时消除了经验参数并修正了离子体系描述不足的问题，可作为纯 meta-GGA 色散泛函使用，并在分子晶体晶格能基准上刷新了色散修正 GGA 泛函的最优表现。

Abstract: The inclusion of dispersion effects is important in density-functional theory (DFT) to model non-covalent interactions correctly, a task that is essential in many applications of the theory. Many dispersion functionals have been proposed in the past. The exchange-hole dipole moment (XDM) model combines the simplicity of a damped pairwise asymptotic expression for the dispersion energy with a theory-grounded approach to calculate the dispersion coefficients. XDM is, arguably, the most accurate dispersion correction for the description of molecular crystals and it has been thoroughly tested for other applications across a wide range of chemistries. Here, we address the two main shortcomings of XDM. First, XDM relies on the use of experimentally determined free-atom polarizabilities. Second, because the XDM atom-in-molecule properties (volumes, polarizabilities, exchange-hole dipole moments) use the Hirshfeld partition method, XDM describes systems with large atomic partial charges, like alkali cations or halide anions, poorly. We propose neXDM, a non-empirical variant of XDM that removes the experimental parameters by using the Kirkwood polarizability formula, thereby making neXDM a pure meta-GGA dispersion functional. In addition, following previous work by Bučko et al. on the similar Tkatchenko--Scheffler (TS) method, we replace the Hirshfeld partitioning with its iterative counterpart. The performance of neXDM is shown to be on par with XDM in standard molecular and crystal benchmark sets, and greatly improves the modeling of ionic systems. The new neXDM method sets a new record for the best dispersion-corrected generalized-gradient approximation (GGA) functional for molecular crystal lattice energies in the X23 set (0.700~kcal/mol).

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究借鉴空间 calorimeter 的横向分布分析思路，提出基于 Cherenkov 光子发射位置横向分布的新观测量，并结合似然比方法进行多望远镜联合判别，在 1 TeV 附近将 gamma-proton 区分能力提升约一个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甚高能 gamma 射线天文学需有效区分 gamma 光子与本底（主要为质子）。空间 calorimeter 在 1 TeV 附近可达 10000-100000 的本底抑制能力，而地面成像 Cherenkov 望远镜仅约 10，存在显著优化空间。

Method: 使用 CORSIKA 与 sim_telarray 模拟 H.E.S.S. 探测器响应，并由 Cherenkov 簇射图像重建光子发射位置。受空间 calorimeter 分析技术启发，构建基于发射位置横向分布的新观测量，采用似然比方法联合多望远镜单镜变量，并与传统基于 Hillas 的分析对比。

Conclusion: 基于 Cherenkov 光子发射位置横向分布的观测量及似然比联合方法可显著提升地面 Cherenkov 望远镜的 gamma-proton 区分能力，为甚高能 gamma 射线探测提供有效优化途径。

Abstract: Purpose: Very-high-energy gamma-ray astronomy is an important window to study the extreme astrophysical processes in the universe, and how to effectively distinguish between gamma photons and background signals (mainly protons) is a key technical challenge to realize very-high-energy gamma-ray detection. Spaceborne calorimeters record secondary particle spatial distributions, achieving a background rejection power of 10000-100000 near 1 TeV. Ground-based imaging Cherenkov telescopes measure Cherenkov photon angular distributions, yet they only reach a rejection factor of about 10 in this energy range, leaving substantial room for optimization. Methods: We use CORSIKA and sim_telarray to simulate H.E.S.S. detector responses. Photon emission positions are reconstructed from Cherenkov shower images. Inspired by analysis techniques for space-borne calorimeter, we propose new observables derived from the transverse distribution of Cherenkov photon emission positions to separate gamma-ray signals from proton backgrounds. We employ a likelihood-ratio method to combine single-telescope variables across multiple telescopes and compare its performance with conventional Hillas-based analysis. Results: The results show that observables built from the transverse distribution of Cherenkov photon emission positions are less sensitive to Cherenkov image size and outperform the Hillas width parameter for single-telescope gamma-proton discrimination. Furthermore, the likelihood ratio combining width yields substantially better performance than the conventional mean reduced scaled width: the improvement reaches roughly one order of magnitude near 1 TeV, and this enhancement remains above 50% for energies above 10 TeV.

</details>


### [58] [Self-Similar Mass Spectra of Hierarchical Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21033)
*Liam Blum,Stefano Profumo,Ryan Schantz,Quoc Ha Tran*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文将黑洞层级并合（hierarchical merging）建模为凝聚问题（coagulation problem），由单一核函数（kernel）刻画并合率对质量与宇宙时间的依赖，推导晚期质量谱的标度律并对并合环境进行分类。应用于原初黑洞（primordial black holes）时指出四个标准双星形成通道中三个对应超线性核，可能引发失控增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复并合的黑洞会在质量分布中留下历史记录，但缺乏统一框架将并合率与晚期质量谱联系起来。该文旨在以凝聚理论为工具，提供可解析的标度律与环境分类，并澄清原初黑洞并合通道的增长性质。

Method: 将层级并合视为凝聚问题：单一核函数编码并合率对参与质量与宇宙时间的依赖；推导晚期谱的闭式标度律、给出精确可解基准，并以单一指数对并合环境分类（衡量并合偏好或抑制大质量参与者的程度）。对原初黑洞，须谨慎将已发表并合率转换为核形式。

Result: 框架给出闭式标度律、精确可解基准与单指数环境分类。四个标准双星形成通道中，三个映射为超线性核（superlinear kernels），成为失控增长候选；仅早期三体通道落入非凝胶（nongelling）区，允许缓慢自相似增长。十三个抑制性非凝胶核的数值解表明，谱形不由标度指数单独决定；计入每次并合辐射的能量会显著改变高质端截断并消耗总黑洞质量。

Conclusion: 层级并合可用凝聚核框架统一描述，并据此判别失控增长与非凝胶自相似增长。确认物理凝胶（physical gelation）仍需完整的族群依赖核及其有限凝聚历史，该文未予解决。

Abstract: Black holes that merge repeatedly carry a record of that history in their mass distribution. We treat hierarchical merging as a coagulation problem, in which a single kernel encodes how the merger rate depends on the masses involved and on cosmic time, and we derive the late-time spectra that such populations approach. The framework yields closed scaling laws, an exactly solvable benchmark, and a classification of merger environments by a single exponent measuring how strongly mergers favor or suppress massive participants. Applying it to primordial black holes requires care in translating published merger rates into kernel form; done correctly, three of the four standard binary-formation channels map onto superlinear kernels and are therefore candidates for runaway growth, in which the heaviest objects would dominate and no steady mass-conserving spectrum exists; establishing physical gelation, however, requires the full population-dependent kernel and its finite coagulation history, neither of which we settle here. Only the early three-body channel maps into the nongelling regime and admits slow self-similar growth. Numerical solutions across thirteen suppressive, nongelling kernels show that the shape of the spectrum is not fixed by the scaling exponent alone, and that accounting for the energy radiated at each merger measurably changes the high-mass cutoff and drains the population's total black-hole mass.

</details>


### [59] [Nonlinearly Causal General-Relativistic Two-Fluid Dissipative Magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21923)
*Elias R. Most,Samuel J. Dunham*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作提出广义相对论（GR）19矩耗散磁流体力学（MHD）的完整表述，涵盖一阶耗散项与电子自由度，给出因果性与强双曲性条件，并开发高分辨率激波捕捉格式进行数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建可处理广义相对论双流体等离子体中全部一阶耗散效应（黏性、热导、电阻与霍尔项）及理想电子自由度的理论框架，并使其具备可用于数值模拟的鲁棒性。

Method: 推导受约束一阶19矩系统（19-moment system）的充要非线性因果性条件与强双曲性（strong hyperbolicity）条件；发展将全部演化方程（含耗散与电子部分）写为通量散度形式的高分辨率激波捕捉格式，采用隐式积分跨越动理学尺度，并基于守恒态物理可容许性条件构造新的物理性强制方案与耗散双流体Bondi吸积（Bondi accretion）精确解。

Result: 格式可在不解析全域动理学尺度的情况下模拟跨数量级尺度系统，并系统性地跨越回旋频率与等离子体频率等动理学尺度；与黑洞吸积及磁层动力学（magnetospheric dynamics）的动理学粒子模拟（particle-in-cell）结果对比验证，能捕捉约0.1量级的无量纲重联率（reconnection rates）及类Braginskii各向异性压强与热流等主要特征。

Conclusion: 该表述与数值格式能够正确刻画对全局尺度反馈重要的关键物理特征，为广义相对论耗散双流体磁流体力学模拟提供了兼具理论完备性与鲁棒性的框架。

Abstract: We present a formulation of general-relativistic (GR) 19-moment dissipative magnetohydrodynamics (MHD), capable of handling all first-order dissipative terms (viscosity, heat conductivity, resistivity and Hall terms), as well as all ideal electron degrees of freedom (number density, momentum and energy) in a GR astrophysical two-fluid plasma. We derive necessary and sufficient nonlinear causality conditions for the constrained first-order 19-moment system, as well as both necessary and sufficient conditions for strong hyperbolicity. To assess the formulation numerically, we develop a high-resolution shock-capturing scheme that solves these equations in a performance-portable fashion. The scheme expresses all evolution equations, including the dissipative and electron sectors, in flux-divergence form, allowing us to model systems with scales separated by orders of magnitude without resolving kinetic scales everywhere on the grid. In addition, we use implicit integration methods to systematically overstep kinetic scales in MHD regions, such as cyclotron and plasma frequencies. To make the scheme as robust as GRMHD codes, we construct necessary and sufficient conditions for a conserved state to be physically admissible, and based on these construct a new physicality-enforcement scheme. As an exact validation comparison, we formulate and derive a full solution to dissipative two-fluid Bondi accretion in general relativity. We then validate the equations against a series of results from kinetic particle-in-cell models of black hole accretion and magnetospheric dynamics, demonstrating that our formulation and scheme can correctly capture major features relevant for feedback on global scales of these solutions, including dimensionless reconnection rates of order $0.1$ and Braginskii-like anisotropic pressures and heat fluxes.

</details>

### [60] [Eigenvectors of $p$-Curvature for Geometric Difference Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21090)
*Vitaly Tarasov,Alexander Varchenko*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本摘要研究特征 \(p\)（characteristic \(p\)）下 qKZ 型加性离散平坦联络（qKZ-type additive discrete flat connection）的 \(p\)-曲率算子（\(p\)-curvature operators）。作者给出其本征截面与本征值的 Bethe-ansatz 型构造，本征截面表现为有限格 \(\mathbb{Z}^r/p\mathbb{Z}^r\) 上的离散超几何和（discrete hypergeometric sum）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \(p\)-曲率算子是特征 \(p\) 下 qKZ 型加性离散平坦联络的交换自同构，其本征截面与本征值的显式构造尚待刻画。摘要以此为出发点，寻求对这类算子的谱数据进行组合式描述。

Method: 采用 Bethe-ansatz 型构造（Bethe-ansatz-type construction）来给出 \(p\)-曲率算子的本征截面与本征值。构造将本征截面表达为有限格 \(\mathbb{Z}^r/p\mathbb{Z}^r\) 上的离散超几何和，即超几何积分（hypergeometric integral）的有限离散类比。

Result: 获得了 \(p\)-曲率算子的本征截面与本征值；其本征截面是有限格 \(\mathbb{Z}^r/p\mathbb{Z}^r\) 上的离散超几何和。该结果将本征截面刻画为超几何积分的有限、离散模拟。

Conclusion: \(p\)-曲率算子的本征截面可由有限格上的离散超几何和给出，并通过 Bethe-ansatz 型构造获得；这确立了连续超几何积分与有限离散情形之间的对应。

Abstract: A qKZ-type additive discrete flat connection in characteristic $p$ has $p$-curvature operators. They are commuting automorphisms of the connection. We present a Bethe-ansatz-type construction of eigensections and eigenvalues of the $p$-curvature operators. An eigensection of $p$-curvature operators is a discrete hypergeometric sum over the finite lattice $\mathbb{Z}^r/p\mathbb{Z}^r$. Thus, the $p$-curvature eigensections are constructed by a finite, discrete analogue of a hypergeometric integral.

</details>

*Jialin Li,Shaowei Liu,Amil Aligayev,Hao Jiang,Muhammad,Xin Yu,Jin Tao,Jiaqi Wang,Agnieszka Jastrzębska,Qing Huang*

Main category: physics.med-ph

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种集成表面增强拉曼散射（SERS）与比色法的双模式柔性生物传感器，以AgNCs@Co-Ni LDH与MBTH水凝胶贴片为核心，实现对呼出气中己醛（hexanal）的高灵敏检测，SERS检测限低至3.34×10⁻¹³ M，并结合紧凑型CNN智能识别系统用于便携实时分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 己醛（hexanal）作为挥发性有机化合物（VOC），是肺癌早期检测的潜在生物标志物，因此需要开发灵敏、选择性好的检测手段以满足早期筛查需求。

Method: 以Co-Ni层状双氢氧化物包裹的Ag纳米立方体（AgNCs@Co-Ni LDH）作为功能基质，兼具SERS增强与VOC吸附能力；将其与3-甲基-2-苯并噻唑啉酮腙（MBTH）共同嵌入琼脂糖水凝胶，构建MBTH-AgNCs@Co-Ni LDH水凝胶贴片以促进己醛氧化，产生比色与SERS双信号；并开发紧凑型CNN多终端智能识别系统用于AI驱动的便携实时比色分析。

Result: 该水凝胶双模式传感平台在SERS检测己醛中表现出高选择性、优异稳定性与精确度，SERS方法检测限低至3.34×10⁻¹³ M；氧化反应同时生成蓝色产物acrizine，实现比色与SERS信号同步产生。

Conclusion: 该工作可实现疑似肺癌患者呼出气中己醛的有效检测，凸显其在肺癌早期筛查中的潜力，并为多模态水凝胶生物传感器在疾病诊断中的更广泛应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Hexanal, a volatile organic compound (VOC), is a potential biomarker for the early detection of lung cancer. In this study, we developed a dual-mode flexible biosensor that integrates surface-enhanced Raman scattering (SERS) and colorimetric detection for the quantitative analysis of hexanal in human exhaled breath. The biosensor employs Ag nanocubes wrapped with Co-Ni layered double hydroxide (AgNCs@Co-Ni LDH) as a functional matrix, offering both superior SERS enhancement and efficient VOC adsorption properties. To achieve selective detection, AgNCs@Co-Ni LDH were incorporated into agarose hydrogels along with 3-methyl-2-benzothiazolinone hydrazone (MBTH). The resulting MBTH-AgNCs@Co-Ni LDH/hydrogel-patch facilitate the oxidation of hexanal, enabling simultaneous colorimetric and SERS signal generation, while producing acrizine, a blue-colored reaction product. This hydrogel-based dual-mode sensing platform exhibits high selectivity, excellent stability, and precision in SERS-based hexanal detection. The detection limit for the SERS method was determined to be as low as 3.34x10-13 M. Furthermore, developed and optimized compact CNN-based multi-terminal intelligent recognition system for enhanced hydrogel-patch detection through AI-driven, portable, and real-time colorimetric analysis. Therefore, this work not only enables the effective detection of hexanal in the exhaled breath of suspected lung cancer patients, underscoring its potential for early lung cancer screening, but also establishes a foundation for the development of multimodal hydrogel biosensors for broader applications in disease diagnosis.

</details>

### [62] [Axial $U(1)$ Symmetry Breaking in Hot QCD: From Topology to the Chiral Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21907)
*Heng-Tong Ding*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该综述整合理论进展与格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）第一性原理计算，探讨热密物质中有效轴矢 $U(1)$ 对称性（effective axial $U(1)$ restoration）能否在长距离上恢复，并分析其对手征相变（chiral phase transition）临界行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常加热物质可恢复低温下自发破缺的对称性，但量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）的轴矢 $U(1)$ 对称性在经典无质量夸克理论中存在，却因轴矢反常（axial anomaly）在量子化时破缺，且该反常在任何温度下都持续存在。因此需要考察热物质能否在长距离上表现为该对称性有效恢复。

Method: 综述汇合理论发展与格点 QCD 第一性原理计算，考察胶子场拓扑（gluon-field topology）如何塑造低能狄拉克模（Dirac modes）及其关联，以及这些模如何在不同空间尺度上贡献轴矢破缺。

Result: 有效轴矢 $U(1)$ 恢复是否发生，取决于微观夸克与胶子动力学如何决定轴矢破缺的强度与空间范围；相关分析进一步揭示其对夸克质量趋零时手征相变级数与临界行为（critical behavior）的含义。

Conclusion: 有效轴矢 $U(1)$ 对称性的长距离恢复问题与手征相变的级数和临界行为密切相关，需要结合胶子拓扑、低能狄拉克模及其跨尺度关联加以理解。

Abstract: Heating matter can restore symmetries spontaneously broken at low temperature. The axial $U(1)$ symmetry of quantum chromodynamics (QCD) is different: it is present in the classical theory with massless quarks but is broken upon quantization by the axial anomaly. The anomaly persists at every temperature, yet its observable effects can weaken. Can hot matter nevertheless behave as though this symmetry were restored at long distances? Whether such effective axial $U(1)$ restoration occurs depends on how microscopic quark and gluon dynamics governs the strength and spatial range of axial breaking.
  This review brings together theoretical developments and first-principles lattice QCD calculations. We examine how gluon-field topology shapes the low-lying Dirac modes and their correlations, how these modes contribute to axial breaking at different spatial scales, and what this implies for the order and critical behavior of the chiral phase transition as quark masses approach zero.

</details>

### [63] [Gravitational and mass distribution effects on stationary superwinds](https://arxiv.org/abs/1608.01266)
*Gabriel Alejandro Añorve-Zeferino*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该工作将非均匀动力学质量分布及其引力场引入稳态星系超级风（galactic superwind）模型，推导解析解并划分流态与金属增丰情景，进而约束引力、自引力与辐射冷却抑制稳态流的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有超级风解析模型通常忽略质量分布的非均匀性及其引力影响，难以刻画不同星系间外流的差异。为与宇宙学模拟、星系巡天观测及金属丰度关系对比，需要建立包含暗物质与重子晕多种轮廓的通用解析框架。

Method: 以恒星风与超新星（supernovae, SNe）的质量和能量注入作为类比注入源，考虑不超出星系光学半径R_opt的致密暗物质与重子晕，以及引力相互作用的延展晕；采用与近期宇宙学模拟和观测估计一致的晕轮廓，推导超级风存在的解析判据、流态与增丰情景，并给出抑制稳态流的近似参数上限。

Result: 获得自由超级风水动力学剖面的解析表达式；超级风解的存在或被抑制，强烈依赖于动力学质量的陡峭度与集中度以及质量、能量注入率。结果与观测数据及近期数值工作进行了比较。

Conclusion: 结果被置于质量—金属丰度关系（mass-metallicity relationship）语境中，用于讨论最小质量星系选择性丢失金属的观测证据，并兼论大质量星系的情形。

Abstract: Here, we model the effect of non-uniform dynamical mass distributions and their associated gravitational fields on the stationary galactic superwind solution. We do this by considering an analogue injection of mass and energy from stellar winds and SNe. We consider both compact dark-matter and baryonic haloes that does not extend further from the galaxies optical radii $R_{\rm opt}$ as well as extended gravitationally-interacting ones. We consider halo profiles that emulate the results of recent cosmological simulations and coincide also with observational estimations from galaxy surveys. This allows to compare the analytical superwind solution with outflows from different kinds of galaxies. We give analytical formulae that establish when an outflow is possible and also characterize distinct flow regimes and enrichment scenarios. We also constraint the parameter space by giving approximate limits above which gravitation, self-gravitation and radiative cooling can inhibit the stationary flow. We obtain analytical expressions for the free superwind hydrodynamical profiles. We find that the existence or inhibition of the superwind solution highly depends on the steepness and concentration of the dynamical mass and the mass and energy injection rates. We compare our results with observational data and a recent numerical work. We put our results in the context of the mass-metallicity relationship to discuss observational evidence related to the selective loss of metals from the least massive galaxies and also discuss the case of massive galaxies.

</details>

### [64] [FrFNO:Injecting the analytic Mittag-Leffler propagator into a resolution-robust neural operator for space-time fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21512)
*Guofei Pang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出分数阶傅里叶神经算子（Fractional Fourier Neural Operator, FrFNO），将冻结线性部分的解析 Mittag–Leffler 响应预计算为分辨率无关的传播子表，仅用谱卷积网络学习变量系数与非线性对流引起的残差，并连续条件化于分数阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶偏微分方程（fractional partial differential equations）耦合记忆依赖的时间导数与非局部分数阶拉普拉斯算子（fractional Laplacian），在不同分数阶、初值或扩散场下重复求解代价高昂。神经算子（neural operators）虽可作快速代理，但标准架构需从数据中重新学习主导的线性分数阶演化。

Method: FrFNO 为分辨率鲁棒的条件算子：将冻结线性部分的解析 Mittag–Leffler 响应以一次预计算、分辨率无关的传播子表形式注入，并仅在变量系数与非线性对流导致的残差上训练谱卷积网络，同时对两个分数阶连续条件化。

Result: 理论表明，在零样本超分辨率（zero-shot super-resolution）下，注入的传播子填补了标准傅里叶神经算子置零的带外模态（out-of-band modes），将带外全场尾部替换为更小的残差尾部（弱扰动下为 K 无关的常数因子缩减）；残差与全解具有相同 Sobolev 阶，故优势来自幅值缩减而非更陡尾部。二维非线性时空分数阶 Burgers 问题上，FrFNO 在训练分辨率与零样本超分辨率下取得六种基线（FNO、PINO、PDNO、CNO、DeepONet、U-Net）中最低相对 L² 误差，谱相位误差最小，并在整数阶极限与长积分窗口下保持最优。

Conclusion: 该构造可扩展至耦合分数阶系统，如分数阶 Allen–Cahn 与 Navier–Stokes 方程；网格加密使误差降至残差控制的误差下限。代码、训练脚本与参考输出已公开。

Abstract: Fractional partial differential equations couple a memory-dependent time derivative with a nonlocal fractional Laplacian, and their repeated solution under varying fractional orders, initial data, or diffusivity fields is computationally expensive. Neural operators offer a fast surrogate, but standard architectures must relearn the dominant linear fractional evolution from data. We propose the \emph{Fractional Fourier Neural Operator} (FrFNO), a resolution-robust conditional operator. It injects the analytic Mittag--Leffler response of the frozen linear part as a once-precomputed, resolution-independent propagator table, and trains a spectral convolutional network only on the residual induced by variable coefficients and nonlinear advection, conditioned continuously on both fractional orders. The central theoretical result is that under zero-shot super-resolution the injected propagator fills the out-of-band modes that a standard Fourier neural operator sets to zero, replacing the out-of-band full-field tail by the smaller residual tail (a $K$-independent constant-factor reduction under weak perturbation). The residual and the full solution carry the same Sobolev order, so the advantage is amplitude reduction rather than a steeper tail. Mesh refinement drives the error to a residual-controlled floor. On a nonlinear two-dimensional space--time fractional Burgers problem FrFNO achieves the lowest relative $L^2$ error among six baselines (FNO, PINO, PDNO, CNO, DeepONet, U-Net) at the training resolution and under zero-shot super-resolution, yields the smallest spectral phase error, and remains the best at the integer-order limit and for long integration windows. The same construction extends to coupled fractional systems such as fractional Allen-Cahn and Navier--Stokes equations. Code, training scripts, and reference outputs are available at https://github.com/Derek2021Pang/FrFNO.

</details>

### [65] [Equivalence of states and bimodule quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20879)
*Linzhe Huang,Chunlan Jiang,Zhengwei Liu,Jinsong Wu*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文在冯诺依曼代数（von Neumann algebra）框架下研究量子信道（quantum channel）对态的作用，以子代数（subalgebra）编码守恒量来定义“相”（phase）为保子代数的正规态等价类。借助Petz恢复映射（Petz recovery map）给出相的相对准熵（relative quasi-entropy）刻画，并证明在不可约有限指标II₁型子因子（irreducible finite-index subfactor）情形下相等价蕴含算子代数对称性等价，从而为相变检测提供机制并给出相基数的有限上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画量子信道作用下、以子代数编码守恒量的态空间结构，并为量子相与相变提供代数化判据。作者试图将“相”定义为保守恒量的正规态等价类，以统一描述信道演化下的不变量与对称性破缺现象。

Method: 方法上，首先用Petz恢复映射建立相等价的相对准熵刻画；随后引入态的算子代数（operator algebra, OA）对称性，并借助双投影（biprojection）的双移位（bishift）定义OA对称性之间的等价关系。在𝓝⊆𝓜为II₁型不可约有限指标子因子时，结合有限指标子因子理论与量子傅里叶分析（quantum Fourier analysis）进行证明。

Result: 主要结果为：在II₁型不可约有限指标子因子情形下，相等价蕴含OA对称性等价，因此OA对称性破缺可作为检测相变的充分机制。此外，证明每个相的基数（cardinality）有限，并给出仅依赖Jones指标（Jones index）的显式上界。

Conclusion: 结论表明，守恒量子信道所定义的相可通过相对准熵与OA对称性等价联系，实现代数量子相变分析。相基数有限且上界由Jones指标控制，凸显子因子指标在相结构定量刻画中的核心作用。

Abstract: We study the action of quantum channels on states on a von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}$ with conserved quantities encoded by a subalgebra $\mathcal{N}$. We define a phase as an equivalence class of normal states on $\mathcal{M}$ under the action of quantum channels preserving $\mathcal{N}$. First, we establish a relative quasi-entropic characterization of phase equivalence using the Petz recovery map. We further introduce the operator algebra (OA) symmetry of a state and define an equivalence relation between OA symmetries via bishifts of biprojections. When $\mathcal{N}\subseteq\mathcal{M}$ is an irreducible finite-index subfactor of type II$_1$, we prove that phase equivalence implies OA symmetry equivalence. Consequently, OA symmetry breaking provides a sufficient mechanism for detecting phase transitions. Furthermore, combining finite-index subfactor theory with quantum Fourier analysis, we prove that the cardinality of each phase is finite and obtain an explicit upper bound depending only on the Jones index.

</details>

### [66] [Flow, dynamics and active fracture in hydraulic multicellular systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21021)
*John D. Treado,Arthur Boutillon,Frank Jülicher,Otger Campàs*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个统一框架，将细胞力学（cellular mechanics）与流体流动（fluid flow）纳入同一理论体系，揭示水力（hydraulics）可显著影响组织行为，并在斑马鱼胚胎实验中获得证据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从间质空间（interstitial space）到腔状空隙（luminal cavities），流体压力与流动可重塑甚至重新定义生物组织，且流体既可能主导也可能响应细胞间力学相互作用。然而在细胞尺度上测量流动困难，导致组织水力（tissue hydraulics）难以理解，这构成本研究的核心动机。

Method: 研究建立一种理论方法（theoretical approach），在同一框架内同时刻画细胞力学（cellular mechanics）与流体流动（fluid flow），并辅以实验验证，即在早期斑马鱼胚胎（zebrafish embryos）中注入增稠剂（thickening agent）以考察水力对细胞运动的影响。

Result: 结果显示水力可大幅影响组织行为：细胞形状与尺寸间的液压力学耦合（hydraulic coupling）主导组织对渗透冲击（osmotic shock）的响应；调节组织渗透率（permeabilities）可使流体在细胞膜之间或跨膜流动；在活性组织（active tissues）中，水力可将细胞迁移性（cell mobility）抑制至断裂（fracture）程度，并发现一种液压力学棘轮（hydraulic ratchet），驱动流体排出细胞以生成小的腔状空隙。注入增稠剂的斑马鱼胚胎实验提供了水力抑制细胞运动的证据。

Conclusion: 结果表明水力可能普遍支配多种多细胞系统（multicellular systems）的行为，凸显将流体力学纳入组织尺度建模的必要性。

Abstract: From interstitial space to luminal cavities, fluid pressure and flow can remodel, reshape and even redefine a biological tissue. Fluids can either govern or react to mechanical interactions between cells. However, measuring flows at cellular scales is difficult, which makes it challenging to understand tissue hydraulics. Here, we develop a theoretical approach that captures cellular mechanics and fluid flow in one framework. We find that hydraulics can drastically influence tissue behavior. Hydraulic coupling between cell shape and size governs a tissue's response to osmotic shock, while tuning a tissue's permeabilities can channel fluid either between or across cell membranes. In active tissues, hydraulics can suppress cell mobility to the point of fracture, where we discover a hydraulic ratchet that drives fluid out of cells to generate small luminal spaces. We find experimental evidence that hydraulics can suppress cell motion in early stage zebrafish embryos injected with a thickening agent, which indicates that hydraulics may generally govern the behaviors of many multicellular systems.

</details>
