### [67] [Pressure and Proximity Tuned Twisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20958)
*David T. S. Perkins,Joseph J. Betouras*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMD）封装的双层扭转石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene），发现其具有非径向自旋织构（non-radial spin texture）却仍呈现纯共线Edelstein效应（collinear Edelstein effect），并揭示单轴压力（uniaxial pressure）可调控能带与费米面数目，以及量子几何（quantum geometry）中贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的分布变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状原子级薄材料间由范德华力（van der Waals forces）介导的耦合可产生强电子关联、独特拓扑特征与非平庸自旋织构，对自旋、谷与轨道电子学器件具有重要意义。研究据此探究封装与压力对莫尔异质结构（moiré heterostructures）拓扑与自旋-电荷转换过程的调控能力。

Method: 采用过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides）封装的扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）体系，分析其自旋织构与Edelstein效应；并通过施加单轴压力（uniaxial pressure）调节能带结构，考察费米面数目及量子几何（quantum geometry）随压力的变化。

Result: 该封装扭转双层石墨烯呈现普适的非径向自旋织构（non-radial spin texture），但仍具有纯共线Edelstein效应（collinear Edelstein effect），尽管缺乏径向对称性。单轴压力可在不损害共线响应的前提下调控能带结构并改变活跃费米面（Fermi surfaces）数目；较大压力下，贝里曲率（Berry curvature）在莫尔布里渊区（moiré Brillouin zone）大范围内扩展，量子几何随之改变。

Conclusion: 封装与压力可作为有效手段，显著改变莫尔异质结构（moiré heterostructures）的拓扑性质与自旋-电荷转换（spin-charge interconversion）过程。

Abstract: The coupling between layered atomically-thin materials mediated by van der Waals forces allows strong electronic correlations, unique topological signatures, and non-trivial spin textures, all of which play an important role in the creation of spin-, valley-, and orbitronic devices. Here, we demonstrate that twisted bilayer graphene encapsulated by transition metal dichalcogenides exhibits a generically non-radial spin texture yet hosts a purely collinear Edelstein effect despite this lack of radial symmetry. Moreover, we show how uniaxial pressure can be used to further tune the band structure and change the number of active Fermi surfaces without compromising the collinear response. Lastly, we illustrate how the quantum geometry changes in the encapsulated twisted graphene bilayer with larger pressures spreading the Berry curvature over large regions of the moiré Brillouin zone. These results illustrate how encapsulation and pressure can be used to drastically alter the topology and spin-charge interconversion processes of moiré heterostructures.

</details>


### [68] [Singular high-harmonic transport above a quantum threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20990)
*Yugo Onishi,Su-Yang Xu,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作揭示小带隙绝缘体中的Landau-Zener隧穿会产生奇异的直流电流—电压关系，并在交流输运中引发强高次谐波产生，其高次谐波响应随谐波阶数呈亚指数衰减，并给出电流幅度的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索固体中基于频率倍增的太赫兹（THz）产生途径，并理解小带隙绝缘体中隧穿机制主导的非线性输运行为与普通导体、PN二极管的差异。

Method: 方法基于对小带隙绝缘体中Landau-Zener隧穿的理论分析，考察其直流电流—电压关系及交流输运下的高次谐波产生，并推导频率为 nω 的电流幅度在基频电场 E（频率 ω）作用下的标度律。

Result: 结果显示，小带隙绝缘体产生奇异的直流电流—电压关系，交流输运中表现出强高次谐波产生；高次谐波响应随谐波阶数呈亚指数衰减，其衰减速度在参数上慢于普通导体和PN二极管。

Conclusion: 结论表明，该机制为基于固体中频率倍增的太赫兹产生提供了一条有前景的途径。

Abstract: We show that Landau-Zener tunneling in a small-gap insulator produces a singular DC current--voltage relation, and correspondingly, strong high-harmonic generation in AC transport. Remarkably, high-harmonic responses decrease subexponentially with harmonic order, which is parametrically slower than in ordinary conductors and PN diodes. We further derive a scaling law for the current amplitude at frequency $nω$ produced by the applied electric field $E$ at fundamental frequency $ω$. Our work offers a promising route to THz generation based on frequency multiplication in solids.

</details>


### [69] [Bend Contour Electron Tomography (BCET): Quantitative strain and topography mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21041)
*Henry G. Bell,Chenhang Xu,Arthur McCray,Minyong Han,Yicheng Zhuang,Harold Y. Hwang,Colin Ophus,Alfred Zong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出弯曲等值线电子断层扫描（BCET）计算框架，将透射电镜图像中的弯曲等值线转换为应变与形貌的定量二维映射，并在自支撑 SrTiO₃ 薄膜上验证重构效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自支撑量子材料薄膜天然形成亚微米、非均匀应变场，显著影响其电子、磁性与结构性质；但现有定量解析介观特征的方法主要依赖扫描探针，难以支持单发成像、飞秒显微等动态测量。

Method: BCET 通过迭代最小化实验与模拟弯曲等值线图像之间的设计损失函数（loss function），在无需扫描或衍射映射的条件下同时反演表面形貌与面内应变张量场（in-plane strain tensor fields）。

Result: 将该方法应用于自支撑 SrTiO₃ 薄膜，成功以高保真度重构了局部应变分布与曲率场（curvature field）。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用宽场成像（wide-field imaging）定量表征自支撑薄膜介观结构提供了框架，为研究二维量子材料中空间不均匀性如何主导相变与非平衡动力学开辟新途径。

Abstract: Freestanding thin films of quantum materials naturally develop sub-micrometer, nonuniform strain fields that strongly affect their electronic, magnetic, and structural properties in both equilibrium and nonequilibrium conditions. However, current methods to quantitatively resolve these mesoscopic features are primarily restricted to scanning probes, making it challenging for dynamical measurements such as single-shot imaging and femtosecond microscopy. Here, we present a new computational framework which we denote as bend contour electron tomography (BCET), which efficiently converts bend contours in transmission electron microscope images into quantitative two-dimensional maps of strain and topography. By iteratively minimizing a designed loss function between experimental and simulated bend contour images, BCET retrieves both surface morphology and in-plane strain tensor fields without requiring scanning or diffraction mapping. We applied BCET to freestanding SrTiO$_3$ thin films, demonstrating the successful reconstruction of the local strain distribution and curvature field with high fidelity. Our approach provides a quantitative framework for characterizing mesoscale structures in freestanding films using wide-field imaging, opening new avenues to investigate how spatial inhomogeneity governs phase transitions and nonequilibrium dynamics in two-dimensional quantum materials.

</details>


### [70] [Defect-mediated orientational ordering in stripe-forming magnetic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21091)
*Premarun Barik,Johan van Lierop,Robert L. Stamps*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本工作通过淬火（quench）实验研究二维条带状磁性系统中的取向序（orientational order）演化，提出以T型结点（T-junction）缺陷动力学为核心的微观描述框架，并在实空间与倒空间同时给出证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条带状磁性系统中竞争相互作用（competing interactions）导致的取向序建立过程尚缺乏微观层面的机制刻画，尤其缺乏对超畴（super-domain）重组过程的可量化描述。

Method: 将二维条纹形成磁性系统淬火至条纹有序区（stripe-ordered regime），追踪水平与垂直条纹所构成的取向区域（超畴）及其间缺陷的运动与消除，并以T型结点数量作为超畴重组的微观度量；同时考察温度、系统尺寸与平衡条纹宽度对时间尺度的影响，并将实空间缺陷动力学与结构因子（structure factor）对应。

Result: T型结点数目随演化呈不同动力学区间，分别对应超畴的形成、生长与平衡化；温度、系统尺寸和平衡条纹宽度改变这些特征时间尺度，热涨落（thermal fluctuations）在较高温度下维持有限缺陷密度；缺陷消除过程在结构因子中呈现可辨识的倒空间信号。

Conclusion: 结果建立了以缺陷为核心、描述条纹形成磁性系统中取向序建立的微观图景，并为超畴重组的实空间观测与倒空间特征之间提供联系。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium development of orientational order in a two-dimensional stripe-forming magnetic system with competing interactions. Following a quench into the stripe-ordered regime, horizontal and vertical stripes organize into orientational regions(super-domains) separated by characteristic defects. We show that the evolution towards a globally oriented stripe state is mediated by the motion and elimination of these defects, with T-junctions providing a direct microscopic measure of super-domain reorganization. The T-junction population reveals distinct dynamical regimes associated with super-domain formation, growth, and equilibration. Temperature, system size, and equilibrium stripe width modify the characteristic time scales of this evolution, while thermal fluctuations produce a finite defect population at higher temperatures. We further show that the defect dynamics observed in real space are reflected in the structure factor, providing a reciprocal-space signature of defect elimination. These results establish a microscopic defect-based description of orientational ordering in stripe-forming magnetic systems.

</details>


### [71] [Projective representation theory of spin space groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21289)
*Zheng Zhang,Peiyuan Wang,Y. X. Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文借助 Mackey 群扩张理论，构建了涵盖共线、共面与非共面磁性晶体的自旋空间群（spin space group）投影不可约协表示（irreducible corepresentation）统一构造框架，并将其应用于弱自旋轨道耦合材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋空间群是描述弱自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）磁性晶体的自然对称性框架，但需要一般的不可约表示理论来提取物理结果。其核心困难在于自旋空间群以投影方式实现：因子系统（factor system）可使格矢平移非对易、在动量空间诱导非简单（nonsymmorphic）作用，并改变小余群（little cogroup）的投影结构，这些均超出传统空间群与双群表示理论的范畴。

Method: 基于 Mackey 的群扩张理论（Mackey's theory of group extensions），建立适用于所有自旋空间群的统一构造框架，涵盖反幺正对称性（antiunitary symmetry）与一般因子系统。关键技术结果是因子系统 ν 的规范分解：平移因子 σ、平移与点群操作的混合因子 γ、点群因子 α。并以动量空间轨道上的诱导方法构造全部投影不可约协表示。

Result: 该分解明确揭示了普通表示理论的修正方式：σ 决定投影平移代数与相应的布里渊区；γ 控制动量空间群作用并可能使其成为非简单型；α 贡献于小余群的因子系统。框架确定了自旋空间群可实现哪些非简单动量空间对称性，并揭示出布里渊空间为紧致平坦流形（compact flat manifold）而非环面、对称性强制的 Zak 相位、重构的高对称动量与能带简并，以及新型准粒子。

Conclusion: 结果为弱自旋轨道耦合磁性材料的系统研究奠定了表示论基础。

Abstract: Spin space groups provide the natural symmetry framework for magnetic crystals with weak spin-orbit coupling, but extracting their physical consequences requires a general theory of irreducible representations. The central difficulty is that spin space groups are represented projectively: their factor systems can render lattice translations noncommuting, induce nonsymmorphic actions in momentum space, and modify the projective structure of little cogroups. These effects lie beyond conventional space-group and double-group representation theory. Here, using Mackey's theory of group extensions, we develop a unified constructive framework for all collinear, coplanar, and noncoplanar spin space groups, including antiunitary symmetries and general factor systems. A central technical result is a canonical decomposition of the relevant factor system $ν$ into a translational factor $σ$, a mixed factor $γ$ coupling translations to point-group operations, and a point-group factor $α$. This decomposition makes transparent how ordinary representation theory is modified: $σ$ determines the projective translation algebra and the appropriate Brillouin zone, $γ$ controls the momentum-space group action and can make it nonsymmorphic, and $α$ contributes to the factor systems of little cogroups. On this basis, we construct all projective irreducible corepresentations by induction over momentum-space orbits. The framework identifies which nonsymmorphic momentum-space symmetries can be realized by spin space groups and reveals Brillouin spaces that are compact flat manifolds rather than tori, symmetry-enforced Zak phases, reconstructed high-symmetry momenta and band degeneracies, and new types of quasiparticles. Our results establish the representation-theoretic foundation for systematic studies of weak-spin-orbit-coupled magnetic materials.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察核壳纳米结构中里德伯激子的光学行为，并明确激子能量对体系几何与维度的依赖关系，以拓展对受限激子（confined excitons）体系的理解。

Method: 方法包括计算电子与空穴的本征函数（eigenfunctions）和本征能量（eigenenergies），据此求激子能量及其对系统几何和维度的依赖；光学性质采用实密度矩阵方法（Real Density Matrix Approach）进行计算。

Result: 计算结果给出了激子能量随体系几何与维度的变化关系，并讨论了极小与极大结构两个极限情形，所得结果同时适用于低主量子数激子与里德伯态（Rydberg states）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了适用于含里德伯激子的Cu$_2$O-金属核壳纳米结构光学性质的分析框架，其结论在从低主量子数激子到里德伯态、以及从小尺寸到大尺寸结构的范围内均具适用性。

Abstract: Optical properties of Cu$_2$O-metal core-shell nanostructures containing Rydberg excitons are examined. Calculations of electron and hole eigenfunctions and eigenenergies are used to calculate exciton energy and its dependence on system geometry and dimensionality. The limits of very small and very large structures are discussed, making the results applicable to low principal quantum number excitons as well as to Rydberg states. The Real Density Matrix Approach method is used to calculate the optical properties of the studied systems.

</details>


### [73] [Floquet-spin-orbit compensation and flat- and quadratic-band contact in the $α$-$T_3$ lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21586)
*Imtiaz Khan,Muzamil Shah,Reza Asgari,Gao Xianlong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究三带 α-T₃ 体系（介于石墨烯与骰子晶格之间）中 Floquet–自旋轨道补偿，识别出三类能带简并条件，并表明本征横向热电响应可编码这些谱结构，从而实现 α 与谱能标的逆推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯（graphene）与骰子晶格（dice lattice）之间的连续插值体系 α-T₃ 具有可调的平带与锥形能带结构，但 Floquet 调控与内禀自旋轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）的协同补偿效应尚缺乏系统刻画；本文旨在识别该补偿点处的能带简并模式及其可观测响应。

Method: 结合非共振圆偏振光场（off-resonant circularly polarized optical field）与内禀自旋轨道耦合调节准能谱（quasienergy spectrum），利用连续哈密顿量与三带 Kubo 计算，分析简并条件、卷绕数为二的低能哈密顿量（winding-two low-energy Hamiltonian）及贝里曲率（Berry-curvature）极大值。

Result: 识别出三类成对能带简并：(i) 谷–自旋简并（valley–spin degeneracy）、(ii) 自旋–能带简并（spin–band degeneracy）、(iii) 平带–色散带简并（flat–dispersive band degeneracy）；平带为连续哈密顿量的精确性质，对应动量无关的暗态（dark state）。失谐该接触点后出现卷绕数为二的低能哈密顿量与半径按失谐量平方根标度的贝里曲率极大值。

Conclusion: 本征横向热电响应（transverse thermoelectric response）可编码上述谱结构；在 0<α<1/2 区间，由于与线性接触相关的两个响应标度之比值单调且与共同自旋轨道能标无关，可预测平带–二次带简并，并实现 α 与共同谱能标的逆推。三带 Kubo 计算确认了该逆表征的可观测区间，连接了可实验探测的贝里曲率敏感热电响应与可调 Floquet–自旋轨道能带几何。

Abstract: We investigate Floquet--spin-orbit compensation in the three-band $α$-$T_3$ system, which interpolates between graphene at $α=0$ and the dice lattice at $α=1$. By tuning the interplay between an off-resonant circularly polarized optical field and intrinsic spin--orbit coupling, we identify three distinct pairwise band-degeneracy conditions in the quasienergy spectrum: (i) valley--spin degeneracy, (ii) spin--band degeneracy, and (iii) flat--dispersive band degeneracy. The flat band is not an artifact of a two-band reduction but rather an exact property of the continuum Hamiltonian at this compensation point, where the complete three-band Hamiltonian supports a momentum-independent dark state. A winding-two low-energy Hamiltonian and a finite-momentum Berry-curvature maximum, whose radius scales as the square root of the detuning, emerge from detuning this contact. We further show that the intrinsic transverse thermoelectric response encodes these spectrum patterns. The flat- and quadratic-band degeneracy can be predicted for $0<α<1/2$ because the two response scales linked to the linear contacts have a monotonic ratio independent of the common spin--orbit energy scale. This allows an inverse determination of $α$ and of the common spectral scale. The analytical results are confirmed by full three-band Kubo calculations, which also define the regime in which this inverse characterization is still observable. These results connect an experimentally accessible, Berry-curvature-sensitive thermoelectric response to tunable Floquet--spin--orbit band geometry.

</details>


### [74] [Transport statistics and thermoelectric performance of Hofstadters butterfly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21588)
*Friedemann Queißer,Gernot Schaller*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究考察在磁场下可沿正方形晶格隧穿、并连接两个电子电极的电子体系，利用非平衡格林函数（non-equilibrium Green's functions）计算透射率，进而得到粒子流、能量流与零频噪声，发现透射率可在较宽频率窗口内达到1的“甜点”（sweet spots），其由拓扑保护的边界模式（topologically protected boundary modes）承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索磁场下晶格电子体系与电子电极耦合时的输运特性，并寻找可用于鲁棒电流标准、时钟与热电器件的候选机制。

Method: 采用非平衡格林函数方法，计算体系透射率，并由透射率导出粒子流、能量流以及零频噪声（zero-frequency noise）。

Result: 识别出透射率达到1的“甜点”（sweet spots），且在较宽频率窗口内形成透射平台（transmission plateaus）；该透射由拓扑保护的边界模式承载，对格点能（on-site energies）的局域涨落具有较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这些透射平台可被用于实现鲁棒的电流标准、时钟与热电器件（thermoelectric devices）。

Abstract: We investigate electrons subject to a magnetic field that may tunnel along a square lattice and additionally to two electronic leads. Using non-equilibrium Greens functions we evaluate the transmission and from that particle and energy currents and zero-frequency noise of the setup. We identify sweet spots for which the transmission reaches unity over broad frequency windows -- carried by topologically protected boundary modes that are quite robust against local fluctuations of on-site energies. These transmission plateaus can be exploited for the implementation robust current standards, clocks and thermoelectric devices.

</details>


### [75] [Spatially resolved quantum magnetometry and stray-field reconstruction of permalloy microdisks using boron-vacancy centers in hexagonal boron nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21630)
*Peiting Wen,Shuyu Wen,Jiang Qu,Zeling Xiong,Katrin Schultheiss,Slawomir Prucnal,Artur Erbe,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jürgen Lindner,Jürgen Fassbender,Manfred Helm,Shengqiang Zhou,Ruslan Salikhov,Helmut Schultheiss,Yonder Berencén*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将可转移的六方氮化硼（hBN）薄片与4微米直径坡莫合金（Py）微盘集成，在室温下通过空间分辨光探测磁共振（ODMR）定量重建了涡旋态磁化产生的面外杂散场分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可转移hBN中负电荷硼空位（VB$^{-}$）自旋缺陷为集成量子磁测量提供了通用平台，但对磁性微结构的定量成像仍具挑战性。

Method: 将转移hBN薄片与4 μm直径坡莫合金（Py = Ni81Fe19）微盘集成，在室温下进行空间分辨光探测磁共振（ODMR）测量；施加面内磁场使涡旋态磁化畸变，并通过参考每个像素的局域零场分裂（zero-field splitting）及校正残余面外偏置场实现定量重建。

Result: 面内磁场在微盘相对边缘产生边缘局域磁表面电荷与显著杂散场特征；定量重建得到面外杂散场分布，峰值场约11.2 mT；边缘电荷模型可复现重建场的空间分布与幅度，将ODMR响应与涡旋态的场驱动演化相关联。

Conclusion: 结果确立了转移hBN VB$^{-}$传感器用于磁性微结构定量磁测量的可行性。

Abstract: Transferable hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) hosting negatively charged boron-vacancy (VB$^{-}$) spin defects offers a versatile platform for integrated quantum magnetometry, yet quantitative imaging of magnetic microstructures remains challenging. Here, we integrate a transferred hBN flake with a 4 $μ$m-diameter permalloy (Py = Ni${81}$Fe${19}$) microdisk and perform spatially resolved optically detected magnetic resonance measurements at room temperature. An applied in-plane magnetic field distorts the vortex-state magnetization, generating edge-localized magnetic surface charges and pronounced stray-field signatures at opposite disk edges. By referencing each pixel to its local zero-field splitting and correcting for a residual out-of-plane bias field, we quantitatively reconstruct the out-of-plane stray-field distribution, revealing peak fields of approximately 11.2 mT. An edge-charge model reproduces the spatial distribution and amplitude of the reconstructed field, linking the ODMR response to the field-driven evolution of the vortex state. These results establish transferred hBN VB$^{-}$ sensors for quantitative magnetometry of magnetic microstructures.

</details>


### [76] [Field-free and Sub-ns Magnetization Reversal in Synthetic Anti-ferromagnets using Sequential Spin-Orbit Torque and Spin-Transfer Torque Pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21669)
*Subham Das,Naveen Sisodia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文对采用合成反铁磁（Synthetic Antiferromagnet, SAF）自由层结构的垂直磁隧道结（p-MTJ）进行数值研究，利用自旋轨道矩（Spin Orbit Torque, SOT）与自旋转移矩（Spin Transfer Torque, STT）顺序施加的亚纳秒电流脉冲，实现亚纳秒且无外场辅助的磁化翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单自由层垂直磁各向异性（perpendicular easy axis）磁隧道结（Magnetic Tunnel Junctions, MTJs）已展示快速确定性翻转、非易失性与低功耗特性，但其自由层结构在亚纳秒时间尺度下仍需进一步优化，本文旨在探索基于合成反铁磁（SAF）自由层结构的 p-MTJ 的亚纳秒、无场翻转特性。

Method: 采用数值方法研究 p-MTJ，施加顺序的亚纳秒 SOT 与 STT 电流脉冲；系统考察 SOT 与 STT 电流幅度及脉冲宽度的多种脉冲协议（pulse protocols），并考虑参考层（Reference Layer, RL）的影响，同时在热涨落（thermal fluctuations）存在下分析 SOT、STT 参数的局限性。

Result: 观察到显著的确定性翻转（deterministic switching）区域；其中施加于某一层的 SOT 脉冲使耦合层进入面内构型（in-plane configuration），从而缩短翻转过渡时间，随后的 STT 脉冲决定翻转方向；在热涨落存在时，SOT 与 STT 参数存在受限范围。

Conclusion: SAF 自由层结构的 p-MTJ 可借助 SOT 与 STT 顺序脉冲实现亚纳秒、无场确定性翻转，且参考层与热涨落对 SOT、STT 参数的允许窗口具有限制作用。

Abstract: Single free-layer Magnetic Tunnel Junctions (MTJs) with a perpendicular easy axis driven by spin currents have demonstrated fast, deterministic switching, non-volatility, and low-power memory capabilities when integrated with modern nanoelectronic devices. In this article, we numerically investigate sub-ns and field-free switching characteristics of a p-MTJ with a Synthetic Antiferromagnet (SAF) based free layer structure using sequential sub-ns current pulses of Spin Orbit Torque (SOT) and Spin Transfer Torque (STT). While the SOT pulse applied to one layer decreases the transition time by bringing the coupled layers into an in-plane configuration, the subsequent STT pulse on the system determines the switching direction. Various pulse protocols have been investigated for both SOT and STT current amplitude and pulse widths, while also considering the effect of the Reference Layer (RL). While significant regions of deterministic switching events are observed, limitations in SOT, STT parameters are further investigated in presence of thermal fluctuations.

</details>


### [77] [Impact of exciton-phonon and exciton-magnon interaction on transport in anisotropic 2D magnetic semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21746)
*Daniel Erkensten,Katarzyna Sadecka,Marie-Christin Heißenbüttel,Thorsten Deilmann,Ermin Malic*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立微观多体理论，研究单层与双层 CrSBr 中激子-声子与激子-磁振子相互作用，揭示准一维激子导致的各向异性散射与方向依赖扩散，并给出单声子与双磁振子过程特有的温度依赖特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁性半导体（two-dimensional magnetic semiconductors）是研究电荷、晶格、自旋与光相互作用的独特平台；强库仑相互作用（Coulomb interaction）与长程磁序使激子与晶格振动、自旋激发高效耦合，从而显著影响激子动力学与输运，但缺乏描述该耦合的微观理论框架。

Method: 构建单层与双层 CrSBr 中激子-声子（exciton–phonon）与激子-磁振子（exciton–magnon）相互作用的微观多体理论（microscopic many-body theory）。

Result: CrSBr 中激子的准一维（quasi-one-dimensional）性质导致激子-声子与激子-磁振子散射具有强各向异性（anisotropic），产生方向依赖的扩散系数（direction-dependent diffusion coefficients）；并识别出来自单声子（one-phonon）与双磁振子（two-magnon）散射过程的独特温度依赖，作为微观相互作用的特征信号。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解磁性范德华半导体（magnetic van der Waals semiconductors）中的激子输运建立了微观框架，并为未来光学光谱学（optical spectroscopy）与激子输运实验提供指导。

Abstract: Two-dimensional magnetic semiconductors provide a unique platform for exploring the interplay between charge, lattice, spin, and light. The strong Coulomb interaction combined with long-range magnetic order enables efficient coupling between excitons and both lattice vibrations and spin excitations, thereby strongly influencing exciton dynamics and transport in these materials. Here, we develop a microscopic many-body theory of exciton--phonon and exciton--magnon interactions in mono- and bilayer CrSBr. We demonstrate that the quasi-one-dimensional character of excitons in CrSBr gives rise to strongly anisotropic exciton--phonon and exciton--magnon scattering, resulting in direction-dependent diffusion coefficients. Furthermore, we identify a distinct temperature dependence originating from one-phonon and two-magnon scattering processes, providing characteristic signatures of the underlying microscopic interactions. Our work establishes a microscopic framework for understanding exciton transport in magnetic van der Waals semiconductors and provides guidance for future optical spectroscopy and exciton transport experiments.

</details>

### [78] [Sine-Gordon Model with Bosonic Tensor Networks: Continuum Matching and Soliton Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21846)
*Florian Hechenberger,Tommaso Rainaldi,Felix Ringer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用玻色张量网络（bosonic tensor networks）将哈密顿形式下的格点 sine-Gordon 模型与其连续场论定量连接，无需可调参数即复现孤子质量、色散关系及散射位移等精确预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在建立格点 sine-Gordon 模型与连续理论的定量联系，并为非可积扩展、其他哈密顿格点场论（含规范理论）的重整化以及连续变量量子模拟提供基础与基准。

Method: 将格点顶点算符（vertex operator）在自由玻色紫外不动点处匹配到其共形归一化，得到裸格点耦合与重整化连续质量参数的精确关系；采用均匀矩阵乘积态（uniform matrix product states）与准粒子试探波函数（quasiparticle ansatz），并模拟高斯孤子-反孤子波包在无反射点附近的实时碰撞。

Result: 孤子质量在所研究参数范围内逼近 Zamolodchikov 的精确连续预言；恢复了相对论性孤子色散关系与两个最轻呼吸子（breather）质量，精度达百分级；提取了 Wigner 空间位移并复现其随快度的特征依赖，与由透射相位动量导数得到的精确连续预言一致。

Conclusion: 玻色张量网络方法能够无拟合参数地从格点模型定量重构 sine-Gordon 连续场论的关键物理量，为向非可积情形推广及规范理论等格点场论的重整化研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We use bosonic tensor networks to connect the lattice sine-Gordon model in the Hamiltonian formulation quantitatively to its continuum theory. Matching the lattice vertex operator to its conformal normalization at the free-boson ultraviolet fixed point yields the exact relation between the bare lattice coupling and the renormalized continuum mass parameter. The soliton mass then approaches Zamolodchikov's exact continuum prediction throughout the studied parameter range, without adjustable parameters. Using uniform matrix product states and a quasiparticle ansatz, we also recover the relativistic soliton dispersion and the two lightest breather masses at the percent level. We simulate real-time collisions of Gaussian soliton-antisoliton wave packets near a reflectionless point and extract the Wigner spatial displacement. We compare this displacement with the exact continuum prediction obtained from the momentum derivative of the transmission phase, recovering its characteristic rapidity dependence. Our bosonic simulations provide a foundation for nonintegrable extensions, offer lessons for renormalization in other Hamiltonian lattice field theories, including gauge theories, and provide benchmarks for continuous-variable quantum simulations.

</details>


### [79] [Nonlocal Magic Spreading in Many-body Quantum Dynamics: From Chaotic Evolution to Quasi-particle Picture in Integrable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20951)
*Sreemayee Aditya,Piotr Sierant,Xhek Turkeshi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文将非局域魔法（nonlocal magic）与纠缠容量（capacity of entanglement）建立联系，从而对混沌与可积多体系统中魔法资源的动力学扩散给出可解析预测，并用大规模数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠（entanglement）与魔法（magic）揭示量子多体系统的互补特性，两者的相互作用由非局域魔法刻画，即对任意局域基变换保持不变的那部分魔法。然而二者显著不同的动力学行为，使这一不可约魔法分量如何扩散尚不清楚。

Method: 将非局域魔法与纠缠容量这一可处理的量（用于度量纠缠哈密顿量的涨落）联系起来，据此对从混沌到可积的多种多体动力学进行解析可控预测；同时开展大规模数值模拟；对可积系统发展准粒子图像（quasiparticle picture）。

Result: 混沌系统中，非局域魔法呈现瞬态积累，随时间对数增长，随后衰减至与尺寸无关的值。可积系统中，准粒子图像与数值定量一致，显示相同的初始增长最终饱和至与子系统尺寸成对数的值。这两类行为在纠缠窃取（entanglement embezzlement）上具有操作后果：最强扰乱者仅能瞬态窃取，而自由费米子动力学可在稳态维持普适窃取。

Conclusion: 非局域魔法与纠缠容量的联系为多体动力学中的魔法扩散提供了统一且可解析的框架，并揭示混沌与可积系统在资源动力学及纠缠窃取能力上的本质差异。

Abstract: Entanglement and magic are resources that reveal complementary aspects of quantum many-body systems. Their interplay is captured by nonlocal magic, the magic that survives arbitrary local changes of basis. Yet their markedly different dynamical behavior leaves open how this irreducible component of magic spreads. Here we connect nonlocal magic to the capacity of entanglement, a tractable quantity measuring fluctuations of the entanglement Hamiltonian. This connection enables analytically controlled predictions across a wide range of many-body dynamics, from chaotic to integrable systems, which we investigate also using large-scale numerical simulations. In chaotic systems, nonlocal magic exhibits a transient buildup, with logarithmic growth in time followed by decay to a size-independent value. For integrable systems, we develop a quasiparticle picture in quantitative agreement with numerics, showing that the same initial growth instead leads to saturation at a value logarithmic in subsystem size. These contrasting behaviors have an operational consequence for entanglement embezzlement: the strongest scramblers embezzle only transiently, whereas free-fermionic dynamics can sustain universal embezzlement in the steady state.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We examine the geometric interpretation of the quantum geometric tensor proposed in [Phys. Rev. B {\bf 110}, 035404 (2024)] for mixed quantum states, focusing on its imaginary part, which is proportional to a weighted sum of the Berry curvatures of the eigenstates of the density operator. While the real part naturally decomposes into Fisher--Rao and weighted Fubini--Study contributions, we show that the imaginary part does not, in general, coincide with the curvature of a connection on a globally defined U(1) line bundle whose holonomy yields a mixed-state geometric phase. Using a two-level system as an explicit example, we demonstrate that its surface integral depends on the choice of surface bounded by the same closed path, with ambiguities that are not integer multiples of $2π$.

Method: 保留准束缚谱的离散性以消除发散；在 Bohr-Sommerfeld 能级上评估均匀 Kemble 透射概率（uniform Kemble transmission probability），从而导出半经典 Arrhenius 定律的闭式结果。

Conclusion: 逃逸率低温发散源于连续谱近似，而非物理本质；采用离散准束缚谱并结合均匀半经典透射处理，可获得跨交叉温度统一且高精度的逃逸率表达式。

Abstract: The quantum-thermal escape rate from a metastable well in the absence of dissipation is customarily written as a Boltzmann average of the Hill-Wheeler flux over a continuum of energies. However, this continuum treatment diverges exponentially at low temperature. The divergence originates in a mismatch between a quantum partition function and a classical flux integral; retaining the discrete nature of the quasibound spectrum removes it exactly, and the resulting semiclassical Arrhenius law is obtained in closed form on both sides of the crossover temperature. Evaluating a uniform Kemble transmission probability on Bohr-Sommerfeld levels further recovers the standard WKB decay rate of the lowest resonance at zero temperature. Benchmarked against the resonances of cubic and quintic potentials, obtained by complex scaling, this uniform semiclassical result stays within $9\%$ of the exact rate over eleven orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [82] [Floquet physics from quantized light-matter interaction: geometric phases, gauge consistency, and entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21741)

TL;DR: 该工作通过将腔场表示于大光子数相位基（large-photon-number phase basis），证明经典驱动系统的时间依赖薛定谔方程可直接由全量子问题导出，从而在不使用经典轨迹替换或平均场解耦的前提下，建立 Floquet 物理与腔量子电动力学之间的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet 工程与腔量子电动力学（cavity quantum electrodynamics, cQED）虽为光-物质相互作用的两种互补描述，但二者在超出小耦合区域时的精确联系仍不清晰。本文旨在建立二者之间不依赖微扰与小耦合近似的严格对应。

Method: 将腔场以大规模光子数相位基（large-photon-number phase basis）表示，直接从全量子哈密顿量出发推导经典驱动系统的时间依赖薛定谔方程（time-dependent Schrödinger equation），既不将场替换为经典轨迹，也不假设平均场解耦（mean-field decoupling）；并利用量子哈密顿量层面严格保持的规范不变性（gauge invariance）保证对应关系的普适性。

Result: 建立了准能（quasienergies）与光子扇区平移后的量子能量之间的一一对应，并给出 Floquet 平均能量（Floquet mean energy）与 Anandan 相的量子解释。进一步表明光-物质纠缠（light-matter entanglement）被编码于对应 Floquet 模式（Floquet mode）的物质-谐振子关联中，由此识别出一类半经典极限（semiclassical limit），其中 Floquet 物理可在量子场本身并不趋近相干经典态的条件下出现。

Conclusion: 经典驱动与全量子描述之间的对应关系因规范不变性的精确保持而超越微扰与小耦合区域；Floquet 物理的涌现并不要求量子场进入经典相干态，而是与光-物质纠缠在 Floquet 模式中的关联结构相联系。

Abstract: Floquet engineering and cavity quantum electrodynamics represent two complementary descriptions of light-matter interaction, yet their precise connection beyond the small-coupling regime remains subtle. Here, by representing the cavity field in a large-photon-number phase basis, we show that the time-dependent Schrödinger equation of a classically driven system emerges directly from the fully quantized problem, without replacing the field by a classical trajectory or assuming a mean-field decoupling. This establishes a one-to-one correspondence between quasienergies and photon-sector-shifted quantum energies, while providing a direct quantum interpretation of the Floquet mean energy and Anandan phase. Because gauge invariance is exactly preserved at the level of the quantum Hamiltonian, the quantum-to-classical correspondence remains valid beyond perturbative and small-coupling regimes. We further show that light--matter entanglement is encoded in matter--harmonic correlations of the corresponding Floquet mode, thus identifying a semiclassical limit in which Floquet physics emerges without requiring the quantum field itself to approach a coherent classical state.

</details>

### [83] [Combining astrometry with pulsar timing: the first joint analysis of very low frequency gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21606)
*Lorenzo Filipello,William Beordo,Volodymyr Akhmetov,Mariateresa Crosta,Mario Gilberto Lattanzi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该研究首次联合真实天体测量自行（astrometric proper motions）与双脉冲星计时（binary pulsar timing）数据，在 pHz 至亚 nHz 频段搜寻超大质量双黑洞（SMBHBs）发出的连续引力波（CWs），未获显著信号，但在该频段给出了迄今最严格的应变（strain）上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pHz 至亚 nHz 引力波频段长期缺乏探测，却对刻画 SMBHB 早期旋进（early inspiral）阶段和探测早期宇宙物理至关重要。天体测量与脉冲星计时提供正交且互补的长期观测量，联合使用有望突破单一观测手段的局限。

Method: 采用贝叶斯模型选择（Bayesian model selection）与上限估计框架，联合 Gaia CRF3 星表中约 1.5×10^6 个类星体的视自行位移与 11 颗高精度双脉冲星的视线方向轨道周期导数（$\dot{P}_b$）。为抑制虚假探测，对系统误差重度建模：以蒙特卡洛模拟传播脉冲星的银河势（Galactic potential）不确定性，并对类星体做最高至八极阶（l=3）的矢量球谐（Vector Spherical Harmonics, VSH）分解。

Result: 联合分析未发现统计显著的 CWs 证据（$\ln B_{joint} = -0.42 \pm 0.03$）。在无探测情况下，于参考频率 $f_{ref} = 4 \times 10^{-10}$ Hz 处给出 95% 置信上限 $h_0 \le 6.4\times10^{-11}$，为 pHz 频段目前最严格约束。联合数据集实现全天覆盖，并较单数据集上限改善 20%–30%。

Conclusion: 联合正交观测量成功打破了单一搜寻固有的空间简并（spatial degeneracies）。注入-恢复（injection-recovery）预测表明，借助 Gaia DR4 更长的时基与更小的不确定度，该联合框架有望突破亚 nHz 连续引力波 $h_0 < 10^{-11}$ 的上限壁垒。

Abstract: The pHz to sub-nHz GW regime remains largely unexplored but is crucial for mapping the early inspiral stage of SMBHBs and probing early-Universe physics. Astrometry and pulsar timing offer orthogonal and deeply complementary secular observables to investigate this frequency band. We aim to present the first joint data analysis combining real astrometric proper motions with binary pulsar timing, to search for and constrain continuous gravitational waves (CWs) sourced by SMBHBs in the ultra-low-frequency regime ($10^{-12} \le f_{GW} \le10^{-9} Hz$). We employ a Bayesian model selection and upper limit estimation framework to combine apparent proper motion displacements of $\sim 1.5 \times 10^6$ quasars from the Gaia CRF3 catalog with the line-of-sight orbital period derivatives ($\dot{P}_b$) of 11 high-precision binary pulsars. To prevent spurious detections, we heavily model instrumental and astrophysical systematics: we propagate the Galactic potential uncertainty for pulsars via Monte Carlo simulations and perform a Vector Spherical Harmonics (VSH) decomposition up to the octupole order (l=3) for quasars. We find no statistically significant evidence for a CWs signal in the joint analysis ($\ln B_{joint} = -0.42 \pm 0.03$). In the absence of detection, we set the tightest constraints to date on CW strain in the pHz band, yielding a 95% upper limit of $h_0\le6.4x10^{-11}$ at a reference frequency of $f_{ref} = 4 \times 10^{-10}$ Hz. The combined dataset achieves full sky coverage and improves single-dataset upper limits by 20%-30%. Combining orthogonal observables successfully breaks spatial degeneracies intrinsic to isolated searches. Furthermore, forecasts from inj.-rec. indicate that with the extended temporal baseline and reduced uncertainties of the upcoming Gaia DR4, this joint framework is poised to break the $h_0 < 10^{-11}$ upper limit barrier for sub-nHz CWs.

</details>

### [84] [Calculating Axion-Matter Couplings in String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20911)

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次在弦理论（string theory）中显式计算了轴子（axion）与物质耦合的主导微扰贡献，针对杂化弦（heterotic string）在 Calabi-Yau 紧致化且向量丛为线丛之和的情形：将耦合表示为需要 Ricci-平坦度规、Hermitian Yang-Mills 丛度规与物质场调和形式的交叠积分，并用神经网络求解相应耦合微分方程。结果给出四维耦合的特征层级：模型依赖轴子与费米子耦合系数为 Kähler 模的 O(1) 函数，模型无关轴子耦合被 α_YM 压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子-物质耦合虽已有微扰计算，但此前未在弦理论中显式给出主导贡献，缺乏从紫外完备理论出发的定量刻画。该工作旨在填补这一空白，明确不同轴子（模型依赖与模型无关）在四维有效理论中耦合强度的来源与层级。

Method: 在杂化弦理论的 Calabi-Yau 紧致化中，取向量丛为线丛（line bundles）之和，计算轴子-物质耦合。耦合被表示为交叠积分（overlap integrals），涉及 Ricci-平坦度规、Hermitian Yang-Mills 丛度规与物质场调和形式（harmonic forms）；通过神经网络（neural networks）求解相应的耦合微分方程组得到这些量。

Result: 建立了四维耦合的特征层级：模型依赖轴子（model-dependent axions）与费米子的耦合形如 L ⊃ c_ψ (∂_μ a / f_a) ψ^† σ̄^μ ψ，其中 c_ψ 为 Kähler 模（Kähler moduli）的 O(1) 函数；而模型无关轴子（model-independent axion）的相应耦合在紫外匹配标度（ultraviolet matching scale）被 α_YM 压低。因此，模型依赖轴子与费米子的耦合类似 DFSZ 型四维轴子，模型无关轴子的耦合则如 KSVZ 型构造一样受到圈图压低。

Conclusion: 本文方法可直接应用于具有现实谱的大类杂化弦线丛模型，并有望推广至 type II 构造。研究确立了弦理论中轴子-物质耦合的显式微扰计算路径，并揭示了两类轴子耦合强度的层级差异。

Abstract: In this work we calculate, for the first time, the dominant perturbative contributions to axion--matter couplings explicitly in string theory. Concretely, we compute these couplings in Calabi--Yau compactifications of heterotic string theory where the vector bundle is a sum of line bundles. They are expressed as overlap integrals requiring the Ricci-flat metric, the Hermitian Yang-Mills bundle metric, and the matter field harmonic forms. We compute these by solving the corresponding coupled differential equations with neural networks. Our results establish a characteristic hierarchy of four-dimensional couplings: axions couple to fermions as $\mathcal{L} \supset c_ψ\,\frac{\partial_μa}{f_a}\, ψ^\dagger \barσ^μψ$ with $c_ψ$ an $\mathcal{O}(1)$ function of Kähler moduli for the model-dependent axions, while the corresponding coupling for the model-independent axion is suppressed by $α_{\rm YM}$ at the ultraviolet matching scale. The model-dependent axions thus couple to fermions similarly as DFSZ-like four-dimensional axions, whereas the model-independent axion's couplings are loop-suppressed as in KSVZ-like constructions. The methods developed here apply directly to the large class of heterotic line bundle models with realistic spectra, and can be adapted to type II constructions.

</details>

Abstract: We present evidence that ten-dimensional type IIB string theory orbifolded by the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ duality symmetry at the strong-coupling fixed point $τ=\exp(2πi/3)$ defines a consistent theory. Motivated by its F-theory dual description and its duality with M-theory in nine dimensions and type IIA string theory in eight dimensions, we argue that the resulting theory is a ten-dimensional chiral non-supersymmetric theory with a discrete gauge symmetry whose bosonic quotient is the automorphism group of the Narain lattice $Γ^{2,2}(A_2)$ ($S_3 \times S_3 \times \mathbb{Z}_2$). We further identify the ten-dimensional tachyon fields with the corresponding Narain moduli. Gauge anomalies cancel nontrivially through a topological Green-Schwarz mechanism involving a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-valued two-form gauge symmetry: the ten-dimensional lift of the familiar zero-form orbifold quantum symmetry in the eight-dimensional type IIA description.

### [86] [Discrete Gauging of 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ $E_n$ SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20917)
*Guillermo Arias-Tamargo,Mario De Marco,Craig Lawrie,Shani Nadir Meynet,Alessandro Mininno*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 5d rank-one $E_n$ 超共形场论（SCFT）中离散对称性的规范化（gauging），这些对称性固定库仑支（Coulomb branch）坐标但在希格斯支（Higgs branch）手征环上非平凡作用。借助广义环面多边形（generalized toric polygons）与 $(p,q)$-膜网（$(p,q)$-brane webs），识别可延拓至完整 SCFT 的模空间对称性，并分析规范化理论的模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5d rank-one $E_n$ SCFT 的模空间存在固定库仑支坐标、却在希格斯支手征环上非平凡作用的离散对称性，其规范化及相应模空间结构尚未被系统刻画。论文旨在识别那些能延拓为完整 SCFT 对称性的模空间对称性，并给出规范化后理论的模空间描述。

Method: 采用广义环面多边形与 $(p,q)$-膜网识别模空间对称性；在许多情形下这些对称性非反常，据此分析规范化理论模空间。将所得希格斯支商实现为编织磁量子图（wreathed magnetic quivers）的库仑支并计算希尔伯特级数（Hilbert series）；从矩映射（moment maps）提取的味代数（flavor algebras）与 $\mathfrak{e}_n$ 的不动点子代数比较。并将离散作用提升到 Seiberg--Witten 几何。

Result: 从矩映射提取的味代数与 $\mathfrak{e}_n$ 的不动点子代数一致；在 Seiberg--Witten 几何中恢复了质量形变（mass deformations）的预期限制。对于交换相邻规范节点的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-编织（$\mathbb{Z}_2$-wreathings），发现单极子公式（monopole formula）需要依赖磁通的符号，并提出修正方案。

Conclusion: 论文提出更高秩（higher-rank）的推广猜想，并在 rank-two $E_6/\mathbb{Z}_2$ 希格斯支上验证了该猜想。研究给出了离散对称性规范化后模空间的一致描述，并指出单极子公式在特定编织情形下需作修正。

Abstract: We study the gauging of discrete symmetries of the 5d rank-one $E_n$ SCFTs that fix the Coulomb branch coordinate but act nontrivially on the Higgs branch chiral ring. Using generalized toric polygons and $(p,q)$-brane webs, we identify symmetries of the moduli space that extend to symmetries of the full SCFT. In many cases they are non-anomalous, and we analyze the moduli space of the gauged theories. We realize the resulting Higgs branch quotients as Coulomb branches of wreathed magnetic quivers and compute their Hilbert series; the flavor algebras extracted from moment maps agree with the fixed-point subalgebras of $\mathfrak{e}_n$. We also lift the discrete actions to the Seiberg--Witten geometry and recover the expected restriction on the mass deformations. For $\mathbb{Z}_2$-wreathings that exchange adjacent gauge nodes, we find that the monopole formula requires a flux-dependent sign and propose a corrected prescription. Finally, we conjecture a higher-rank extension and verify it for the rank-two $E_6/\mathbb{Z}_2$ Higgs branch.

</details>


### [87] [Massive type 0A string theory and M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20919)
*Niccolò Cribiori,Andriana Makridou,Salvatore Raucci*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出 Type 0A 弦论两个有质量形变（massive deformations）的 M 理论起源，通过将 Hull 关于有质量 Type IIA 的实现推广至近期由 M 理论导出的无质量 Type 0A 方案，引入称为"量子纤维化（quantum fibration）"的 M 理论构造，其中两个独立整单值性（integral monodromies）给出 Romans 质量，并与该纤维化的两个陈数（Chern numbers）T 对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作已从 M 理论实现无质量 Type 0A，且 Hull 曾给出有质量 Type IIA 的 M 理论实现，但 Type 0A 的两个有质量形变（由其两个 Romans 质量刻画）尚缺乏对应的 M 理论起源。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造一种称为量子纤维化（quantum fibration）的 M 理论设置，其两个独立整单值性（integral monodromies）产生 Romans 质量，并通过 T 对偶（T-duality）联系到该纤维化的两个陈数（Chern numbers）。关键技术要素是以带标记椭圆曲线（marked elliptic curve）表述 Type 0B 对偶群（duality group）。

Result: 两个独立整单值性给出两个 Romans 质量，与量子纤维化的两个陈数 T 对偶，从而为 Type 0A 的两个有质量形变提供 M 理论起源；Type 0B 对偶群得以用带标记椭圆曲线表述。

Conclusion: 该描述为 Type 0A 有质量形变的 M 理论起源提供了统一框架，并进一步讨论了其含义，以及关于膜的 M 理论起源与 Chern–Simons 项的相关疑题（puzzles）。

Abstract: We propose an M-theory origin for the two massive deformations of type 0A string theory by extending Hull's realization of massive type IIA in the context of a recent proposal for massless type 0A from M-theory. We introduce an M-theory setup that we call quantum fibration, in which two independent integral monodromies yield the Romans masses, T-dual to the two Chern numbers of the quantum fibration. A key ingredient is a formulation of the type 0B duality group in terms of a marked elliptic curve. We discuss further implications of this description and some puzzles concerning the M-theory origin of branes and Chern--Simons terms.

</details>

*Maddalena Ferragatta,Balt C. van Rees*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出 Carmi 与 Caron-Huot 共形色散关系（conformal dispersion relation）的一个简洁推导，该关系可由双不连续（double discontinuity）重构共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）四点函数。文中揭示了一个隐藏的共形对称性（hidden conformal symmetry），用以解释“魔法变量（magic variable）” x 作为由四个物理交比（cross-ratio）构成的简单交比而出现，并引入调节子（regulator）证明一次减除（one subtraction）即可保证所有积分收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在为已有的共形色散关系提供更简单、更透明的推导，并阐明其中“魔法变量”x 的来源。其动机在于理解双不连续重构四点函数这一机制背后的结构，而非仅停留在技术性计算层面。

Method: 方法上，作者通过对双不连续操作引入一个调节子（regulator），并在此框架下进行积分收敛性分析。同时，作者构造并展示了一个隐藏的共形对称性，说明“魔法变量”x 可表示为四个物理交比构成的简单交比。

Result: 结果表明，“魔法变量”x 确实可写成四个物理交比的简单组合，其出现由隐藏共形对称性所解释。此外，引入调节子后可以证明，对所有积分而言，一次减除（one subtraction）即足以保证收敛。

Conclusion: 结论是，Carmi–Caron-Huot 共形色散关系可被简洁地重新推导，其核心的“魔法变量”具有清晰的共形对称性解释，且双不连续积分的收敛性可由单次减除保证。

Abstract: We provide a simple derivation of the conformal dispersion relation of Carmi and Caron-Huot which reconstructs a CFT four-point function from its double discontinuity. We exhibit a hidden conformal symmetry which explains the appearance of the "magic variable" x as a simple cross-ratio, built from four physical cross-ratios. We also introduce a regulator in the double discontinuity operation and then prove that one subtraction suffices for all integrals to converge.

</details>


### [89] [Cosmological Correlators from Resurgence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20925)
*Yuanzhao Li,Zhong-Zhi Xianyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在宇宙学（cosmology）背景下，低能有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）结合幺正性（unitarity）与 Bunch-Davies 边界条件，可重构含重粒子交换的暴胀关联函数（inflationary correlators），从而恢复被积掉的重自由度信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 积掉重粒子通常被视为不可逆过程，会抹除截断尺度以上的物理信息。作者受 Schwinger 对产生（Schwinger pair production）可由重求和的 Euler-Heisenberg 有效作用量恢复这一经典例子启发，探讨在宇宙学中是否也能从低能 EFT 反推重粒子的传播自由度。

Method: 针对离散重粒子谱（discrete heavy spectrum）与任意树图拓扑（tree topologies），将发散的 EFT 级数通过三种方式重求和：边界微分算符（boundary differential operators）、谱表示（spectral representation）与 Borel 重求和（Borel resummation），并借助解析延拓（analytic continuation）恢复信号。

Result: 在给定幺正性与 Bunch-Davies 边界条件下，可从低能有效理论重构含重粒子交换的暴胀关联函数，并通过解析延拓恢复指数压低的宇宙对撞机信号（cosmological collider signals）。

Conclusion: 该构造在局域 EFT 背景与非解析的重粒子产生之间建立了 resurgence 关系（resurgent relation），亦可视为色散 bootstrap（dispersive bootstrap）的一类逆问题（inverse problem）。

Abstract: Integrating out heavy particles is generally viewed as an irreversible procedure that erases some physical information above the cutoff scale of the resulting effective theory. However, this does not preclude the possibility of recovering the heavy propagating degrees from the low-energy effective theory with appropriate boundary data supplied. A classic example is the recovery of Schwinger pair production from the resummed Euler-Heisenberg effective action. In this work, we perform a similar exercise in a cosmological setting. We show that it is possible to reconstruct the inflationary correlators with massive exchanges, for a discrete heavy spectrum and arbitrary tree topologies, from the low-energy effective theory together with unitarity and Bunch-Davies boundary conditions. The divergent EFT series is resummed in three ways: the boundary differential operators, spectral representation, and Borel resummation, and the exponentially suppressed cosmological collider signals are recovered via analytic continuation. Our construction provides a resurgent relation between the local EFT background and the nonanalytic heavy-particle production, and may also be viewed as an inverse problem for the dispersive bootstrap.

</details>


### [90] [Vector Superradiance without Separability: Instability Rates from the Worldline Effective Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20960)
*Keegan Cove,Jingping Li,Riccardo Penco*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文用点粒子有效场论（point-particle effective field theory）研究超轻质量矢量（ultralight massive vector）在缓转黑洞周围的超辐射不稳定性（superradiant instability），证明此前报道的与 Proca 方程结果之间的 O(1) 差异并不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期有效场论处理在 j=1、ℓ=2 模式上与固定黑洞背景下求解 Proca 方程所得速率存在表观 O(1) 差异，本文旨在澄清这一文献中的张力。

Method: 在点粒子有效场论框架下，将引力子（graviton）精确积掉后再按 α 展开；正确识别退化 {ℓ=0, ℓ=2} 子空间中的领头阶态；除远区（far zone）外仅引入一个保守的世界线接触项（worldline contact term），即宇称偶（parity-even）j=1 Proca 扇区中视界的静态单极响应，并通过匹配近区（near zone）加以确定。

Result: 有效场论精确重现了全部 j=1 Proca 不稳定性速率，且无需援引 FKKS ansatz；同一接触项亦使束缚态的精细结构分裂与已发表的 Proca 谱完全吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作既提供了矢量超辐射的简洁模型、解决了文献中的表观矛盾，也用于说明点粒子有效场论中耗散效应（dissipative effects）可能涉及的技术微妙之处；由于不依赖可分离性（separability），该方法原则上可推广至缺乏隐藏对称性的旋转背景与天体。

Abstract: We use a point-particle effective field theory to study the superradiant instability rates of an ultralight massive vector around a slowly rotating black hole. Earlier effective-theory treatments reported an apparent $O(1)$ discrepancy with the rates obtained by solving the Proca equation on a fixed black-hole background, e.g. for the mode with $j=1$ and $\ell=2$. We show that there is in fact no discrepancy: once the leading-order states in the degenerate $\{\ell=0,\ell=2\}$ subspace are correctly identified, the effective theory (implemented here with the graviton integrated out exactly, and expanded in $α$ only afterwards) reproduces \emph{all} of the $j=1$ Proca instability rates exactly, and it does so \emph{without} ever invoking the FKKS ansatz on which the full-theory calculation rests. Since it never relies on separability, the same approach applies, at least in principle, to rotating backgrounds and bodies that lack the hidden symmetries underlying that ansatz. The single ingredient beyond the far zone is a conservative worldline contact term, the static monopole response of the horizon in the parity-even $j=1$ Proca sector, which we fix by matching to the near zone. The same term shifts the fine-structure splitting of the bound states into exact agreement with the published Proca spectrum. In addition to providing a conceptually straightforward model of vector superradiance and resolving an apparent tension in the literature, we present this work to illustrate potential technical subtleties involving dissipative effects in point particle effective theories.

</details>


### [91] [Morse Bridge between Planar Kepler and Hyperbolic Landau Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01778)
*Mikhail S. Plyushchay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作以共同的 Morse 哈密顿量（Morse Hamiltonian）为桥梁，将平面 Kepler–Coulomb 系统与双曲 Landau 系统联系起来，在约化扇区（reduced sectors）上建立了经典轨道映射、Poincaré Kähler 度量等距以及量子算符束的精确交织恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示平直电场动力学（flat electric dynamics）与弯曲磁场动力学（curved magnetic dynamics）之间可能存在的深层结构联系，即平面 Kepler–Coulomb 问题与双曲 Landau 问题是否共享同一底层可积系统。

Method: 采用带耦合常数蜕变（coupling-constant metamorphosis）的径向 Liouville 变换，将分离的 Kepler 问题映射到 Morse 系统；同时用固定等时线动量约化（fixed-horocyclic-momentum reduction）将双曲 Landau 问题映射到同一系统。并构造经典轨道映射与由约化 Kepler 辛形式（symplectic form）及 Binet 选取的复结构决定的 Poincaré Kähler 度量。

Result: 在共同归一化下，Coulomb 耦合等于磁场与守恒等时线动量的带符号乘积，量子 Landau 约化出现普适半密度位移 1/4。轨道映射把 Landau 高度对应为缩放的逆 Kepler 半径，将 Kepler 椭圆、抛物线与双曲线分别映为磁圆、等时线与超圆，并将 Hamilton 速矢端迹（hodograph）仿射映为 Landau 载圆。该度量与双曲 Landau 问题的 Poincaré 构型空间度量等距。

Conclusion: 复合量子 Liouville 映射可消去 Morse 波函数，得到径向 Kepler 与等时线 Landau 算符束之间的精确加权交织恒等式；在半整数磁场处，固定 Kepler 壳层的角动量分级重组为有限约化 Landau 塔及其阈值端点。这些精确对应在所述约化扇区成立，尽管完整母系统之间并不幺正等价。

Abstract: A common Morse Hamiltonian bridges the planar Kepler--Coulomb and hyperbolic Landau systems, linking flat electric dynamics to curved magnetic dynamics. A radial Liouville transformation with coupling-constant metamorphosis maps the separated Kepler problem to the Morse system, while fixed-horocyclic-momentum reduction maps the hyperbolic Landau problem to the same system. In a common normalization, the Coulomb coupling is the signed product of the magnetic field and conserved horocyclic momentum, while the quantum Landau reduction has the universal half-density shift $1/4$. Beyond this shared reduction, we construct a direct classical orbit map on the regular nonradial sectors for all signs of the Kepler energy. It maps the Landau height to a scaled inverse Kepler radius, Kepler ellipses, parabolas and hyperbolas to magnetic circles, horocycles and hypercycles, and Hamilton's hodograph affinely to the Landau carrier circle. The reduced Kepler symplectic form and the Binet-selected complex structure determine a Poincaré Kähler metric. Its nondegenerate Binet orbits have constant geodesic curvature of magnitude equal to the inverse eccentricity, while zero coupling gives geodesics. The orbit map identifies this metric isometrically with the Poincaré configuration-space metric of the hyperbolic Landau problem. Composing the quantum Liouville maps eliminates the Morse wavefunction and yields an exact weighted intertwining identity between the radial Kepler and horocyclic Landau operator pencils. At distinguished half-integer magnetic fields, the angular-momentum grading of a fixed Kepler shell is reorganized into the finite reduced Landau tower and its threshold endpoint. These exact correspondences hold in the stated reduced sectors despite the unitary inequivalence of the complete parent systems.

</details>


### [92] [A boundary-addition identity for Weil-Petersson volumes and their supersymmetric generalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21030)
*Clifford V. Johnson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在 KdV 可积层级（KdV hierarchy）框架下统一处理 Weil-Petersson 体积及其超对称推广，导出一个精确的“增边界恒等式”，将 n+1 边界体积与 n 边界的 KdV 数据直接联系，并给出几何与递归关系的新解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weil-Petersson 体积及其超对称推广在文献中存在若干零散的已知结果与递归关系，缺乏统一的生成机制。作者希望借助可积系统（integrable system）框架，将这些量视为更大族量的特例，从而系统化其结构与相互关系。

Method: 利用 KdV 层级（KdV hierarchy）控制体积族；推导精确的增边界恒等式（boundary-addition identity），将 \(V_{g,n+1}(\{b_1,\dots,b_n,b\})\) 与 \(V_{g,n}\) 对应的 KdV 数据相联系；对普通 Weil-Petersson 体积在可去锥值 \(b=2\pi i\) 附近展开，并以附加 KdV 数据构造更高层级，在交截理论（intersection theory）中解释为 \(\kappa\)-装饰体积之塔；方法覆盖 \(N=1,2\) 及小 \(N=4\) 超对称情形。

Result: 增边界恒等式在普通情形下于 \(b=2\pi i\) 展开复现 Do 与 Norbury 的三个已知结果，并将其组织为完整层级的前几层；更高层由附加 KdV 数据给出，对应一列 \(\kappa\)-装饰体积。该恒等式亦阐明了文献中若干递归关系并导出新递归；对 \(N=1,2\) 与小 \(N=4\) 情形给出各自显著特征与结果，并据此揭示 \(N=2\) 及小 \(N=4\) Weil-Petersson 体积的交截理论描述。

Conclusion: 增边界恒等式为 Weil-Petersson 体积及其超对称推广提供了统一且可递归生成的框架，既重构已知结果，又在交截理论上赋予其 \(\kappa\)-装饰的体积解释，并推广至 \(N=1,2\) 与小 \(N=4\) 超对称情形。

Abstract: We consider $V_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$, the Weil-Petersson volumes of the moduli space of Riemann surfaces of genus $g$ with $n$ geodesic boundaries of lengths $b_i$, $(i=1,...,n)$, and various ${ N}{\geq}1$ supersymmetric generalizations of them, using a framework within which they are specializations of a larger family of quantities naturally governed by the integrable KdV hierarchy. An exact boundary-addition identity is derived that directly relates $V_{g,n+1}(\{b_1,b_2,...,b_n,b\})$, for any $b$, to the corresponding $n$-boundary KdV data underlying $V_{g,n}(\{b_1,b_2,...,b_n\})$. For ordinary Weil-Petersson volumes, expansion about the special removable-cone value $b=2πi$ reproduces three known results of Do and Norbury, organizing them as the first levels of a complete hierarchy. The higher levels are naturally built from additional KdV data, which we interpret geometrically in intersection theory as a tower of $κ$-decorated volumes. Several other recursive relations in the literature are illuminated by the identity, and various new ones are derived. The cases with ${ N}=1,2$ and (small) ${N}=4$ supersymmetry are also covered by the identity, and we exhibit some of the striking special features and results arising in each case. Among many applications presented, we use the identity to uncover the intersection theory descriptions of ${N}=2$ and small ${ N}=4$ Weil-Petersson volumes.

</details>


### [93] [On asymptotically and anomaly-free SU(N) chiral gauge theories for arbitrarily large N](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21129)
*Kort Beck,Patrick Draper*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文计算了Eichten–Kang–Koh构造的一类在大N极限下渐近自由、无反常的SU(N)手征规范理论（chiral gauge theory）的整体对称群结构，并系统列出其忠实对称性（faithful symmetry）的't Hooft反常，包括仅在携带分数通量（fractional flux）背景下可见的反常。作者用下降法（descent procedure）与四环面（four-torus）两种方法计算反常，并论证分数通量反常匹配可作为零形式（zero-form）反常匹配之外的额外约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eichten、Kang与Koh曾构造出大量具有大N极限的渐近自由、无反常SU(N)手征规范理论目录，但其整体对称群结构与't Hooft反常尚未被完整刻画。多数此类理论含基本荷物质，不具备真正的一形式电中心对称（one-form electric center symmetry），然而忠实商（faithful quotient）仍可产生分数通量并贡献反常，这一效应值得系统研究。理解这些反常有助于约束理论的IR行为。

Method: 首先确定目录中各理论忠实对称群的整体结构，并表格化其't Hooft反常，涵盖仅在分数通量背景下可见的部分。反常计算采用两种独立方法：一是下降程序（descent procedure），二是将理论置于四环面（four-torus）上，后者可支持背景规范场的扭曲通量（twisted fluxes）。

Result: 绝大多数理论含基本荷物质，不支持真正的一形式电中心对称；但分数通量仍由忠实商产生，其贡献方式等价于一形式对称的二形式背景（two-form background）。基于大N推理，作者为两个模型提出新的IR行为提案，该方案符合零形式't Hooft反常匹配条件，并额外通过涉及分数通量的匹配检验。

Conclusion: 分数通量相关反常的匹配构成了超出普通零形式反常匹配的一项额外约束，可用于筛选和约束候选IR行为。该工作展示了分数通量't Hooft反常匹配作为检验IR情景有效性的独立判据的价值。

Abstract: A large catalog of asymptotically free and anomaly-free SU(N) chiral gauge theories that admit a large-N limit was constructed by Eichten, Kang, and Koh. Here we compute the global structure of their symmetry groups and tabulate the 't Hooft anomalies of the faithful symmetries, including those that are visible only in backgrounds carrying fractional flux. Most theories in the catalog contain fundamentally charged matter and support no genuine one-form electric center symmetry. However, fractional fluxes still arise from the faithful quotient and contribute to the anomaly in the same way as a two-form background for a one-form symmetry. The anomalies are computed using two methods, first with the descent procedure, and second by placing the theory on a four-torus, which supports twisted fluxes for background gauge fields. We discuss a selection of candidate IR behaviors and present a general discussion of how matching the anomalies associated with fractional flux constrains these theories beyond the imposition of ordinary zero-form anomaly matching. Based on large-N reasoning, we formulate a new proposal for the IR behavior of a pair of models, consistent with the zero-form 't Hooft anomaly matching conditions. The matching condition involving fractional flux amounts to one additional test, which the scenario is shown to pass.

</details>


### [94] [The Chern-Simons Function and the Quantum Hall Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21182)
*Edward Witten*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文定义三维流形上规范联络的陈-西蒙斯不变量（Chern-Simons invariant），并阐释其特殊性质，重点说明其在理解量子霍尔效应（quantum Hall effect）及分数量子霍尔效应（fractional quantum Hall effect）中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陈-西蒙斯不变量具有若干独特性质，且与凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）中的重要现象密切相关，值得系统阐述。

Method: 在三维流形（three-manifold）上定义规范联络（gauge connection）的陈-西蒙斯不变量，并对其特殊之处进行解释性说明。

Result: 阐明了陈-西蒙斯不变量在理解量子霍尔效应及其推广形式——分数量子霍尔效应中所扮演的角色。

Conclusion: 陈-西蒙斯不变量是连接数学与凝聚态物理的重要工具，可用于刻画量子霍尔效应与分数量子霍尔效应。本文基于2013年詹姆斯·西蒙斯（James Simons）75岁生日会议上的讲座，将发表于《美国数学会公报》（Bulletin of the American Math Society）。

Abstract: In this article, I will define the Chern-Simons invariant of a gauge connection over a three-manifold and explain some of the things that are special about it, in particular its role in understanding the quantum Hall effect, which is an important effect in condensed matter physics, and its generalization, the fractional quantum Hall effect. (The article is based on a lecture at a conference celebrating the 75th birthday of James Simons, held at the CUNY Graduate Center in 2013. It will appear in the Bulletin of the American Math Society.

</details>


### [95] [Entanglement entropy in holographic CFTs with generic boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21370)
*Fabio Ori,Peng-Xiang Hao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文指出，对具有一般时空号差（spacetime signature）边界的全息共形场论（holographic conformal field theory），其纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）无法完全由实体内空（real bulk spacetime）的标准全息对偶（holographic duality）描述，需将双时空（dual spacetime）延拓至复坐标（complex coordinates）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全息对偶假设实体（real bulk）时空，但此类框架在描述具有一般时空号差边界的全息共形场论的纠缠熵时存在不足。作者旨在说明，为与边界二维共形场论（conformal field theory）预测精确匹配，对偶时空需要扩展至复坐标。

Method: 在静态与含时（time-dependent）两种设定下，将双时空延拓至复坐标；由此得到的体（bulk）对象，如 Ryu-Takayanagi 曲面（Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces）与膜（branes），一般为复对象，但锚定在实渐近边界（real asymptotic boundary）上。另以二维自由高斯费米子（free Gaussian fermions）系统进行第三次数值检验。

Result: 复坐标延拓能够与二维边界共形场论的预测精确匹配；复的 Ryu-Takayanagi 曲面与膜仍锚定于实渐近边界，从而保持场论量的物理意义。二维自由高斯费米子系统在不同纠缠相（entanglement phases）下表现出相同的渐近行为（asymptotic regimes），构成对全息预测的第三次数值检验。

Conclusion: 具有一般时空号差边界的全息共形场论纠缠熵的描述，需要将双时空扩展至复坐标；体对象虽为复，但因锚定于实渐近边界而保留物理解释。自由高斯费米子模型提供了支持该全息预测的进一步数值证据。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy in holographic conformal field theories with boundaries of generic spacetime signature cannot be described entirely within the standard holographic duality to a real bulk spacetime. We show, both in static and time-dependent setups, that an exact match with the conformal field theory predictions in two boundary dimensions entails an extension of the dual spacetime to complex coordinates. As a consequence, relevant bulk objects such as Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces and branes are in general complex, yet anchored on a real asymptotic boundary preserving the physical meaning of field-theoretical quantities. We also provide a third numerical check of the holographic prediction with a two-dimensional system of free Gaussian fermions exhibiting the same asymptotic regimes across different entanglement phases.

</details>


### [96] [A Differential Form Description of Partial Entanglement Entropy: Testing a Killing Vector Construction in Covariant Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21398)
*Chuanjia Zhu,Dong-Hui Du,Wen-Cong Gan,Fu-Wen Shu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入偏纠缠熵流（PEE thread flow）的微分形式（differential form）描述，并以真空 AdS₃/CFT₂ 中的区间为例验证其通量可重现已知纠缠轮廓。随后考察该 PEE 形式能否（至多相差恰当形式改进）与协变相空间（covariant phase space, CPS）形式构造的流相关联，结果表明直接使用 Killing 矢量的 CPS 构造不足以重现中心 PEE 流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特线程（bit thread）表述为全息纠缠熵提供了几何描述，而偏纠缠熵（partial entanglement entropy, PEE）及其 PEE 线程将这一结构细化到边界单点层面。由于 PEE 线程流可叠加重构常规比特线程流，作者希望为 PEE 线程流建立微分形式描述，并探究其与协变相空间形式所得流的可能联系。

Method: 提出 PEE 线程流的微分形式表述；在真空 AdS₃/CFT₂ 中对区间计算该形式的通量并与已知纠缠轮廓比对。进一步在 Rindler-AdS₃ 中取精确 Killing 矢量，将其代入 Iyer-Wald 表面荷形式并保留对 Rindler 参数 a 的完整依赖，通过对 a 积分定义有限候选流，再变换到 Poincaré-AdS₃ 与已知 PEE 流比较。

Result: 所提出微分形式的通量重现了已知纠缠轮廓，构成对该描述的一致性检验。对源自中心边界点 r₀=0 的反射对称 PEE 流，变换到 Poincaré-AdS₃ 后，没有任何 Killing 参数选择能同时重现已知 PEE 流的两个分量。

Conclusion: 本文考虑的直接基于 Killing 矢量的 CPS 构造不足以重现中心 PEE 流。可能的推广方向包括：加入恰当形式改进、采用不同的 CPS 流，或选用更一般的生成元。

Abstract: The bit thread formulation provides a geometric description of holographic entanglement entropy, while partial entanglement entropy (PEE) and PEE threads resolve this structure with respect to individual boundary points. Motivated by the fact that PEE thread flows can be superposed to reconstruct conventional bit thread flows, we introduce a differential form description of PEE thread flow. For an interval in the vacuum AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ setup, we show that the flux of the resulting form reproduces the known entanglement contour, providing a consistency check of the proposed description. We then investigate whether the PEE form can be related, possibly up to an exact form improvement, to a current constructed from the covariant phase space (CPS) formalism. As a first test, we consider exact Killing vectors in Rindler-AdS$_3$. The Rindler parameter $a$ parametrizes a one-parameter family of backgrounds. At each value of $a$, the Killing vector of the corresponding background is substituted into the Iyer-Wald surface charge form, and the full $a$-dependence of the resulting form is retained. We define a finite candidate current by integrating this surface charge form over $a$. For the reflection-symmetric PEE flow sourced at the central boundary point $r_0=0$, we find that no choice of the Killing parameters reproduces both components of the known PEE flow after transforming to Poincaré-AdS$_3$. This result shows that the direct Killing vector CPS construction considered here is not sufficient to reproduce the central PEE flow. Possible extensions include an exact form improvement, a different CPS current, or a more general choice of generator.

</details>


### [97] [Complex scalar field thick branes: stability of linear perturbation and evolution of scalar Kaluza-Klein modes coupled with gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21421)
*Wang-Long Dong,Heng Guo,Qun Wei,Yong-Tao Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究由复标量场生成的 Minkowski、de Sitter（dS）与 anti-de Sitter（AdS）厚膜的稳定性与局域化性质。标量、矢量扇区无不稳定迹象，张量模不含快子；引力子零模在 Minkowski 与 dS 膜上局域，在 AdS 膜上不可归一。测试标量场零模在三种膜上均可局域，AdS 膜因边界发散势形成离散质量谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察含温度参数的自相互作用势下复标量场厚膜的稳定性与各类模的局域化行为。摘要通过临界温度附近子膜形成这一现象，探讨不同背景几何（Minkowski、dS、AdS）对局域化与共振/束缚态的影响。

Method: 采用复标量场 Φ 生成厚膜，其自相互作用势含温度参数 a；对线性标量、矢量与张量扰动进行分析，并对张量扰动方程进行因式分解。进一步引入带动能-曲率耦合函数 F(R) 的实测试标量场 Ψ，研究标量 Kaluza–Klein（KK）模谱与共振。

Result: 当 a 趋近临界值时，Φ 的虚部呈双扭结，能量密度分裂为双峰，形成两个子膜。标量与矢量扇区无不稳定，张量扰动因式分解排除快子模。引力子零模在 Minkowski 与 dS 膜上局域，在 AdS 膜上不可归一。标量零模在 Minkowski 与 dS 膜上总可局域，具连续无隙谱；临界 a 附近、适当 β 下出现大质量标量共振，β 增大则共振数增多、低阶共振寿命延长。AdS 膜上标量零模亦局域，但边界发散有效势使大质量 KK 模成为束缚态，谱为离散。

Conclusion: 三类厚膜在标量与矢量扇区保持稳定，张量模无快子不稳定性。局域化行为依赖背景几何：Minkowski 与 dS 膜支持局域的引力子与标量零模及连续 KK 谱，AdS 膜则否定引力子零模归一化而使标量 KK 模离散化为束缚态。共振性质受参数 a 与 β 调控。

Abstract: We investigate the stability and localization properties of Minkowski, de Sitter (dS), and anti-de Sitter (AdS) thick branes generated by a complex scalar field $Φ$. The scalar self-interaction potential contains a temperature parameter $a$. As $a$ approaches its critical value, the imaginary part of $Φ$ becomes double-kinked and the energy density splits into two peaks, indicating the formation of two sub-branes. The analysis of linear scalar, vector, and tensor perturbations shows no signs of instability in the scalar and vector sectors, while the factorization of the tensor perturbation equation excludes tachyonic tensor modes. The graviton zero mode is localized on the Minkowski and dS branes but is not normalizable on the AdS brane. We further study a real test scalar field $Ψ$ with a coupling function $F(R)$ between its kinetic term and the spacetime curvature. The scalar zero mode can always be localized on the Minkowski and dS branes, with a continuous gapless spectrum of scalar Kaluza--Klein (KK) modes. Massive scalar resonances emerge near the critical value of $a$ for suitable values of $β$. Increasing $β$ produces more resonances and prolongs the lifetimes of the lower resonances, while their time evolution exhibits the characteristic decay of metastable modes. For the AdS brane, the scalar zero mode is also localized, whereas the divergent effective potential at the boundaries traps the massive scalar KK modes as bound states, yielding a discrete mass spectrum.

</details>


### [98] [A Geodesic Route toward Holography beyond AdS: Tessellating the Schwarzschild Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21471)
*Shi Gu,Qiang Wen,Haocheng Zhong,Yiwei Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出并证明，Schwarzschild黑洞外部区域与其Einstein--Rosen桥可被从边界发出的特定测地线气体（geodesic gas）完美镶嵌，且该构造可推广至更一般的黎曼流形，从而将部分纠缠熵（partial entanglement entropy, PEE）张量网络模型从真空AdS推广到更一般的几何背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真空反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）空间中，几何可被测地线网络完美镶嵌，并据此定义AdS/CFT的PEE张量网络玩具模型；但该构造能否推广到真空AdS之外此前尚不清楚，本文旨在迈出这一方向的第一步。

Method: 证明Schwarzschild黑洞外部区域及其Einstein--Rosen桥可被从边界发射的特定测地线气体完美镶嵌，使Crofton重构在该区域成立；随后证明此类测地线镶嵌及其定义的Crofton重构可推广到更一般的黎曼流形（Riemannian manifolds），据此构造更一般几何背景上的PEE张量网络模型。

Result: 在Schwarzschild背景的PEE张量网络模型中，具体实现了Bekenstein--Hawking熵、Ryu--Takayanagi公式以及ER=EPR提议；并将测地线镶嵌与Crofton重构推广至更一般的黎曼流形。

Conclusion: 该工作为超越AdS/CFT的全息玩具模型开辟了新路径。

Abstract: In vacuum anti-de Sitter (AdS) space, the geometry admits a perfect tessellation by a geodesic network. This tessellation characterizes the background exactly, and a partial entanglement entropy (PEE) tensor-network toy model of AdS/CFT can be defined on it. It has so far been unclear whether this construction can be extended beyond vacuum AdS. In this paper we take the first step in this direction. We show that the exterior region of the Schwarzschild black hole and its Einstein--Rosen bridge are perfectly tessellated by a specific geodesic gas emitted from the boundary, so that Crofton reconstruction holds in these regions. We then prove that such a geodesic tessellation and the Crofton reconstruction it defines extend to more generic Riemannian manifolds. This allows us to construct a PEE tensor-network model of holographic duality on more generic geometric backgrounds. In the case of the PEE tensor-network model for the Schwarzschild background, we can concretely realize the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy, the Ryu--Takayanagi formula and the ER=EPR proposal. Our approach opens a new route towards holographic toy models beyond AdS/CFT.

</details>


### [99] [BMS$_3$ modules from path integrals on the coadjoint orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21691)
*Pujian Mao,Xin-Cheng Mao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文通过单圈（one-loop）路径积分量子化，系统构造了与渐近对称群（asymptotic symmetry groups）余伴随轨道（coadjoint orbits）相关的量子模（quantum modules），并对 Virasoro 群与 BMS\(_3\) 群给出具体结果与特征标计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近对称群的余伴随轨道在引力与规范理论中具有重要物理意义，但其量子模的系统构造尚不清晰，尤其是哈密顿量（Hamiltonians）无下界的轨道如何定义收敛的路径积分这一问题亟需解决。

Method: 采用单圈阶的路径积分量子化（path integral quantization at one-loop order）系统构造量子模；对无下界哈密顿量的轨道，选取合适的积分围道（integration contour）以定义收敛的欧几里得半直线路径积分（Euclidean half-line path integral）；参考态由几何作用量（geometric action）在该余伴随轨道上的路径积分确定，余伴随模由参考态及其后代态（descendants）张成。

Result: 将形式体系应用于 Virasoro 群与 BMS\(_3\) 群，导出了对应于所有常轨道（constant orbits）的余伴随模；并通过统计模内状态计算了所有常 Virasoro 与 BMS\(_3\) 余伴随轨道的特征标（characters），结果与在具有周期性欧几里得方向（periodic Euclidean direction）下几何作用量路径积分的相应计算一致。

Conclusion: 该构造为渐近对称群余伴随轨道的量子模提供了系统框架，且特征标计算结果的自洽性验证了几何作用量路径积分方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a systematic construction of quantum modules associated with coadjoint orbits of asymptotic symmetry groups through the path integral quantization at one-loop order. In particular, for the orbits with Hamiltonians unbounded from below, we show that an appropriate choice of integration contour defines a convergent Euclidean half-line path integral. The reference state associate with the orbit is determined by the path integral of the geometric action on the corresponding coadjoint orbit. The coadjoint module is therefore spanned by the reference state and its descendants. We apply this formalism to the Virasoro and BMS$_3$ groups and derive the coadjoint modules corresponding to all constant orbits. We also compute the characters of all constant Virasoro and BMS$_3$ coadjoint orbits by counting the states within the modules, and the results agree with the corresponding computations from the path integral of the geometric action with a periodic Euclidean direction.

</details>


### [100] [A Split-Signature Type IIB Background on $\mathrm{AdS}_{5,\mathbb C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21783)
*Brenno Carlini Vallilo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在复 \(\mathrm{AdS}_5\) 底流形 \(T^*S^5\) 上构造了一个 \((5,5)\) 号差的极大超对称 Type IIB 背景，其非线性自对偶五形式应力张量支撑标量平坦、非爱因斯坦（non-Einstein）度规，并给出三十二个整体实 Killing 旋量与对应的实形式超代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要以“构造”（We construct）直接切入，未明确交代研究缺口；可推断其动机在于探索非洛伦兹号差下的超引力/弦理论背景及其超对称结构，尤其是无扭曲 \(\mathrm{IIB}_5\) 理论的实条件实现。

Method: 在 \(T^*S^5\) 上取最一般的 \(\mathrm{SL}(4,\mathbb C)\) 不变度规与五形式拟设，要求五形式自对偶（self-duality）并满足爱因斯坦方程，由此定出场的相位（整体标度、定向、通量符号除外）。采用全纯余标架（holomorphic coframe）使超协变曲率相消显式化，并通过平坦输运求解 Killing 旋量。

Result: 所得背景为极大超对称（maximally supersymmetric）：度规 \(G=\mathrm i(\bar q-q)\) 标量平坦且非爱因斯坦，五形式 \(F_5=(4\mathrm i/L)(\Omega-\bar\Omega)\) 自对偶并支撑该度规；场满足无扭曲 \(\mathrm{IIB}_5\) 的实条件；得到三十二个整体实 Killing 旋量，其 Killing 超代数（Killing superalgebra）为 \(\mathfrak{psl}(4|4,\mathbb C)\) 的“因子交换”（factor-exchanging）实形式。

Conclusion: 该构造给出一个号差为 \((5,5)\) 的极大超对称 Type IIB 背景，并识别出其对应的超陪集（supercoset）结构，表明这类非洛伦兹背景可用 \(\mathrm{SL}(4,\mathbb C)\) 不变拟设与全纯余标架方法系统地导出。

Abstract: We construct a maximally supersymmetric Type IIB background in signature $(5,5)$ on the real ten-manifold underlying complex $\mathrm{AdS}_5$. The spacetime is diffeomorphic to $T^*S^5$ and carries the invariant metric $G=\mathrm i(\bar q-q)$, where $q$ is the holomorphic constant-curvature metric. The real five-form $F_5=(4\mathrm i/L)(Ω-\barΩ)$, built from its normalized volume form, is self-dual, and its stress tensor supports this scalar-flat, non-Einstein metric. We derive these fields from the most general $\mathrm{SL}(4,\mathbb C)$-invariant metric and five-form ansatz: self-duality and the Einstein equation fix their phases up to the overall scale, orientation, and flux sign. The fields obey the reality conditions of the untwisted $\mathrm{IIB}_5$ theory. The holomorphic coframe makes the supercovariant curvature cancellation explicit, and flat transport gives thirty-two global real Killing spinors. Their Killing superalgebra is the factor-exchanging real form of $\mathfrak{psl}(4|4,\mathbb C)$, and we identify the corresponding supercoset.

</details>


### [101] [Diagnosing Inconsistencies in 4d N=1 Gauge Theories with Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21867)
*Seong-Jin Lee,Rak-Kyeong Seong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用卷积神经网络（CNN）从Kasteleyn矩阵直接判别膜铺砌（brane tilings）的几何一致性，并通过显著性分析定位导致不一致的手征场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膜铺砌（brane tilings）是编码D3膜探测环面Calabi-Yau 3-folds所得4d N=1超对称规范理论拉格朗日量的二分图，其中仅满足几何一致性条件者对应良定义量子场论，故需要高效判别一致性的方法。

Method: 构建由在C^3/Z_3 x Z_3阿贝尔orbifold的六边形面上添加对角边得到的4d N=1理论族，训练CNN直接以Kasteleyn矩阵为输入区分几何一致与不一致的膜铺砌，并采用基于梯度的显著性（saliency）分析。

Result: CNN能以高准确率识别几何不一致性；对于可通过Higgsing单个双基本手征场（bifundamental chiral field）恢复一致的膜铺砌，显著性分析定位责任手征场的准确率显著高于匹配的随机基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明可解释人工智能（XAI）可用于识别超对称规范理论中导致不一致性的局域缺陷。

Abstract: Brane tilings are bipartite graphs on a 2-torus that encode the Lagrangians of 4d N=1 supersymmetric gauge theories arising on D3-branes probing toric Calabi-Yau 3-folds. Among these bipartite graphs, only those that satisfy geometric consistency conditions correspond to well-behaved quantum field theories. We train a convolutional neural network (CNN) to distinguish geometrically consistent from inconsistent brane tilings directly from their Kasteleyn matrices. We study a family of 4d N=1 theories obtained by adding diagonal edges to the hexagonal faces of the brane tiling for the abelian orbifold C^3/Z_3 x Z_3, and find that the CNN identifies geometric inconsistency with high accuracy. For inconsistent brane tilings that can be rendered consistent by Higgsing a single bifundamental chiral field, we show that gradient-based saliency analysis can be used to localize the responsible chiral fields with accuracy well above a matched random baseline. These results demonstrate that explainable AI (XAI) can be used to identify local defects responsible for inconsistencies in supersymmetric gauge theories.

</details>


### [102] [Multi-Boundary Spinning $AdS_5$ Black Holes, Strongly Coupled Plasma Balls and a dual locally de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22084)
*Andrés Anabalón,Guillermo Buckle,Annelies Fullgraff,Marcelo Oyarzo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在五维负宇宙学常数的广义相对论中构造出一族新的精确旋转黑洞解，其对偶为四维闵可夫斯基时空上的强耦合旋转等离子球。边界洛伦兹因子（Lorentz factor）在体时空中充当新坐标，产生一个新的紫外区域（UV region），该区域局部为三维德西特时空（de Sitter spacetime）与实直线的乘积，其对偶能动张量描述满足弱、强能量条件的共形各向异性流体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作延续近期四维体时空中此类黑洞解的发现，旨在将旋转黑洞解及其全息对偶（holographic duality）推广到五维体时空，以刻画四维边界上强耦合旋转等离子球（spinning plasma balls）的对偶描述。

Method: 在含负宇宙学常数（negative cosmological constant）的五维广义相对论（General Relativity）中构造精确旋转黑洞解，采用全息方法（holography）将其对偶到四维闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）上的强耦合旋转等离子球；核心机制是令边界洛伦兹因子（Lorentz factor）充当体时空中的新坐标，从而给出新的紫外区域。

Result: 所得解具有新的紫外区域，该区域局部为三维德西特时空（de Sitter spacetime）乘以实直线（real line）；对应的对偶能动张量（energy-momentum tensor）描述一种共形各向异性流体（conformal anisotropic fluid），并满足弱能量条件与强能量条件（weak and strong energy conditions）。

Conclusion: 该族五维精确旋转黑洞解为强耦合旋转等离子球提供了新的全息对偶描述，并揭示由边界洛伦兹因子作为体坐标所诱导的紫外区域及其共形各向异性流体性质。

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact spinning black hole solutions in five-dimensional General Relativity with a negative cosmological constant, which are dual to strongly coupled spinning plasma balls on four-dimensional Minkowski spacetime. In analogy with the recent discovery of such black solutions in four bulk dimensions, the boundary Lorentz factor acts as a new coordinate in the bulk, giving rise to a new UV region that, in our case, is locally a three-dimensional de Sitter spacetime times the real line. The dual energy-momentum tensor in this region describes a conformal anisotropic fluid that satisfies the weak and strong energy conditions.

</details>


<div id='math.RA'></div>

# math.RA [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Restricted (Relative) Rota-Baxter operators on restricted Lie algebras and restricted Lie triple systems and related structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21703)
*Jon Beristain,Abdenacer Makhlouf*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 本文将限制 Rota-Baxter 算子（restricted Rota-Baxter operator）的概念推广至限制李代数（restricted Lie algebra）与限制李三系（restricted Lie triple system），建立其与 pre-Lie 结构的联系，并引入限制 pre-Lie 三系（restricted pre-Lie triple system）的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在在正特征（positive characteristic）的限制（restricted）代数框架下定义并研究限制 Rota-Baxter 算子，并将其结构理论与 pre-Lie 结构及算子分裂（splitting of operads）问题相联系。

Method: 在限制李代数与限制李三系上定义限制 Rota-Baxter 算子，讨论其相关性质及与 pre-Lie 结构的通常联系；并证明 pre-Lie 三系的算子（operad）是李三系算子的分裂，以及正特征下 pre-Lie 三系中 Jacobson 恒等式（Jacobson identity）成立。

Result: 给出了限制 Rota-Baxter 算子在限制李代数与限制李三系上的性质及其与 pre-Lie 结构的联系；证明了 pre-Lie 三系算子是李三系算子的分裂；并证明正特征下 pre-Lie 三系满足 Jacobson 恒等式。

Conclusion: 基于上述结果，论文进一步引入限制 pre-Lie 三系的概念，从而把 Rota-Baxter 算子与 pre-Lie 结构的研究扩展到正特征下的限制代数体系。

Abstract: The main purpose of this paper is to define and study the notion of restricted Rota-Baxter operator on restricted Lie algebras and restricted Lie triple systems. We provide the relevant properties and the usual connections with pre-Lie structures. We show that the operad of pre-Lie triple systems is the splitting of the operad of Lie triple systems. Moreover, we prove that Jacobson identities hold in pre-Lie triple systems in positive characteristic and then introduce the notion of restricted pre-Lie triple system.

</details>
