### [104] [Deformation-induced amorphous complexion transitions elevate strength and ductility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20908)
*Masoud Ahmadi,Jin Qin,Gabrielle Tiphéne,Mohamed Charai,Alejandro Gómez-Pérez,Khalid Hoummada,Thomas Pardoen,Matteo Ghidelli,Hosni Idrissi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在化学预调的纳米晶二元 CuZr 合金（nanocrystalline binary CuZr alloy）中发现，塑性变形（plastic deformation）可在室温下驱动非晶复杂相（amorphous complexion）转变，并由此提出一种新的复杂相转变诱导塑性（transformation-induced plasticity）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界工程（grain boundary engineering）是调控多晶材料力学行为的主要途径，而晶界复杂相（grain boundary complexions，含非晶晶间膜）传统上依赖热驱动力与溶质偏聚（solute segregation）获得。研究旨在探索是否存在非热（non-thermal）途径触发此类非晶界面状态，以改善纳米结构金属（nanostructured metals）的损伤容限（damage tolerance）。

Method: 采用高分辨与四维扫描透射电子显微镜（four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy, 4D-STEM）表征非晶复杂相的空间分布，并结合原子尺度的空间分辨电子对分布函数分析（electron pair distribution function analysis）刻画其局域有序度。力学性能通过拉伸试验与设计参考材料对比评估。

Result: 非晶复杂相优先在非共格孪晶界（incoherent twin boundaries）处形成；电子对分布函数分析显示，从晶体模板化界面到类金属玻璃核心存在短程与中程有序梯度（short-range and medium-range order gradients）。所揭示的机制使屈服强度（yield strength）、断裂应变（fracture strain）与拉伸韧性（tensile toughness）相对设计参考材料同时提高约两倍。

Conclusion: 研究确立机械变形（mechanical deformation）可作为触发非晶界面状态的非热途径，为提升纳米结构金属的损伤容限提供新思路；但摘要未提供变形量、温度区间上限或成分范围等定量边界条件。

Abstract: Grain boundary engineering is a major avenue for tailoring the mechanical behavior of polycrystalline materials. Grain boundary complexions, including amorphous intergranular films, are classically accessed through thermal driving forces and solute segregation. Here, we discover that plastic deformation can drive amorphous complexion transitions at room temperature in a chemically primed nanocrystalline binary CuZr alloy. High-resolution and four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy reveal that the amorphous complexions preferentially emerge at incoherent twin boundaries. Spatially-resolved electron pair distribution function analysis at the atomic scale, the local-order characterization of amorphous complexions, demonstrates short-range and medium-range order gradients from crystal-templated interfaces to a metallic-glass-like core. We thus uncover a novel amorphous complexion transformation-induced plasticity mechanism that concurrently increases the yield strength, fracture strain, and tensile toughness about a factor of two relative to a designed reference material. Our findings establish mechanical deformation as a non-thermal pathway to trigger amorphous interfacial states for enhancing damage tolerance in nanostructured metals.

</details>


### [105] [Faulted loop nucleation and dopant activation in Al-implanted 4H-SiC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20986)
*Sabine Leroch,Robert Stella,Andreas Hössinger,Lado Filipovic*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟研究 4H-SiC 中 Al 注入，揭示注入温度与剂量如何通过缺陷团簇演化影响退火过程中 Al 的化学激活，并给出平面缺陷向层错环转变的原子尺度机制与激活能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验假设认为过饱和条件下退火时二次缺陷（secondary defects）导致 Al 激活率下降，但缺乏原子尺度的机制证据；本文旨在从原子尺度厘清注入温度、剂量与缺陷演化及掺杂激活之间的关系。

Method: 基于 Gao-Weber 势（potential），并将 Al–SiC 相互作用以 Morse 势重新参数化，使其符合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）给出的扩散势垒与 kick-in/kick-out 势垒，开展 Al 注入 4H-SiC 的分子动力学（MD）模拟，统计注入与退火过程中的缺陷与原子轨迹。

Result: 在 Al 浓度超过约 2e20/cm³ 的饱和极限（saturation limit）时，900 K 注入在注入阶段即形成富间隙平面团簇（interstitial-rich planar clusters）；退火中这些团簇俘获 Al 并演化为层错间隙环（faulted interstitial loops），降低替位 Al（substitutional Al）的并入。较低注入温度保留更强局部无序，该无序在再结晶（epitaxial regrowth）中被消耗，从而在 MD 可及退火时间内获得更高的化学激活。平面团簇达到约 60 个间隙原子时发生热激活转变为层错环，且先形成多个局部层错区再合并，而非以单一共格圆盘形核。位错形核激活能约 1.1 eV，层错长大约 2.1 eV；高温以 Frank 型环为主，瞬态 Shockley 分位错主要出现在早期及 2000 K 以下；大平面缺陷形成与溶解过程中还形成稳定的补偿性 Al–C 复合体（Al-C complexes）。

Conclusion: 模拟结果支持实验假设：过饱和退火条件下二次缺陷的形成是 Al 激活率降低的重要原因；注入温度通过调控注入态无序程度影响后续激活效率，平面缺陷演化与补偿复合体的生成共同决定最终替位 Al 含量。

Abstract: We present a molecular dynamics (MD) study of Al implantation in 4H-SiC to determine how implantation temperature and dose affect defect evolution and dopant activation during annealing. Simulations use the Gao-Weber potential with a Morse Al-SiC interaction reparameterized to density functional theory diffusion and kick-in/kick-out barriers. At Al concentrations above the saturation limit of ~2e20/cm3, implantation at 900 K promotes interstitial-rich planar clusters already during implantation. During annealing, these clusters trap Al and evolve into faulted interstitial loops, reducing substitutional Al incorporation. In contrast, lower implantation temperatures preserve stronger local disorder that is consumed during epitaxial regrowth, resulting in higher chemical activation within MD-accessible annealing times. Atomistic trajectories show a thermally activated transition to faulted loops once planar clusters reach about 60 interstitials. Rather than nucleating as a single coherent disk, several locally faulted regions form first and subsequently merge. The activation energy is ~1.1 eV for dislocation nucleation and ~2.1 eV for stacking-fault growth. Frank-type loops dominate at high temperature, while transient Shockley partials occur mainly at early stages and below 2000 K. As large planar defects form and dissolve, stable compensating Al-C complexes also emerge. These findings support the experimental hypothesis that secondary defects contribute to the reduced Al activation observed under supersaturation during annealing.

</details>


### [106] [Dual-Sublattice Ferromagnetism Driven by Cooperative Double Exchange and Superexchange at NdNiO$_3$/CaMnO$_3$ Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21002)
*Sharup Sheikh,Uditha M. Jayathilake,Michael Terilli,Mikhail Kareev,Jay R. Paudel,Arian Arab,Christoph Klewe,Tien-Lin Lee,Jak Chakhalian,Alexander X. Gray*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过 NdNiO$_3$/CaMnO$_3$ 超晶格（superlattice）的界面价态重构（valence reconstruction），实现了同时包含 Mn 与 Ni 的双子晶格铁磁性（dual-sublattice ferromagnetism）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关联氧化物（correlated oxide）界面工程化涌现铁磁性（emergent ferromagnetism），可为创生母体材料中不存在的集体态提供有效途径，本文旨在探索此类界面的实现机制。

Method: 结合深度分辨驻波 X 射线光电子能谱（depth-resolved standing-wave X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, SW-XPS）与元素特异性 X 射线磁圆二色性（element-specific XMCD）对 NdNiO$_3$/CaMnO$_3$ 超晶格界面进行表征。

Result: SW-XPS 显示界面 CaMnO$_3$ 一侧 Mn$^{3+}$ 特征增强、NdNiO$_3$ 一侧 Ni$^{2+}$ 特征增强；低温 XMCD 显示两个子晶格均具铁磁响应，且 Ni 响应集中于界面增强的 Ni$^{2+}$ 谱分量。

Conclusion: CaMnO$_3$ 内的协同双交换（double exchange）与跨界面的超交换（superexchange）耦合了 Mn 与 Ni 子晶格，为关联氧化物界面铁磁性工程提供通用策略。

Abstract: Engineering emergent ferromagnetism at correlated-oxide interfaces offers a powerful route to creating collective states that do not exist in the parent materials. Here, we show that interfacial valence reconstruction in NdNiO$_3$/CaMnO$_3$ superlattices generates dual-sublattice ferromagnetism involving both Mn and Ni. Depth-resolved standing-wave X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals enhanced Mn$^{3+}$ character on the CaMnO$_3$ side of the interface and enhanced Ni$^{2+}$ character on the NdNiO$_3$ side, establishing the configurations required for Mn$^{4+}$-O-Mn$^{3+}$ double exchange and Ni$^{2+}$-O-Mn$^{4+}$ superexchange, respectively. At low temperature, element-specific XMCD reveals ferromagnetic responses from both sublattices, with the Ni response concentrated predominantly in the Ni$^{2+}$-derived spectral component that SW-XPS independently shows to be enhanced at the interface. Together, these results show that cooperative double exchange within CaMnO$_3$ and superexchange across the interface couple the Mn and Ni sublattices, providing a general strategy for engineering interfacial ferromagnetism in correlated oxides.

</details>


### [107] [Mechanism of charge transfer and electrostatic field fluctuations in complex metallic alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21065)
*Wai-Ga D. Ho,Wasim Raja Mondal,Swarnava Ghosh,Hanna Terletska,Ka-Ming Tam,Mariia Karabin,Markus Eisenbach,Yang Wang,Vladimir Dobrosavljević*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作建立理论框架，揭示化学无序合金中由无序驱动的电荷转移与马德隆场涨落（Madelung-field fluctuations）具有普适统计规律，并提出高效刻画无序静电涨落的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂金属合金（complex metallic alloys）因强化学无序呈现丰富的电子、磁性与振动行为，但化学无序合金中电荷再分布与静电涨落的微观本质尚未被充分理解。

Method: 发展解析理论形式（analytical formalism），刻画局域电荷转移与静电势的统计性质，并结合大超胞密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）计算进行验证。

Result: 发现局域电荷转移与静电势呈高斯型（Gaussian-like）统计，并存在普适的线性电荷—势（qV）关联；其物理起源为电子屏蔽（electronic screening）与杂质散射（impurity scattering）的相互作用，且统计性质依赖载流子密度、无序强度与组分复杂度，并给出跨二元与多组分合金（含高熵材料）的标度关系（scaling relations）。

Conclusion: 该框架可将无序驱动的静电涨落纳入有效介质电子结构理论（effective-medium electronic-structure theories），并为复杂合金的预测性设计提供统计物理（statistical physics）基础。

Abstract: Complex metallic alloys exhibit rich disorder-driven electronic, magnetic, and vibrational behavior arising from strong chemical disorder, leading to unconventional structure-property relationships with applications in extreme-environment materials, catalysis, spintronics, and thermoelectrics. Despite their technological relevance, the microscopic nature of charge redistribution and electrostatic fluctuations in chemically disordered alloys remains incompletely understood. Here, we develop a theoretical framework that uncovers universal statistical trends of disorder-driven charge transfer and Madelung-field fluctuations. Our analytical formalism demonstrates that local charge transfer and electrostatic potentials exhibit Gaussian-like statistics and universal linear charge-potential (qV) correlations emerging directly from the underlying disorder landscape. We identify the physical origin of these correlations in the interplay between electronic screening and impurity scattering and show how their statistical properties depend on carrier density, disorder strength, and compositional complexity. We further derive scaling relations governing qV trends across binary and multicomponent alloys, including high-entropy materials. Large-supercell density-functional theory (DFT) calculations show good quantitative agreement with the predicted statistical behavior for representative metallic alloys. Our results provide a computationally efficient framework for incorporating disorder-driven electrostatic fluctuations into effective-medium electronic-structure theories and establish a statistical-physics foundation for the predictive design of complex alloys.

</details>


### [108] [Polymer-free Assembly of Unencapsulated van der Waals Heterostructure Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21120)
*Han Xuan Wong,Junjie Yu,Yuyi Yan,Adam Cronin,Felix R. Fischer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出以白云母（muscovite mica）作为无聚合物（polymer-free）平台，实现范德华（van der Waals, vdW）异质结构的转移、逐层组装与接触图案化，并可用于表面敏感表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vdW 异质结构是研究新奇量子现象的重要平台，但其制备常受界面污染（interfacial contamination）制约，且与表面敏感表征技术不兼容。现有方法需借助聚合物且难以获得洁净暴露表面，因此需要一种简便、清洁的组装与图案化途径。

Method: 以白云母作为聚合物-free 支撑平台：通过 mica 介导转移（mica-mediated transfer）获得洁净暴露表面，经温和热退火（mild thermal annealing）后适用于原子分辨扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）；mica 兼具机械稳健性，可支持连续拾取组装（sequential pickup assembly）并省去中间释放步骤；剥离的 mica 薄片作为可移除阴影掩模（removable shadow mask）用于接触沉积（contact deposition），从而无需传统光刻（lithography）即可实现图案化。

Result: 量化了 mica 介导石墨烯拾取（graphene pickup）的温度依赖性，并在所制备的三端石墨烯/hBN 器件中展示了栅压依赖的输运（gate-dependent transport）行为。

Conclusion: 将转移、逐层组装与接触图案化集成于单一材料平台，mica 为未封装（unencapsulated）vdW 异质结构提供了一条简单且易获取的途径，服务于表面敏感光谱学（surface-sensitive spectroscopy）与量子器件应用。

Abstract: Van der Waals (vdW) heterostructures provide a versatile platform for exploring emergent quantum phenomena, yet their fabrication is often limited by interfacial contamination and incompatibility with surface-sensitive characterization. Here, we demonstrate muscovite mica as a polymer-free platform for assembling and patterning vdW heterostructure devices. Mica-mediated transfer enables clean exposed surfaces suitable for atomic-resolution scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) following mild thermal annealing, without aggressive post-processing. Mica also serves as a mechanically robust support for sequential pickup assembly, eliminating intermediate release steps. Furthermore, exfoliated mica flakes function as removable shadow masks for contact deposition, enabling straightforward patterning without conventional lithography. We quantify the temperature dependence of mica-mediated graphene pickup and demonstrate gate-dependent transport in a three-terminal graphene/hBN device fabricated using the approach. By integrating transfer, sequential assembly, and contact patterning within a single materials platform, mica provides a simple and accessible route to unencapsulated vdW heterostructures for surface-sensitive spectroscopy and quantum-device applications.

</details>


### [109] [Adaptive Mesh Coarsening for Efficient Phase-Field Fracture Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21201)
*Aarosh Dahal,Abhinav Gupta,Aditya Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向相场断裂（phase-field fracture）的自适应网格粗化（adaptive mesh coarsening）与细化框架，通过将裂纹尾部已断裂区域替换为保持裂纹关键力学行为的粗裂纹带（coarse crack band），仅需在裂纹尖端附近保留细网格，并引入基于材料强度面违背的物理型细化指示因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场模型需用足够细的有限元网格（finite element mesh）分辨小正则化长度（regularization length），均匀网格在三维、分布式裂纹萌生及软近不可压材料等问题中计算成本过高。现有自适应细化（adaptive mesh refinement）方法多依赖启发式指示因子、主要针对含预置裂纹问题，且裂纹扩展时细化区持续增大。

Method: 提出自适应网格粗化与细化相结合的框架：粗化策略以粗裂纹带替代裂纹尾部的断裂区，保留裂纹关键力学行为；并引入由材料强度面（material strength surface）违背推导的物理型细化指示因子（physics-based refinement indicator），可稳健识别即将发生裂纹萌生或扩展的区域。框架在 FEniCSx 中并行实现并公开有限元代码。

Result: 通过涵盖准静态与动态加载下的裂纹扩展与萌生、软硬材料以及热机械断裂（thermomechanical fracture）等基准问题，展示了方法的通用性与显著计算收益。

Conclusion: 该自适应粗化-细化框架可适用于任意材料、几何与加载条件，将细网格需求限制在裂纹尖端小区域，从而在相场断裂模拟中显著降低计算成本并保持通用性。

Abstract: Phase-field models provide a versatile framework for simulating fracture nucleation and propagation without explicit crack tracking. Their principal computational challenge is the need to resolve a small regularization length with a sufficiently fine finite element mesh. This requirement can render uniform-mesh simulations prohibitively expensive, particularly for three-dimensional problems, problems involving distributed crack nucleation, and soft nearly incompressible materials. Adaptive mesh refinement offers a natural means of reducing this cost, but existing approaches often rely on heuristic refinement indicators, are primarily designed for problems containing pre-existing cracks, and retain increasingly large refined regions as cracks grow in size.
  This work presents an adaptive mesh coarsening framework along with adaptive mesh refinement for phase-field fracture. The coarsening strategy replaces the fractured region in the crack wake with a coarse crack band that preserves the essential mechanical behavior of a crack, making it necessary to have a refined mesh only in a small region near the tip of the growing crack. The method introduces a physics-based refinement indicator derived from the violation of the material strength surface, which is a necessary condition for fracture evolution. The indicator therefore robustly identifies regions where crack nucleation or propagation is imminent and can be applied across arbitrary materials, geometries, and loading conditions. The framework is implemented in parallel within FEniCSx; the supporting finite element codes are made available. Its generality and substantial computational benefit are demonstrated through benchmark problems involving crack propagation and nucleation under quasi-static and dynamic loading, for soft and hard materials, and for thermomechanical fracture.

</details>


### [110] [The Critical Role of Itinerant Contributions to Orbital Angular Momentum Relaxation and Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21300)
*Andrew C. Grieder,Luis M. Canonico,Frederico Simões,Aron W. Cummings,Yuan Ping*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理 Lindblad 密度矩阵动力学，阐明了轨道角动量（OAM）弛豫中原子中心近似（ACA）与 itinerant（巡游）贡献的差异，指出 ACA 轨道寿命被晶体场劈裂驱动的超快进动显著低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OAM 是低耗散输运与磁化控制的重要自由度，但其弛豫机制存在争议：原子中心近似（ACA）预测的 OAM 扩散长度远短于实验值，需澄清该差异来源。

Method: 采用第一性原理 Lindbladian 密度矩阵动力学，纳入电子-声子散射与巡游（itinerant）OAM 贡献；并结合第一性原理参数化的紧束缚（tight-binding）方法，分离 ACA 与巡游分量。

Result: 在强自旋轨道耦合（SOC）的 MoS2 中，轨道弛豫呈多时间尺度：先是快速谷间再分布，随后是与自旋耦合的较慢衰减。在弱 SOC 的硅烯（silicene）中，自旋与轨道动力学解耦，电场可调控自旋弛豫而不改变轨道寿命。两种材料中 ACA 轨道寿命均比巡游寿命短至少一个数量级，源于晶体场劈裂驱动的超快进动，该效应在巡游分量中不存在。

Conclusion: 结果表明，必须超越原子中心模型（atom-centered models）才能正确描述轨道弛豫与扩散。

Abstract: Orbital angular momentum (OAM) is a promising degree of freedom for low-dissipation transport and magnetization control, yet its relaxation mechanisms remain controversial, with atomcentered approximations (ACA) predicting much shorter OAM diffusion lengths than experiments. We address this discrepancy using first-principles Lindbladian density-matrix dynamics, capturing electron-phonon scattering and itinerant OAM contributions, together with a first-principles parameterized tight-binding approach that separates the ACA and itinerant components. In MoS2,a strong-spin-orbit-coupling (SOC) system, orbital relaxation is multi-timescale, with fast intervalley redistribution followed by slower decay coupled to the spin. In weak-SOC silicene, spin and orbital dynamics decouple; an electric field tunes spin relaxation while leaving orbital lifetimes unchanged. In both materials, ACA orbital lifetimes are at least one order of magnitude shorter than itinerant ones, due to ultrafast precession driven by crystal-field splitting, absent from the itinerant component. These results demonstrate that going beyond atom-centered models is essential for describing orbital relaxation and diffusion.

</details>


### [111] [Strain-Induced Relaxor Multiferroicity at Room Temperature in Hexaferrite BaFe12O19 Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21352)
*Yilin Evan Li,Harikrishnan KP,Sankalpa Hazra,Zhiren He,Jayanti Higgins,ChanJu You,Mario Brützam,Jiaqiang Yan,Anna Park,Maya Ramesh,Wenwen Zhao,Jayda Shine,David G. Mandrus,Ramamoorthy Ramesh,Ankit S. Disa,Christo Guguschev,Guru Khalsa,Craig J. Fennie,Venkatraman Gopalan,Yu-Tsun Shao,David A. Muller,Darrell G. Schlom*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过外延应变（epitaxial strain）在室温铁磁体六方铁氧体（hexaferrite）BaFe12O19中诱导出极性有序，使其成为室温弛豫多铁（relaxor multiferroic）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兼具磁有序与电有序的室温多铁材料（multiferroic materials）极为稀少，因此需要寻找将本征（incipient）铁电性与铁磁性结合的新途径。

Method: 第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）预测压应变（compressive strain）有利于极性畸变；在等结构（isostructural）Sr1.03Ga10.81Mg0.58Zr0.58O19衬底上生长具1.1%面内双轴压应变的共格应变（coherently strained）BaFe12O19薄膜，并结合二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation）、多层电子叠层成像（multislice electron ptychography）与路径积分蒙特卡洛（path-integral Monte Carlo）模拟进行表征与机理分析。

Result: 二次谐波产生测量证实反演对称性破缺（inversion-symmetry breaking），应变稳定的极性相（polar phase）至少持续到1000 K；电子叠层成像直接揭示应变薄膜三角双锥（trigonal-bipyramidal）位点中Fe3+离子偏心增强与空间变化的局域极化，形成极性纳米区（polar nanoregions）；模拟显示压应变抑制量子涨落并稳定局域极性畸变。

Conclusion: 应变工程（strain-engineered）的BaFe12O19被确立为室温弛豫多铁，兼具稳健铁磁性（ferrimagnetism）与纳米尺度极性有序；该工作展示了通过外延应变将本征铁电铁磁体（incipient ferroelectric ferrimagnetic）转变为极性磁性材料的路径。

Abstract: Multiferroic materials that combine magnetic and electric order at room temperature are rare. Here, we demonstrate strain-induced room-temperature polar order in the ferrimagnetic hexaferrite BaFe12O19. First-principles calculations reveal a strain-tunable energy landscape with multiple competing dipolar configurations and predict that compressive strain favors polar distortions. Using an isostructural Sr1.03Ga10.81Mg0.58Zr0.58O19 substrate, we grow coherently strained BaFe12O19 films with 1.1% in-plane biaxial compression. Second-harmonic generation measurements demonstrate inversion-symmetry breaking and establish a strain-stabilized polar phase that persists to at least 1000 K. Multislice electron ptychography directly reveals enhanced off-centering of Fe3+ ions within the trigonal-bipyramidal sites of the strained films and spatially varying local polarization, demonstrating the formation of polar nanoregions. Path-integral Monte Carlo simulations further show that compressive strain suppresses quantum fluctuations and stabilizes these local polar distortions. Together, these results establish strain-engineered BaFe12O19 as a room-temperature relaxor multiferroic, in which robust ferrimagnetism coexists with nanoscale polar order. Our work demonstrates a route for transforming an incipient ferroelectric ferrimagnetic into a polar magnetic material through epitaxial strain.

</details>


### [112] [Single Best Fits Can Be Misleading: Resolving Common Trapping Signatures across FA--Cs Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21545)
*Maxim Simmonds,Katarzyna Pydzińska-Białek,Thomas C. Rossi,Mostafa Othman,Aïcha Hessler-Wyser,Christian Wolff,Christophe Ballif,Vincent M. Le Corre,Eva Unger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合注量依赖的时间分辨光致发光、完整Shockley–Read–Hall建模与贝叶斯后验推断，在FA$_{1-x}$Cs$_x$PbI$_3$组分序列中识别非辐射复合缺陷特征。发现陷阱占据主导载流子动力学与缺陷参数可辨识性，并提取出跨组分共有的$Tβ$、$Tα$特征及FAPbI$_3$特有的浅能级$Tε$特征，为室温下比较不同半导体的陷阱特征提供框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属卤化物钙钛矿（metal-halide perovskites）中造成非辐射复合（non-radiative recombination）的缺陷难以识别，且各组分间尚未建立通用的缺陷特征。研究旨在解决缺陷归属不明与跨组分可比性缺失的问题。

Method: 采用注量依赖的时间分辨光致发光（fluence-dependent time-resolved photoluminescence）、完整Shockley–Read–Hall建模与贝叶斯后验推断（Bayesian posterior inference），对FA$_{1-x}$Cs$_x$PbI$_3$组分序列进行系统分析。

Result: 陷阱占据（trap occupation）决定载流子动力学与缺陷参数可辨识性（identifiability）：弱占据态存在电子俘获系数–陷阱密度的简并性（degeneracy），而陷阱填充可解除简并。所有组分均出现非简并特征$Tβ$（具强不对称$β_p/β_n$俘获系数比），表明显著的陷阱填充是普遍现象；$Tα$为共有且限制器件性能的非辐射复合通道；FAPbI$3$特有的浅能级$Tε$产生最大稳态非辐射复合速率，并与该组分此前观测到的高堆垛层错密度一致。

Conclusion: 该框架可在室温下比较不同半导体材料间的陷阱特征（trapping signatures），为缺陷识别与跨材料对比提供通用基础。

Abstract: Identifying defects responsible for non-radiative recombination in metal-halide perovskites remains challenging, with no common defect signatures established across compositions. Here, we combine fluence-dependent time-resolved photoluminescence, full Shockley--Read--Hall modelling and Bayesian posterior inference across an FA$_{1-x}$Cs$_x$PbI$_3$ compositional series. We show that trap occupation governs carrier dynamics and defect-parameter identifiability in semiconducting materials: weakly occupied states exhibit electron-capture-coefficient--trap-density degeneracies, whereas trap filling lifts them. In our perovskite systems, a non-degenerate signature $Tβ$, characterized by a strongly asymmetric $β_p/β_n$ capture-coefficient ratio, occurs across all compositions, suggesting substantial trap filling is a general feature of perovskite carrier dynamics. We further identify $Tα$ as a shared, device-performance-limiting non-radiative recombination channel, while a shallow $Tε$ signature unique to FAPbI$_3$ produces the largest steady-state non-radiative recombination rate and is consistent with the previously observed high stacking-fault density of this composition. This framework therefore provides a basis for comparing trapping signatures across different semiconducting materials at room temperature.

</details>


### [113] [Orbital angular momentum accumulation in SrVO3 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21551)
*Julien Brehin,Montserrat X. Aguilar-Pujol,Dongwook Go,F. Casanova,J. Fontcuberta,E. Longo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在窄带 d¹ 氧化物 SrVO₃（SVO）外延薄膜中观测到与轨道汉勒磁阻（orbital Hanle magnetoresistance, HMR）一致的正、偶场、近似二次方的 Δρxy 响应，并结合 DFT 计算证明其响应以轨道霍尔效应（orbital Hall effect, OHE）为主，表明窄带 d¹ 金属氧化物是有前景的轨道输运平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻过渡金属中的轨道输运被视为角动量电子学（angular-momentum electronics）的潜在途径，而汉勒磁阻（HMR）可对非磁导体中的轨道霍尔效应（OHE）提供直接电学探测。研究旨在将这一探测手段拓展至窄带 d¹ 氧化物体系，以寻找新的轨道输运平台。

Method: 在 (001) 取向的 (LaAlO₃)₀.₃(Sr₂TaAlO₆)₀.₇ 衬底上外延生长不同厚度与纵向电阻率的 SrVO₃（SVO）薄膜，进行场依赖与角度依赖的磁阻测量，并借助密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算本征轨道霍尔电导率与自旋霍尔电导率，最后以扩散型 HMR 框架估算轨道霍尔角与电导率下界。

Result: 测量得到可复现的正、偶场、近似二次方的 Δρxy 响应，符合 HMR 预期；角度测量刻画了相应的磁阻各向异性并揭示出额外的非汉勒贡献。DFT 给出费米能级处 σ_OH^DFT = 390 (ħ/e) Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹ 与 σ_SH^DFT = −12 (ħ/e) Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹，比值约 33，表明响应以轨道为主。以 λ_OD = 2 nm 为参考值，20.8 nm 薄膜给出保守的饱和极限下界估计 θ_OH,LB = 0.0144 ± 0.0004 与 σ_OH,LB = (460 ± 11) (ħ/e) Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹，与 DFT 值相当。

Conclusion: 同系列样品的下界估计随纵向电导率整体增大。结果表明窄带 d¹ 金属氧化物可作为轨道输运的有利平台。

Abstract: Orbital transport in light transition metals has emerged as a promising route toward angular-momentum electronics, with Hanle magnetoresistance (HMR) providing a direct electrical probe of the orbital Hall effect (OHE) in non-magnetic conductors. Here we report magnetoresistance signatures consistent with orbital HMR in epitaxial SrVO$*3$ (SVO), a narrow-band $d^1$ oxide grown on (001)-oriented (LaAlO$*3$)$*{0.3}$(Sr$*2$TaAlO$*6$)$*{0.7}$ substrates. In films of different thickness and longitudinal resistivity, field-dependent measurements reveal a reproducible positive, even-in-field, and approximately quadratic $Δρ*{x-y}$ response, as expected for HMR. Angular measurements characterize the corresponding magnetoresistance anisotropy and reveal an additional non-Hanle contribution. Density functional theory (DFT) calculations yield an intrinsic orbital Hall conductivity $σ*{\mathrm{OH}}^{\mathrm{DFT}} = 390,(\hbar/e),Ω^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$ and a spin Hall conductivity $σ_{\mathrm{SH}}^{\mathrm{DFT}} = -12,(\hbar/e),Ω^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$ at the Fermi level, corresponding to $|σ_{\mathrm{OH}}^{\mathrm{DFT}}/σ_{\mathrm{SH}}^{\mathrm{DFT}}| \approx 33$, thus indicating a predominantly orbital response. Using the diffusive HMR framework with $λ_{\mathrm{OD}} = 2$ nm as a reference value, the 20.8 nm film gives conservative saturation-limit lower-bound (LB) estimates $θ_{\mathrm{OH,LB}} = 0.0144 \pm 0.0004$ and $σ_{\mathrm{OH,LB}} = (460 \pm 11),(\hbar/e),Ω^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$, comparable to the DFT value. Across the series, lower-bound estimates obtained with the same $λ_{\mathrm{OD}}$ increase overall with longitudinal conductivity. These results suggest that narrow-band $d^1$ metallic oxides are a promising platform for orbital transport.

</details>


### [114] [Validation of an Ab Initio-Informed Electronic Stopping Model for Large-Scale Atomistic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21559)
*Glen P. Kiely,Rafael Nuñez-Palacio,Andrea E. Sand*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作首次将 Tamm 等人提出的非经验电子耗散模型在电子阻止本领（electronic stopping）区间与离子透射实验进行直接基准对比，结果表明模型能准确再现能量损失的大小与轨迹依赖性，支持其用于大规模辐照级联的预测性原子模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在辐射损伤的原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulations）中，准确描述非绝热电子能量耗散（nonadiabatic electronic energy dissipation）仍是核心难题；现有模型多依赖人为阈值，且忽略轨迹依赖（trajectory-dependent）、能带结构（band-structure）与非线性效应。

Method: 采用 Tamm 等人提出的非经验（nonempirical）电子耗散模型，将其原子模拟结果与实验离子透射（ion transmission）实验结果进行对比，从而在电子阻止本领（electronic stopping）区间开展首次直接基准测试（benchmark）。

Result: 模拟结果与实验对比显示，该模型能够准确捕捉预测能量损失的量级（magnitude）及其轨迹依赖性（trajectory-dependence）。

Conclusion: 上述结果支持该模型的可靠性，可将其用于辐射级联（radiation cascades）的预测性大规模原子模拟（predictive large-scale atomistic simulations）。

Abstract: Accurately modeling nonadiabatic electronic energy dissipation in atomistic simulations of radiation damage remains a key challenge. Existing models often rely on arbitrary thresholds and neglect trajectory-dependent, band-structure, and nonlinear effects. A model developed by Tamm \textit{et al.} (\href{https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevLett.120.185501}{doi:10.1103/PhysRevLett.120.185501}) offers a nonempirical description of electronic dissipation for atomistic simulations, but has not been directly benchmarked in the electronic stopping regime. We present the first such benchmark via the comparison of atomistic simulations with experimental ion transmission experiments. The model accurately captures the magnitude and trajectory-dependence of the predicted energy losses. These results support the reliability of the model for predictive large-scale atomistic simulations of radiation cascades.

</details>


### [115] [Material chemical compositon impacts on the band alignments: Preliminary results on the 3D/2D perovskite interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21654)
*Philippe Baranek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用基于杂化泛函（hybrid functionals）的原子级第一性原理模拟（first-principles simulation），研究 (PEA)2PbI4 二维钙钛矿与复杂三维钙钛矿 Cs0.125MA0.14FA0.735Pb(I0.87Br0.13)3 所形成 2D/3D 界面的结构与电子性质，以理解二维钝化层对电池性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合有机-无机卤化物钙钛矿太阳能电池（hybrid organic-inorganic halide perovskite solar cells）虽已取得显著认证效率，但其对光、热和湿度的不稳定性阻碍了工业化与社会化应用。在三维薄膜上沉积二维钙钛矿进行钝化是缓解湿度敏感性等问题的策略，但其对电池性能的具体影响尚需深入理解。

Method: 采用基于杂化泛函的原子级第一性原理模拟，研究 2D/3D 界面的结构与电子性质，聚焦 (PEA)2PbI4（PEA = C6H5CH2CH2NH3）与 Cs0.125MA0.14FA0.735Pb(I0.87Br0.13)3（MA = CH3NH3，FA = CH(NH2)2）形成的界面。方法基于优化的杂化泛函方案，一致地确定功函数（work function, WF）、电子亲和能（electron affinity）与带偏移（band offset, EBO）。

Result: 初步结果表明，功函数强烈依赖于表面的解理方式与化学性质；界面形成显著影响宏观电势，并出现与三维材料因离子取代而产生的极化相关的明显斜率。能带对齐（band alignment）对钙钛矿的终止方式与化学性质高度敏感，价带与导带带偏移分别约为 0.2–0.7 eV 与约 0.25 eV。

Conclusion: 该进行中的工作旨在系统描述上述效应，并进一步研究维度损失（dimensionality loss）对钙钛矿稳定性的影响。

Abstract: While hybrid organic-inorganic halide perovskite solar cells have achieved remarkable certified efficiencies, their widespread industrial and societal adoption is hindered by instabilities against light, heat, and moisture. A strategy to mitigate issues, such as moisture sensitivity, involves depositing 2D perovskites on 3D thin films for passivation, but their precise impact on cell performance requires detailed understanding. This work employs atomistic first-principles simulation, based on hybrid functionals, to investigate the structural and electronic properties of 2D/3D interfaces. Specifically, it focuses on interfaces formed by (PEA)2PbI4 (PEA = C6H5CH2CH2NH3, as 2D perovskite) and complex 3D perovskites (Cs0.125MA0.14FA0.735Pb(I0.87Br0.13)3, with MA = CH3NH3 and FA = CH(NH2)2) which are ones of the most used perovskites. The approach is based on optimized hybrid functional methods to consistently determine work function (WF), electron affinity (___), and band offset (EBO) across different layers. Preliminary results demonstrate that WF are strongly dependent on the cleavage and chemical nature of the surfaces. Furthermore, the creation of interfaces significantly impacts macroscopic potential, with a noticeable slope linked to the polarization of the 3D materials due to ionic substitutions. The band alignment is also highly sensitive to the termination and chemical nature of the perovskites, with valence and conduction EBOs varying from 0.2 to 0.7 eV and of approximately 0.25 eV, respectively. This ongoing work aims to provide a systematic description of these effects, with further investigation into the impact of dimensionality loss on perovskite stability.

</details>


### [116] [Adsorption of Phosgene Gas on Pristine and Noble Metal-Doped B12N12 Nanocages: Insights from Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21678)
*Shahariar Chowdhury,Mohammad Abdul Matin,Samiran Bhattacharjee,Ishtiaque M. Syed*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用色散校正密度泛函理论（DFT）研究贵金属（Ag、Au、Pd、Pt）掺杂 B12N12 纳米笼对光气（phosgene, COCl2）的吸附，表明 Pt 掺杂是实现可逆光气检测的最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估原始及贵金属掺杂 B12N12 纳米笼作为光气（COCl2）检测材料的潜力，明确掺杂对吸附亲和力与可逆性的影响。

Method: 使用色散校正密度泛函理论 [B3LYP-D3(BJ)]，采用硼位（B-site）取代（因其使 HOMO-LUMO 能隙收窄幅度更大，69-82%），对所有体系进行全优化；结合自然布居分析（natural population analysis）、QTAIM、振动频率分析、Grimme 准谐近似（quasi-harmonic approximation）与过渡态理论（transition-state theory）进行评估。

Result: 原始 B12N12 对光气吸附较弱（Eads = -12.8 至 -24.9 kJ mol^-1）；掺杂后增强至 -14.0 至 -60.7 kJ mol^-1（经 counterpoise 校正），Pt 亲和力与结构稳定性最高。光气至多转移 0.33 e，QTAIM 判定为闭壳层或中间型键临界点，属物理吸附（physisorption）。吸附熵罚（59-161 J mol^-1 K^-1）具决定性；在准谐近似下，原始笼在 298 K 不能自发吸附（Delta G = +23.8 至 +27.9 kJ mol^-1），仅 Pt-O（-8.8）与 Pd-O（-7.2 kJ mol^-1）可自发吸附。Pt-O 室温恢复时间 tau = 43 ms，热脱附温度为 69.6 摄氏度。

Conclusion: 银掺杂笼虽具窄的吸附后能隙与高亲电性，但无法在常温条件下保持光气；Pt 掺杂因兼具高亲和力、快速室温恢复与实用再生窗口，是 B12N12 纳米笼可逆光气检测的最有效策略。

Abstract: This study examines phosgene (COCl2) adsorption on pristine and noble metal-doped (Ag, Au, Pd, Pt) B12N12 nanocages using dispersion-corrected density functional theory [B3LYP-D3(BJ)]. Boron-site substitution narrows the HOMO-LUMO gap far more than nitrogen-site substitution (69-82% vs 41-67%) and was adopted throughout. All systems were fully optimized; multiple starting geometries converged to two stable minima per dopant, X-O and X-Cl. Pristine B12N12 binds phosgene weakly (Eads = -12.8 to -24.9 kJ mol^-1). Doping strengthens binding, spanning -14.0 to -60.7 kJ mol^-1 after counterpoise correction, with platinum exhibiting the highest affinity and structural stability. Natural population analysis indicates phosgene donates at most 0.33 e, and QTAIM classifies all cage-adsorbate bond critical points as closed-shell or intermediate, confirming physisorption. Vibrational frequency analysis confirms all structures as true minima and reveals that the adsorption entropy penalty (59-161 J mol^-1 K^-1) is decisive. Under Grimme's quasi-harmonic approximation, pristine cages fail to bind phosgene (Delta G = +23.8 to +27.9 kJ mol^-1), whereas only Pt-O (Delta G = -8.8 kJ mol^-1) and Pd-O (-7.2 kJ mol^-1) adsorb phosgene spontaneously at 298 K. Transition-state theory indicates rapid room-temperature recovery (tau = 43 ms for Pt-O), while thermodynamic desorption occurs at 69.6 deg C, ensuring a practical regeneration window. Silver-doped cages provide narrow post-adsorption gaps and high electrophilicity but cannot retain phosgene at ambient conditions. Platinum doping is therefore the most effective strategy for reversible phosgene detection using B12N12 nanocages.

</details>


### [117] [Complete Neural Electronic Initialization Accelerates Materials DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21759)
*Felix Ærtebjerg,Jonas Elsborg,Arghya Bhowmik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出首个完整的机器学习方法，用于加速投影缀加波（projector augmented wave, PAW）形式下的平面波密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）。作者形式化了“完整神经电子初始化器（Complete Neural Electronic Initializer）”的七项准则，并补足先前工作缺失的两个结构相关组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有加速 PAW DFT 的机器学习方法仅预测平滑价电子密度（smooth valence density），无法提供完整、无需参考的初始化。将其对照七项准则后发现缺失两个结构相关组件：缀加占据（augmentation occupancies）与自旋初始化（spin initialization）。

Method: 提出 AugNet——首个针对 PAW 缀加占据的通用等变模型（equivariant model），以及首个面向材料的通用自旋密度模型；后者预测平滑自旋差密度及自旋差 PAW 缀加占据，并利用预测磁矩约束全局磁态。与已有价电子密度模型结合，构成完全无需参考的电子初始化器。

Result: 受控消融实验（controlled ablations）表明，省略上述组件会消除甚至逆转仅预测平滑价电子密度所得模型的加速效果。所提方法在未见结构（unseen structures）上使端到端 DFT 墙钟时间（wall time）最多减少约 25%，同时保持能量收敛结果。

Conclusion: 满足全部七项准则的完整初始化器可实现无需收敛目标计算电子量的全端到端 PAW DFT 加速，在保持收敛能量的前提下显著降低计算时间。

Abstract: We present the first complete machine learning method for accelerating plane-wave density functional theory (DFT) in materials under the projector augmented wave (PAW) formalism. We formalize seven criteria that a \textit{Complete Neural Electronic Initializer} must satisfy for practical end-to-end PAW DFT acceleration. Applying these criteria to prior work reveals two missing structure-dependent components, augmentation occupancies and spin initialization, that prevent existing methods from providing complete reference-free initialization. Controlled ablations show that omitting these components can eliminate or reverse the acceleration obtained via models that only predict the smooth valence density. We satisfy these missing requirements by introducing AugNet, the first general equivariant model for PAW augmentation occupancies, and the first general spin density model for materials, which predicts the smooth spin-difference density and spin-difference PAW augmentation occupancies using predicted magnetic moments to constrain the global magnetic state. Combined with existing valence density models, these components satisfy all seven criteria and form a fully reference-free electronic initializer for materials DFT, requiring no electronic quantities from a converged target calculation. Our method reduces end-to-end DFT wall time by up to ~25% on unseen structures while preserving converged energies.

</details>


### [118] [Electrodeposited Co and Ni Hexacyanoferrates: Insights into Structure and Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21819)
*Larissa de O. Garcia,Michael Pohlitz,Mohammed F. Kalady,Falk Röder,Axel Lubk,Daniel Wolfmand Christian K. Müller*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过恒电位电沉积（potentiostatic electrodeposition）制备了含铁、钴、镍的六氰合铁酸盐（hexacyanoferrates, HCF）薄膜，系统表征其形貌、结构与电化学性质，揭示了金属取代对普鲁士蓝类似物（PBAs）薄膜性能的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普鲁士蓝（Prussian blue, PB）及其类似物（PBAs）因可调氧化还原化学（tunable redox chemistry）与开放骨架结构而在电化学应用中受到关注，但金属取代与沉积参数如何影响其结构与电化学性能仍需系统研究。

Method: 采用恒电位电沉积法制备 FeHCF、CoHCF 与 NiHCF 薄膜，并综合运用循环伏安法（cyclic voltammetry）、SEM、EDX、TEM、XRD、拉曼光谱（Raman）与 XPS 进行电化学、形貌与结构表征。

Result: 循环伏安显示不同样品具有各异氧化还原行为；FeHCF 与 CoHCF 呈均匀金字塔状薄膜生长，NiHCF 因残余应力（residual stress）呈开裂薄膜并伴有立方团簇，但所有样品元素分布均匀。TEM 与 XRD 确认均具立方晶格结构，且因原子半径减小出现由 Fe 到 Co 再到 Ni 的系统性晶格收缩。Raman 与 XPS 表明氧化态向 Fe2+ 主导偏移，CN 键合随 K+ 与水占据情况而变化。

Conclusion: 金属取代与沉积参数可用于调控 PBA 薄膜的结构与电化学性质，为设计定制化电极提供了一条策略性路径。

Abstract: Prussian blue (PB) and its analogues (PBAs) are interesting materials for electrochemical applications due to their tunable redox chemistry and open framework structure. In this study, hexacyanoferrates (HCF) containing iron (FeHCF), cobalt (CoHCF) and nickel (NiHCF) were synthesized via potentiostatic electrodeposition. Cyclic voltammetry revealed distinct redox behaviors. Morphological characterization (SEM, EDX) demonstrated uniform, pyramidal film growth for FeHCF and CoHCF. Otherwise, NiHCF presented a cracked film with cubic clusters on top due to residual stress. Despite this, homogeneous element distribution was found for all samples. Structural characterization (TEM and XRD) confirmed a cubic lattice crystal structure for all films, with systematic lattice contraction from Fe to Co to Ni due to decreasing atomic radius. Raman and XPS data revealed a shift toward Fe2+ dominant oxidation states and modifications in CN bonding with the influence of K+ and water occupancy in the PBAs framework. These findings illustrate how metal substitution and deposition parameters can tune the structural and electrochemical properties of PBA films, presenting a strategic route to design tailored electrodes.

</details>


### [119] [Multivalley 3D Electronic Structure of PbSe from Soft-X-Ray ARPES and First-Principles Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21824)
*Zefeng Cai,Valentine V. Volobuev,Jędrzej Korczak,Enrico Della Valle,Hantian Liu,Moritz Hoesch,Sergey M. Frolov,Tomasz Story,Vladimir N. Strocov,Noa Marom*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合软X射线角分辨光电子能谱（SX-ARPES）与第一性原理模拟研究块体PbSe的价带结构，结果显示HSE杂化泛函与准粒子自洽GW（QP$GW$）能准确重现价带色散，而半局域PBE泛函明显压缩带宽并失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PbSe作为窄带隙IV-VI族半导体，其多能谷价带（L、Σ、Δ点极大值）支撑了中温热电性能，因此准确描述其价带结构与带隙对理解及预测热电行为至关重要，而不同理论方法（DFT泛函与多体微扰理论）的可靠性尚需严格的实验检验。

Method: 采用软X射线角分辨光电子能谱（SX-ARPES）在400-900 eV光子能量下测量块体PbSe价带，沿XΓX、WXW、KΓK方向绘制价带，并在k_z=0平面收集等能面；同时开展密度泛函理论（DFT）计算（半局域PBE泛函与HSE杂化泛函）以及准粒子自洽GW（QP$GW$）多体微扰理论，与实验进行对比。

Result: HSE杂化泛函与QP$GW$在整个价带范围内重现测量色散，误差在0.1-0.2 eV以内；半局域PBE泛函则压缩带宽，与实验偏差最高达0.6 eV。此外，准确的能带结构与带隙是正确描述p型PbSe塞贝克系数（Seebeck coefficient）随空穴浓度变化关系（Pisarenko关系）的必要条件。

Conclusion: HSE与QP$GW$能提供PbSe价带的高精度描述，而PBE存在明显偏差；准确的能带与带隙对计算热电性质（如Pisarenko关系）具有关键影响，这对以计算手段发现热电材料的工作具有方法论启示。

Abstract: PbSe is a narrow-gap IV-VI semiconductor, whose multivalley valence bands, with maxima at the L, $Σ$, and $Δ$ points, underpin its intermediate-temperature thermoelectric properties. We combine soft-X-ray angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (SX-ARPES) with first principles simulations to study the valence band structure of bulk PbSe. High resolution measurements are conducted at photon energies of 400-900 eV to map the valence manifold along X$Γ$X, WXW, and K$Γ$K, and iso-energy surfaces are collected in the $k_z=0$ plane. Comparison to ARPES enables a rigorous assessment of the performance of density functional theory (DFT), using semi-local and hybrid functionals, as well as many-body perturbation theory within the quasiparticle self-consistent $GW$ approximation. We find that the Heyd-Scuseria-Ernzerhof (HSE) hybrid functional and QP$GW$ reproduce the measured band dispersions to within 0.1-0.2 eV over the entire valence band. In contrast, the semi-local Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) functional compresses the band width and deviates from experiment by up to 0.6 eV. We further show that an accurate band structure and band gap are vital to obtaining a correct description of the dependence of the Seebeck coefficient of p-type PbSe on the hole concentration (Pisarenko relation). This has implications for computational efforts to discover thermoelectric materials.

</details>


### [120] [PhonoMC: Occupation-based deviational Monte Carlo for phonon transport with temperature-dependent scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21884)
*Shixian Liu,Fei Yin,Gang Wang,Bin Liu,Ge Zhang,Alexander A. Barinov,Ke Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 PhonoMC，一种基于占据数（occupation-based）的偏离式蒙特卡洛（deviational Monte Carlo）方法，用于在弛豫时间近似（relaxation-time approximation）下求解声子玻尔兹曼输运方程（phonon Boltzmann transport equation），并实现纳米尺度自热（self-heating）模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度自热涉及跨受限几何、材料界面及温度场的声子输运，其中散射率（scattering rates）会随温度显著变化。已有方法难以兼顾稳定参考态、局域温度依赖散射率与持续热沉积的准确描述。

Method: 方法保留固定平衡态作为偏离参考（deviational reference），由所表征的能量重建局域温度以评估模式相关散射率；碰撞更新采用单独确定的弛豫温度（relaxation temperature）以在每个时间步内守恒能量；预设晶格加热通过改变载流子占据数而非持续增加计算粒子实现。

Result: 对 100 nm 硅薄膜的跨平面输运，偏离式表述在 ΔT=100 K、10^5 载流子下重现全布居热流并将标准差降低约 3.3 倍；而将散射率固定于 300 K 会在 ΔT=250 K 时高估热流 23.7%。Si 薄膜计算区分了有限长度效应与表面散射抑制，Si/3C-SiC 双层用于考察界面热阻（interfacial thermal resistance）；在类 FinFET 结构中，以高导热的 3C-SiC 取代下方 Si 衬底因受限 Si 区与 Si/SiC 界面附加阻力导致热点温度更高。

Conclusion: 结果表明，固定偏离参考可与局域温度依赖散射及持续热沉积结合，用于模式分辨的蒙特卡洛描述纳米尺度热输运。

Abstract: Nanoscale self-heating involves phonon transport across confined geometries, material interfaces, and temperature fields over which the scattering rates can vary substantially. We develop PhonoMC, an occupation-based deviational Monte Carlo method for solving the phonon Boltzmann transport equation within the relaxation-time approximation. A fixed equilibrium state is retained as the deviational reference, while the local temperature reconstructed from the represented energy is used to evaluate mode-dependent scattering rates. Collisions are updated with a separately determined relaxation temperature to conserve energy over each time step, and prescribed lattice heating is introduced by changing carrier occupations rather than continuously adding computational particles. For cross-plane transport through a 100-nm Si film, the deviational formulation reproduces the full-population heat flux while reducing its standard deviation by a factor of approximately 3.3 at \(ΔT=100\)~K with \(10^5\) carriers. In contrast, keeping the scattering rates fixed at 300~K overestimates the heat flux by 23.7\% at \(ΔT=250\)~K. Calculations of Si thin films distinguish finite-length effects from surface-scattering suppression, and Si/3C-SiC bilayers are used to examine interfacial thermal resistance. The method is further applied to localized heating in FinFET-like structures, where replacing the lower Si substrate with higher-conductivity 3C-SiC leads to a higher hotspot temperature because of the additional resistance associated with the confined Si region and the Si/SiC interface. These results show that a fixed deviational reference can be combined with local temperature-dependent scattering and sustained heat deposition in a mode-resolved Monte Carlo description of nanoscale thermal transport.

</details>


### [121] [Low resistance NiO/β-Ga_{2}O_{3} heterojunction diodes grown via molecular beam epitaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21901)
*Dagny Sacksteder,Anna Sacchi,Renae Gannon,Michelle Smeaton,Megan E. Holtz,Andriy Zakutayev,M. Brooks Tellekamp*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy, MBE）生长低缺陷、轻掺杂的 p 型 NiO 接触层，可在不降低薄膜质量的前提下降低 NiO/β-Ga₂O₃ 异质结二极管（heterojunction diode）的通态电阻（on-state resistance）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiO 是重要的 p 型氧化物接触材料，但现有生长方法易造成界面损伤并削弱器件性能；在 AlGaN、Ga₂O₃ 等超宽禁带（ultra wide band gap, UWBG）半导体异质结器件中，界面精细控制尤为关键。

Method: 使用分子束外延生长 β-Ga₂O₃ 二极管上的低缺陷、轻掺杂 NiO 接触层，并考察生长温度与生长速率对器件电学性能及薄膜质量的影响，同时以扫描透射电子显微镜（scanning transmission electron microscopy, STEM）表征界面结构。

Result: 高温生长未显著降低二极管通态电阻，而提高生长速率至 600 nm/hr 可降低 p⁻⁻NiO/β-Ga₂O₃ 异质结二极管的通态电阻且不损害薄膜质量；在 380 nm/hr、35 nm 厚 p⁻⁻NiO 条件下，未优化器件的平均比通态电阻（specific on-state resistance）为 1.46 Ω-cm²、理想因子（ideality factor）为 1.46，个别器件比通态电阻低至 25 mΩ-cm²、整流比（rectification ratio）为 2.7×10⁶；STEM 显示 (100) NiO/(100) β-Ga₂O₃ 界面共格且原子级陡峭。

Conclusion: 该工作为优化 NiO 界面、实现基于 β-Ga₂O₃ 及其他 UWBG 半导体的高性能 kV 级功率电子器件提供了新途径。

Abstract: NiO is one of the most important p-type oxide contact materials used in many semiconductor technologies. However, current NiO growth methods can induce interfacial damage that diminishes device performance. Fine control of interfaces is especially important in implementing NiO heterojunction diodes and transistors based on ultra wide band gap (UWBG) semiconductors such as AlGaN and Ga_{2}O_{3} used for power electronic applications. Here, we report on how molecular beam epitaxy can be used to achieve low-defect, lightly doped NiO contact layers for a β-Ga_{2}O_{3} diodes. Although high-temperature growth does not measurably decrease the on-state resistance of the diode, increased growth rates up to 600 nm/hr lower on-state resistance in p-- NiO / β-Ga_{2}O_{3} heterojunction diodes without reducing film quality. At a NiO growth rate of 380 nm/hr, unoptimized diodes with 35 nm thick p-- NiO layers demonstrate a device-average specific on-state resistance of 1.46 Ω-cm^{2} and an ideality factor of 1.46. Individual devices grown at this condition show specific on-state resistance as low as 25 mΩ-cm2 with a rectification ratio of 2.7x106. Scanning transmission electron microscopy imaging reveals the (100) NiO/ (100) β-Ga_{2}O_{3} interface is coherent and atomically abrupt. These results open a new avenue to optimizing the NiO interface to produce robust, competitive kV-class power electronic devices based on β-Ga_{2}O_{3} and other UWBG semiconductors.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Programmable Strain-Induced Intrinsic Circularly Polarized Emission from All-Inorganic Perovskite Nanocrystal Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21575)
*Yujie Jiao,Zhenqin Li,Yide Chang,Yongsen He,Xiaoyu Sun,Wei Lyu,Jiayu Ding,Zhaolin Zhong,Puxin Yang,Chi Chen,Bangjie Song,Min Qiu,Yanming Wang,Siying Peng*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作通过玻璃中直接激光写入（direct laser writing）制备全无机钙钛矿纳米晶（all-inorganic perovskite nanocrystals），实现无手性分子、可编程的圆偏振发光（CPL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半导体纳米晶实现CPL多依赖手性有机分子，阻碍了不同手性态在单一整块基底上的逐像素集成。

Method: 采用玻璃内直接激光写入合成全无机钙钛矿纳米晶；结合高分辨透射电子显微镜（HRTEM）、密度泛函理论（DFT）计算及功率依赖CPL测量，并系统调节入射线偏振角与聚焦深度。

Result: HRTEM揭示类核壳的晶面间距变化，与扭转晶格畸变（torsional lattice distortion）一致；DFT表明该畸变解除带边电子态的自旋简并；功率依赖测量显示从双折射介导的圆偏振向本征CPL的转变；可确定性编程CPL的手性与大小，|glum|约4×10^-3。

Conclusion: 通过直接激光写入实现本征手性光学功能的确定性调控，为整块材料中空间集成的手性发射体提供了策略。

Abstract: Generating circularly polarized luminescence (CPL) is important for chiral photonics and high-density optical information storage. Current approaches to CPL in semiconductor nanomaterials often employ chiral organic molecules, which hinder pixel-by-pixel integration of different chiral states onto a single monolithic substrate. Here, we realize chiral-molecule-free, programmable CPL from all-inorganic perovskite nanocrystals synthesized by direct laser writing in glass. High-resolution transmission electron microscopy reveals a core-shell-like variation in interplanar spacing, consistent with torsional lattice distortion. Density functional theory calculations further show that torsional lattice distortion lifts the spin degeneracy of the band-edge electronic states. Power-dependent CPL measurements reveal a transition from birefringence-mediated circular polarization to intrinsic CPL. Furthermore, by systematically tuning the incident linear polarization angle and focal depth, we deterministically program both the handedness and magnitude of the CPL, with |glum| of approximately 4 x 10^-3. By demonstrating deterministic control of intrinsic chiroptical functionality through direct laser writing, our work establishes a strategy for spatially integrated chiral emitters in monolithic materials.

</details>


### [123] [Symmetry Properties of Bound States in the Continuum in Hexagonal Lattice Photonic Crystal Slabs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21727)
*Rhys Jones,Sang Soon Oh*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文采用群论（group theory）方法分析 $C_{6v}$ 空间群光子晶体（photonic crystal, PhC）腔中连续域束缚态（bound states in the continuum, BICs）的对称性，确定各不可约表示（irreducible representations, irreps）的基函数及电场空间分布，并借助 $k\cdot p$ 微扰理论揭示其远场偏振与拓扑荷（topological charge）特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BICs 在光子晶体腔中实现对光的完全局域化，其应用前景广阔；但对其基础物理的理解仍需深化，以指导未来器件设计。本文旨在从群论角度建立对 BICs 的基本认识。

Method: 采用群论方法确定 $C_{6v}$ 空间群对称性下光子晶体各不可约表示的基函数，据此推导腔内电磁场的空间分布；并结合电场反射宇称与 $k\cdot p$ 微扰理论（perturbation theory）确定各不可约表示的远场偏振。

Result: $k\cdot p$ 微扰解释了 $E_2$ 不可约表示具有 $-2$ 拓扑荷的原因，同时表明拓扑荷并不必然意味着 BIC 的存在。此外发现模式的品质因子（quality factor）并不一定满足 $Q \propto 1/|k|^{2|q|}$ 的标度关系，而是取决于 $k\cdot p$ 微扰中与辐射模式的耦合，该耦合由各不可约表示的对称性决定。

Conclusion: 群论结合 $k\cdot p$ 微扰的方法可系统性刻画光子晶体腔中 BICs 的对称性、远场偏振、拓扑荷与品质因子，为相关器件设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) within photonic crystal (PhC) cavities realise the full confinement of light despite lying within the continuum of radiation modes. While their diverse practical applications has attracted considerable research interest, developing the fundamental understanding of BICs is important for the design of future devices. This paper uses a group theoretical approach to determine the basis functions of the irreducible representations (irreps) of a PhC with the symmetry of the $C_{6v}$ space group, from which the spatial distribution of the electromagnetic fields within the cavity can be determined. Furthermore, the reflection parity of the electric field and a $k \cdot p$ perturbation theory approach are used to determine the far-field polarisation of each irrep. The $k \cdot p$ perturbation explains why the $E_2$ irrep has a topological charge of $-2$, as well as showing that a topological charge does not necessarily indicate the presence of a BIC. Additionally, it is found that the quality factor of the modes do not necessarily scale as $Q \propto 1/|k|^{2|q|}$, but are instead dependent on the coupling to the radiative mode in the $k \cdot p$ perturbation, which is determined from the symmetry of each irrep.

</details>


### [124] [Quantized Transport of Gap Solitons in the Harper-Hofstadter Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21917)
*Oliver Ashfield,Henry Davenport,Tom Sheppard,David G. Reid,Holly A. J. Middleton-Spencer,A. C. Berceanu,Frank Schindler,Iacopo Carusotto,Hannah M. Price*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在具有立方非线性（cubic nonlinearity）的 Harper-Hofstadter 模型中，带隙深处（deep in the band gap）的某些孤子在外力作用下以稳健的量子化速度（quantized velocity）横向输运，且该速度与外力成正比，可实现任意调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注非线性孤子在受力量子体系中的可控输运问题，选取弱相互作用冷原子或光学克尔介质（Kerr nonlinearity）可实现的 Harper-Hofstadter 模型作为研究平台。

Method: 在 Harper-Hofstadter 模型中加入立方非线性项，数值研究位于带隙深处的孤子在外力下的动力学行为，并与带边（band edge）孤子进行对比。

Result: 带隙深处的孤子在整个演化过程中保持空间轮廓，以垂直于外力方向的量子化速度运动，该速度与外力线性相关；而带边孤子在同一外力下发生形变并退局域化（delocalize）。该量子化输运效应在布洛赫态（Bloch states）占据高度非均匀的条件下依然存在。

Conclusion: 该机制为在实验可及的平台上导向非线性孤子提供了一种稳健且高度可控的途径。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of gap solitons found in the Harper-Hofstadter model with an additional cubic nonlinearity, such as that describing weakly-interacting cold atoms or a Kerr nonlinearity in an optical platform. We find that certain solitons with frequencies deep in the band gap travel with a robust, quantized velocity perpendicular to the applied force and maintain their spatial profile over long timescales. Crucially, this velocity is linearly proportional to the applied force, allowing for arbitrary steering of the nonlinear wavepacket, without breakdown, using only a tunable external force. This quantized transport persists despite a highly non-uniform occupation of the linear Bloch states, and contrasts with solitons near the band edge, which deform and delocalize under the same force. These results point to a robust and highly controllable mechanism for steering nonlinear solitons in experimentally accessible platforms.

</details>

### [125] [The effect of rolling friction on the rheology and kinematics of shear-thickening dense suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21066)
*Naveen Kumar Agrawal,Michel Orsi,Martin Trulsson,Luca Brandt*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过二维应力控制离散元模拟（discrete-element simulations）表明，滚动摩擦（rolling friction）使稠密悬浮液（dense suspensions）的连续剪切增稠（continuous shear thickening）转变为非连续剪切增稠（discontinuous shear thickening），其流变效应主要源于堵塞体积分数（jamming packing fraction）的移动；但以 Δφ=φ-φ_m(σ) 归一后流变曲线近似重合，而粒子运动学（kinematics）却保留了滚动约束的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动摩擦（sliding friction）降低稠密悬浮液的堵塞体积分数并在应力激活下驱动非惯性剪切增稠，而附加滚动阻力会进一步降低堵塞体积分数，可表征颗粒粗糙度或棱角度效应。然而，滚动摩擦在趋近堵塞（approach to jamming）过程中如何改变粒子运动仍不清楚，这是本文要解决的问题。

Method: 采用应力控制（stress-controlled）的二维离散元模拟（two-dimensional discrete-element simulations），对比仅含滑动摩擦的悬浮液与具有均匀（uniform）或表面变化（surface-varying）滚动摩擦的系统，并在同一 Δφ=φ-φ_m(σ) 条件下比较高应力增稠态（high-stress thickened state）的粒子运动学。

Result: 固定体积分数下，加入滚动摩擦使连续剪切增稠转为非连续剪切增稠；在同一 Δφ 下流曲线近乎重合，说明流变效应主要来自 φ_m 的移动。平移速度关联（translational velocity correlations）延伸至数个颗粒直径，而转动关联（rotational correlations）保持局域。滚动摩擦促使接触处同向旋转（co-rotation）取代强反向旋转（counter-rotation），并抑制转动相对于平移的涨落；但接近堵塞时转动在各类情形中均愈发重要。表面变化滚动摩擦的悬浮液行为相当于 μ_r≈0.3 的均匀系统，因接触处采样的摩擦系数远低于表面平均值（此处 μ_r≈0.5）。

Conclusion: Δφ 在很大程度上组织（organize）剪切增稠的流变行为，但不能组织粒子运动学；后者保留了滚动约束（rolling constraint）的独特印记，因此在用滚动摩擦模拟粗糙或棱角颗粒时须加以考虑。

Abstract: Sliding friction lowers the jamming packing fraction of dense suspensions and, when activated by stress, drives non-inertial shear thickening. Additional rolling resistance lowers the jamming packing fraction further and can represent effects of particle roughness or angularity. How it changes particle motion on the approach to jamming remains unclear. Using stress-controlled two-dimensional discrete-element simulations, we compare a sliding-only suspension with systems having either uniform or surface-varying rolling friction. At fixed packing fraction, adding rolling friction changes continuous shear thickening into discontinuous shear thickening. At the same distance from the stress-dependent jamming point, $Δφ=φ-φ_m(σ)$, however, the flow curves nearly collapse, showing that the rheological effect arises largely from the shift in $φ_m$. Guided by this collapse, we compare particle kinematics in the high-stress thickened state at matched $Δφ$, revealing differences hidden by the similar bulk response. Translational velocity correlations extend over several particle diameters, whereas rotational correlations remain local. Rolling friction promotes co-rotation at contact in place of strong counter-rotation and suppresses rotational relative to translational fluctuations. Rotation nevertheless becomes increasingly important near jamming in every case. The suspension with surface-varying rolling friction follows the behavior of a uniform system with $μ_r\approx0.3$ because the coefficients sampled at contacts lie well below the surface average value, here $μ_r\approx0.5$. Thus, $Δφ$ largely organizes the shear-thickening rheology, but not the particle kinematics. These retain a distinct signature of the rolling constraint that must be considered when rolling friction is used to model rough or angular particles.

</details>


### [126] [Simulation and Network Assembly Pipelines for Dynamically Bonded Soft Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21204)
*Tanner A. Wilcoxson,Tianhao Li,Tyla R. Holoman,Gaurav Mitra,Thomas M. Truskett,Glen M. Hocky*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文介绍 pySNAP，一个模块化开源 Python 平台，将可调控动态键合（dynamic bonding）与仿真工作流、模板和分析设置集成于统一框架，基于 GPU 加速的 HOOMD-blue 引擎与 DyBond 插件，支持跨工作站与高性能计算调度器的参数扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软材料（soft materials）由可逆共价键或超分子键连接，其微观键合动力学（bonding kinetics）与组分力学如何导致宏观性质仍难以测量。粗粒化分子动力学（coarse-grained MD）可桥接尺度，但多数方法无法控制单个键的动力学，已有的方法多为专用实现，难以通用化。

Method: 提出 pySNAP（Simulation and Network Assembly Pipelines）：在 GPU 加速的 HOOMD-blue MD 引擎上构建，集成 GPU 加速插件 DyBond（按平衡分布成键与断键，支持多类型配对物种）；snap_simulate 包将参数文件目录编译为 HOOMD-blue 仿真，snap_workflow 包在工作站与高性能计算调度器上编排参数扫描。

Result: 在多种动态键合系统上验证该框架，表明其在统一工作流内可容纳不同的相互作用机制、几何构型与物理场景，并支持既有模型的复现以及更复杂复合系统的快速构建。

Conclusion: pySNAP 以模块化、开源方式整合可调控动态键合与仿真工作流，用户仅需对输入文件做小幅修改即可研究广泛系统，并绕开设定参数扫描直至结果分析的完整流程。

Abstract: Soft materials linked by reversible covalent or supramolecular bonds form a diverse class of assemblies with promising applications from nanoscience to medicine. Experiments typically probe bulk phase behavior and rheology, but it remains difficult to measure how microscopic bonding kinetics and the mechanics of the constituent elements give rise to bulk properties. Coarse-grained molecular dynamics (MD) simulations can bridge these scales, but most simulation approaches do not control individual bond kinetics, and those that do were mostly developed for bespoke applications that do not readily generalize. Here we present pySNAP (Simulation and Network Assembly Pipelines), a modular open source Python platform that integrates tunable dynamic bonding with a workflow, template, and analysis setup, so that users can study a wide range of systems with only small changes to input files. The platform is built on the GPU-accelerated HOOMD-blue MD engine and integrates DyBond, a GPU-accelerated plugin that forms and breaks bonds consistent with an equilibrium distribution and supports bonding between multiple types of partner species. Around this core, the snap_simulate package compiles a directory of parameter files into a HOOMD-blue simulation, and the snap_workflow package orchestrates the resulting parameter sweeps across workstations and high-performance computing schedulers. We describe the theory behind simulated dynamic bonding and how to use the package, from setting up a parameter sweep to analyzing its results, and demonstrate the framework on a diverse range of dynamically bonded systems, showing that it accommodates distinct interaction mechanisms, geometries, and physical scenarios within a unified workflow, while enabling both reproduction of existing models and rapid construction of more complex composite systems.

</details>


### [127] [Granular thermostat implementation within the soft-sphere Discrete Element Method (DEM) framework, considerations and limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21297)
*Marco Previtali,Herbert Eric Huppert,Sergio Andres Galindo Torres*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 针对耗散离散元（DEM）系统缺乏可控温度调控方法的问题，提出两种混合型温控算法（thermostat），兼顾确定性温度调节与随机去相关，并在压力控制简单剪切中验证其物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集颗粒流常以软球离散元法（soft-sphere Discrete Element Method, DEM）研究，但大尺度应用需连续介质模型；颗粒温度（granular temperature）可用于坍缩流变标度，然而实验性强迫手段在调控温度的同时会改变颗粒状态的其他方面，因此需要直接控制涨落能量的替代方法。传统温控算法（thermostat algorithms）在分子动力学（Molecular Dynamics, MD）中成熟，其在耗散型 DEM 系统中的行为却研究不足。

Method: 论文分析常规 Langevin 与 Nosé-Hoover 温控器在耗散 DEM 系统中的表现，指出二者存在互补性局限；随后提出两种成对（pairwise）混合温控形式，将确定性温度调节与随机去相关（stochastic decorrelation）机制结合，并通过改变随机去相关时间尺度考察速度统计量与细观性质的变化；最后将其应用于压力控制（pressure-controlled）简单剪切。

Result: Langevin 控制需强耦合以抵消碰撞耗散，因而阻尼颗粒动力学；Nosé-Hoover 则无法独立打断反复非弹性碰撞产生的关联与偏析（segregation），导致非各态历经（non-ergodic）与数值不稳定状态。两种混合形式均能强制实现设定温度，且随机去相关时间尺度的变化可改变速度统计量及扩散率（diffusivity）等细观性质。压力控制简单剪切中，在固定惯性数（inertial number）下提高颗粒温度会降低表观摩擦，与既有趋势一致。

Conclusion: 该框架为比较不同强迫方式的颗粒系统提供了受控参考态（reference states），并有助于识别构建温度相关本构模型（constitutive models）所需的变量。

Abstract: Dense granular flows are commonly investigated using the soft-sphere Discrete Element Method (DEM), whereas large-scale applications generally require continuum models. Granular temperature, defined as the variance of particle velocity fluctuations, is a relevant variable to collapse rheological scaling. However, experimental forcing approaches used to control temperature also affects other aspects of the granular state.
  An alternative is to use a thermostat algorithm to control the fluctuation energy directly. Although thermostat algorithms are well established in Molecular Dynamics (MD), their behaviour in dissipative DEM systems has received comparatively little attention. We show that conventional Langevin and Nosé-Hoover thermostats exhibit complementary limitations: Langevin control requires strong coupling to offset collisional dissipation, thereby damping particle dynamics, whereas Nosé-Hoover does not independently disrupt the correlations and segregation generated by repeated inelastic collisions, leading to non-ergodic and numerically unstable states.
  To address these limitations, we introduce two pairwise hybrid formulations combining deterministic temperature regulation with stochastic decorrelation. Both enforce the prescribed temperature, while variation of the stochastic decorrelation timescale modifies velocity statistics and associated mesoscopic properties such as diffusivity. Finally, application to pressure-controlled simple shear shows that increasing granular temperature at fixed inertial number reduces the apparent friction, consistent with previously reported trends. The framework therefore provides controlled reference states for comparing differently forced granular systems and identifying the variables required for temperature-dependent constitutive models.

</details>


### [128] [Granular structure and heterogeneous deformation preceding avalanches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21531)
*Ibrahim Awada,Michel Bornert,Vincent Langlois,Julien Léopoldès*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过倾斜颗粒层中的颗粒尺度追踪（grain-scale tracking），揭示雪崩前兆（precursors）的活动数量与复发频率取决于制备形成的各向异性结构与加载方向之间的相容性，而位移的指数衰减与局部应变涨落的二次标度等空间特征则与制备协议无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒堆积体在雪崩发生前会出现间歇性重排（即前兆），但其前兆活动与堆积结构、加载条件之间的依赖关系，以及哪些特征属于普适行为，尚不明确。

Method: 采用倾斜颗粒层实验，进行颗粒尺度追踪，比较不同制备协议下前兆的数量与复发情况，并测量迁移前沿（mobility front）的传播；同时通过集合平均位移、局部剪切应变方差及双态模型（two-state model）分析应变分布。

Result: 前兆数量与复发随“制备继承的各向异性结构与后续加载方向的相容性”而变化；前兆频繁时迁移前沿呈小幅渐进推进，前兆稀少时则扩展更大。而集合平均位移随深度呈指数衰减（约四个颗粒直径尺度），局部剪切应变方差与平均应变呈共同的二次标度（quadratic scaling）；双态模型表明局部应变非均匀性影响约四分之一的基元单元。

Conclusion: 研究将依赖制备条件的前兆活动与雪崩前变形的稳健空间特征区分开来，说明前者受结构与加载相容性调控，后者在制备协议间保持不变。

Abstract: Granular packings undergo intermittent rearrangements, known as precursors, before avalanche onset. Using grain-scale tracking in an inclined granular layer, we show experimentally that the number and recurrence of precursors change with the compatibility between the anisotropic structure inherited from preparation and the subsequent loading direction. Preparation also modifies the propagation of the mobility front, defined as the region in which the packing undergo measurable displacement: frequent precursors produce small progressive advances, whereas rare precursors involve larger extensions. By contrast, several features remain unchanged across protocols. The ensemble-averaged displacement always decays exponentially with depth over approximately four grain diameters, while the local shear-strain variance follows a common quadratic scaling with the mean strain. A two-state model suggests that, regardless of the packing structure, local strain heterogeneities affect approximately one quarter of the elementary cells, superimposed on the exponential strain profile. The reported results separate preparation-dependent precursor activity from the robust spatial features of the deformation preceding avalanches.

</details>


### [129] [Phase Separation in a system of Brownian inertial rotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21556)
*Lucio Mauro Carenza,Pasquale Digregorio,Massimiliano Semeraro,Antonio Suma,Ignacio Pagonabarraga,Giuseppe Gonnella*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文揭示了一种由手性对称性破缺驱动的惯性哑铃系统的涌现相分离（emergent phase separation），其机制由惯性主导，并伴随团簇的自发刚性旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于探究恒定扭矩驱动的惯性哑铃（inertial dumbbells）在排斥硬核相互作用下，手性对称性破缺（chiral symmetry breaking）如何引发涌现的相分离现象。

Method: 方法为数值求解该模型的相图，识别双节线（binodal）与旋节线（spinodal）区域，并通过系统压力（pressure）分析揭示相分离机制。

Result: 结果显示系统分离为稠密液体（dense liquid）与一种被识别为六角相（hexatic）的有序相；压力分析表明相分离机制主要受惯性（inertia）控制，局部注入的动能随密度依赖地稳定储存在系统中，在大惯性下形成热稀薄、冷稠密的状态，导致均匀相普遍不稳定。

Conclusion: 结论是哑铃的团簇排布（arrangement into clusters）与手性力（chiral forces）的相互作用，产生团簇绕其质心的自发刚性旋转（spontaneous rigid rotation）。

Abstract: We uncover an emergent phase separation in a system of inertial dumbbells put into persistent rotation by a constant applied torque, breaking chiral symmetry, while interacting with each other through repulsive hard-core interactions. We numerically determine the phase diagram of the model, showing that there exists a binodal and a spinodal region where the system separates into a dense liquid and an ordered phase, which we identify as hexatic. An analysis of the system pressure unveils that the mechanism underlying the phase separation is heavily controlled by inertia. Locally injected kinetic energy is steadily stored into the system in a way that depends on density and that, at large inertia, results in a hot dilute and cool dense state, leading to a generically unstable homogeneous phase. The interplay between dumbbells' arrangement into clusters and chiral forces produces a spontaneous rigid rotation of the clusters around their centers of mass.

</details>


### [130] [Tunable parametric vibrations in fluid ferroelectric fibres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21582)
*Alexander Dieter-Jarosik,Hajnalka Nádasi,Rachel Tuffin,Melanie Klasen-Memmer,Alexey Eremin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作研究自由悬浮的铁电向列相纤维（ferroelectric-nematic fibres）在交变电场下的动力学行为，揭示其从亚阈值脉动到极性依赖轴向流动直至持续横向振荡的序列态，并以参数不稳定性与Floquet模型加以解释，将其确立为电可编程的流体谐振器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由悬浮的铁电向列相纤维同时具备一维流动性（1D fluidity）与宏观自发极化（spontaneous polarisation），为研究极性序与界面流动的耦合提供了独特体系。其在外场驱动下的动力学态序列及内在机制尚待阐明。

Method: 在交变电场（alternating electric fields）下观测纤维的动力学态；将振荡态归因于液弦（liquid string）的参数不稳定性（parametric instability），其有效张力（effective tension）主要由电极化-场耦合贡献主导；构建引入有限时间极化翻转（finite-time polarisation switching）的Floquet模型进行理论描述。

Result: 观测到从亚阈值脉动（subthreshold pulsations）、极性依赖轴向流动（polarity-dependent axial flow）到持续横向振荡（sustained transverse oscillations）的动力学序列；Floquet模型再现了共振舌（resonance tongues）、模态选择（modal selection）与方波响应（square-wave response）。

Conclusion: 结果确立铁电向列相纤维为电可编程的流体谐振器（electrically programmable fluid resonators），实现极性序、界面流动与非线性振动（nonlinear vibration）的耦合。

Abstract: Freely suspended ferroelectric-nematic fibres combine 1D fluidity with macroscopic spontaneous polarisation. Under alternating electric fields, we observe a sequence of dynamical states, from subthreshold pulsations to polarity-dependent axial flow, and sustained transverse oscillations. We show that the oscillatory state arises from a parametric instability of a liquid string whose effective tension is dominated by the electrical polarisation-field coupling contribution. A Floquet model incorporating finite-time polarisation switching captures the observed resonance tongues, modal selection, and square-wave response. These results establish ferroelectric-nematic fibres as electrically programmable fluid resonators coupling polar order and interfacial flow with nonlinear vibration.

</details>


### [131] [Active colloidal rafts perform swimming strokes that propel their host droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21963)
*Airi N. Kato,Julien Brémont,Etienne Harté,Jean-Michel Rampnoux,Jean-François Joanny,Hamid Kellay*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种与直接流体力学驱动不同的液滴运动机制：受限在薄油滴中的活性胶体筏（raft）沿偏心轨道运动，周期性各向异性地使液滴接触线变形，这种非互易变形充当游泳划水动作（swimming stroke），依据低雷诺数游泳（low-Reynolds-number swimming）原理推动液滴运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性粒子受限液滴可使其运动，通常被归因于被包裹粒子的直接流体力学驱动（direct hydrodynamic forcing）。本文旨在揭示一种不同的机制，说明集体内部活性如何转化为运动。

Method: 作者采用一个耦合黏性流体力学（viscous hydrodynamics）与多体活性动力学（many-body active dynamics）的动力学模型（dynamical model）来描述该现象。

Result: 受限于水面薄油滴中的自驱动活性胶体筏通常沿偏心轨道（off-centered orbit）运动，周期性各向异性地使液滴接触线（contact line）变形；该效应在单个胶体情况下不存在，源于筏本质上多体（many-body）的动力学行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，集体的内部活性（collective internal activity）可通过软受限边界（soft confining boundary）的变形被转化为整体运动（locomotion）。

Abstract: Active particles confined in droplets can set their host droplet into motion. This phenomenon is usually attributed to direct hydrodynamic forcing by the enclosed particles. Here we reveal a different mechanism: we show that a self-propelled raft of active colloids confined in a thin oil droplet at the surface of water typically follows an off-centered orbit, thus periodically deforming the droplet's contact line in an anisotropic way. This non-reciprocal deformation acts as a swimming stroke and propels the droplet according to the principles of low-Reynolds-number swimming. The effect is absent for a single colloid and arises from the intrinsically many-body dynamics of the raft, which we capture with a dynamical model coupling viscous hydrodynamics and many-body active dynamics. Our results show how collective internal activity can be converted into locomotion through deformation of a soft confining boundary.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Critical and near-critical influence bounds for ferromagnetic Ising models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20866)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了最大度数 $Δ\ge3$ 图上铁磁 Ising 模型在树唯一性阈值处，影响矩阵每行的量级不超过 $\sqrt n$，并将该估计推广至一般有界耦合情形。作为推论，零场单点 Glauber 动力学在整个超临界窗口内以多项式时间混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于铁磁情形的一般图界（general graphical bounds）含有对数因子，估计不够紧；本文旨在去除该对数项并给出对度数、外场与钉扎（pinning）一致的影响矩阵行界。

Method: 证明结合了逐点空腔界（pointwise cavity bound）、正级数磁化倾斜（positive-series magnetization tilt），以及 Ding、Song 与 Sun 的场比较定理（field comparison theorem）。一般情形下若耦合以 $β$ 为界，则界为 $C(\sqrt n+n\varepsilon)$，其中 $\varepsilon=((Δ-1)\tanhβ-1)_+$。

Result: 在树唯一性阈值处得到影响矩阵每行 $O(\sqrt n)$ 的界，该估计对最大度数、外场及所有钉扎一致；更一般地得到 $C(\sqrt n+n\varepsilon)$ 的界，临界估计去除了近期铁磁情形一般图界中的对数项。

Conclusion: 由影响矩阵界可推出零场单点 Glauber 动力学在整个超临界窗口 $\varepsilon=O(\sqrt{\log n/n})$ 上以多项式时间混合（mixing），且多项式次数取决于窗口大小。

Abstract: For a ferromagnetic Ising model on a graph of maximum degree $Δ\ge3$, we prove a bound of order $\sqrt n$ on every row of the influence matrix at the tree uniqueness threshold. The estimate is uniform in the degree, the external fields, and all pinnings. More generally, if the couplings are bounded by $β$ and $\varepsilon=((Δ-1)\tanhβ-1)_+$, the bound is $C(\sqrt n+n\varepsilon)$. The proof combines a pointwise cavity bound with a positive-series magnetization tilt and the field comparison theorem of Ding, Song and Sun. The critical estimate removes the logarithm in recent general graphical bounds for the ferromagnetic case. As a consequence, zero-field single-site Glauber dynamics mixes in polynomial time throughout the supercritical window $\varepsilon=O(\sqrt{\log n/n})$, with the polynomial degree depending on the window size.

</details>


### [133] [A Functorial Theory of Defects in Abelian Chern-Simons Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21414)
*Daniel Galviz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 理论实现为范畴化 TQFT，引入范畴缺陷并构造其缺陷扩张，同时以有限二次模刻画体理论及其对称性，并指出近期提出的阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 对偶并非真正的 TQFT 对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作将阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 理论构造为范畴拓扑量子场论（categorical TQFT），从而可自然地纳入范畴缺陷（categorical defects）并构造其缺陷扩张。本文在此基础上厘清体实现与双重实现之别，并检验相关对偶的合法性。

Method: 先识别阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 理论在 center 与 doubled pointed modular 情形下的 Turaev-Viro 实现，区分单体实现与典范双重实现；再用 Alterfold 构造给出拓扑边界、畴壁（domain walls）与凝聚扇区，建立 Alterfold/Chern-Simons 对应词典；并以有限二次模（finite quadratic module）作为控制不变量。

Result: 有限二次模被证明是控制体理论、其拓扑对称性、定向反演不变性（orientation-reversal invariance）与缺陷的不变量；多分量阿贝尔 BF 理论（multicomponent Abelian BF theory）作为非对角阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 理论的扩张 TQFT 出现；近期提出的阿贝尔 Chern-Simons 对偶不构成真正的 TQFT 对偶。

Conclusion: 结果为阿贝尔拓扑序（Abelian topological orders）中的缺陷提供了具体模型，并提示了通向非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）情形的路径。

Abstract: Recent work has constructed Abelian Chern-Simons theories as categorical TQFTs, allowing us to naturally incorporate categorical defects and construct defect extensions of Abelian Chern-Simons TQFTs. We first identify the Turaev-Viro realizations of Abelian Chern-Simons theory in the center and doubled pointed modular cases, clarifying the distinction between single bulk realizations and canonical doubled ones. Alternatively, the Alterfold construction supplies the associated topological boundaries, domain walls, and condensation sectors, establishing an explicit Alterfold/Chern-Simons dictionary. We show that the finite quadratic module is the invariant controlling the bulk theory, its topological symmetries, orientation-reversal invariance, and defects. We further show that multicomponent Abelian BF theory arises as the extended TQFT of an off-diagonal Abelian Chern-Simons theory, placing it naturally within the same extended framework. Finally, we demonstrate that recently proposed Abelian Chern-Simons dualities do not define a genuine TQFT duality. These results provide a concrete model for defects in Abelian topological orders and suggest a route toward the non-Abelian case.

</details>


### [134] [Gradient nature of Laplacian growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21430)
*A. Zabrodin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对平面上一类拉普拉斯型（Laplacian type）生长过程，提出将其解释为光滑闭曲线空间中的“梯度下降”（gradient descent），证明生长区域边界的运动沿某泛函的梯度方向进行；在最简单情形下该泛函为 log(1/r)，其中 r 为生长区域的外部共形半径（external conformal radius）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为平面上一类拉普拉斯型生长过程提供统一的几何变分解释，即把边界演化理解为曲线空间中的梯度流。该解释试图以泛函梯度的框架刻画此类生长现象。

Method: 方法上，将生长区域边界视为光滑闭曲线，并在曲线空间（space of curves）中引入某泛函，进而证明边界随时间的运动方向与该泛函的梯度一致。分析聚焦于平面情形下最简单的泛函形式 log(1/r)。

Result: 研究显示，生长区域（growing domain）的边界确实沿曲线空间中某泛函的梯度方向移动。在最简单的情形中，该泛函可取为 log(1/r)，其中 r 表示生长区域的外部共形半径。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：这类拉普拉斯型平面生长过程可被自然地视为光滑闭曲线空间中的梯度下降过程。该视角将生长动力学与泛函梯度（尤其与外部共形半径相关的对数泛函）联系起来。

Abstract: For a class of growth processes of Laplacian type in the plane, we suggest an interpretation as a ``gradient descent'' in the space of smooth closed curves. More precisely, we show that boundary of a growing domain moves along a gradient of a certain functional in the space of curves. In the simplest cases this functional is $\log (1/r)$, where $r$ is the external conformal radius of the growing domain.

</details>


### [135] [Entanglement-Inducing Quantum Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21438)
*J. Fransson,A. P. Sowa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出描述格点阵列中相互作用玻色子（bosons）系统的新模型，其核心为被命名为薛定谔-狄利克雷方程（Schrödinger-Dirichlet equation）的非线性、非局域演化方程，与玻色-哈伯德模型（Bose-Hubbard model）及一类广义玻色子密切相关，并可产生纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统平均场闭合（mean-field closures）方法在描述相互作用玻色子系统时的局限，作者希望构建一个更一般的非线性动力学框架，使演化不再必然保持乘积结构（product structure）。

Method: 提出非线性、非局域的薛定谔-狄利克雷方程作为模型核心；构造上与玻色-哈伯德模型及特定类型的广义玻色子相关；分析所依赖的方法基于正有理数乘法群（multiplicative group of positive rationals）上的调和分析（harmonic analysis）。

Result: 所得到的非线性动力学不必保持乘积结构，且能够从初始可分离态（separable states）生成纠缠（entanglement），这是其区别于传统平均场闭合的关键特征。摘要未给出定量结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个以薛定谔-狄利克雷方程为核心的新模型，为格点相互作用玻色子系统的非线性非局域描述提供了框架，并指出正有理数乘法群上的调和分析是相关分析工具。摘要未涉及具体结论或未来工作。

Abstract: We introduce a new model for a system of interacting bosons placed in an array of sites. At its core is a nonlinear, nonlocal evolution equation, which we have dubbed the Schrödinger-Dirichlet equation. The construction is closely related to the Bose-Hubbard model and to a specific type of generalized bosons. In contrast to conventional mean-field closures, the resulting nonlinear dynamics need not preserve product structure and can generate entanglement from initially separable states. The relevant methods of analysis are based on harmonic analysis for the multiplicative group of positive rationals.

</details>


### [136] [Generalized Hamiltonian formalism for spatially nonlocal nonlinear differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22066)
*Ali Pazarci,Nadir Ghazanfari,Ilmar Gahramanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为拉格朗日密度同时依赖于局域场及其空间反射对应量的空间非局域场论，建立了一套广义哈密顿（Hamiltonian）形式化框架，并将其应用于三类空间非局域非线性薛定谔（nonlinear Schrödinger）方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间非局域场论中拉格朗日密度显式依赖空间反射场，现有哈密顿形式化处理不足，需要一个能一致纳入反射场贡献的统一框架。

Method: 从广义变分原理（generalized variational principle）出发，推导广义欧拉-拉格朗日方程（generalized Euler-Lagrange equations），并引入可一致处理反射场贡献的广义泛函导数（generalized functional derivative）。

Result: 针对 Ablowitz-Musslimani 方程，直接以复场构造出首个标准拉格朗日密度并给出完整哈密顿形式；框架进一步应用于 Velasco-Juan 与 Fujioka 提出的两个拉格朗日非局域非线性薛定谔方程，得到与广义欧拉-拉格朗日方程一致的哈密顿形式。

Conclusion: 结果为一大类空间非局域非线性场论确立了统一的哈密顿框架。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a generalized Hamiltonian formalism for spatially nonlocal field theories whose Lagrangian densities depend explicitly on both the local field and its spatially reflected counterpart. Starting from a generalized variational principle, we derive generalized Euler-Lagrange equations and introduce a generalized functional derivative that consistently accounts for reflected-field contributions. The proposed formalism is applied to three spatially nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger equations. For the Ablowitz-Musslimani equation, we construct, to the best of our knowledge, the first standard Lagrangian density formulated directly in terms of the complex fields and derive its complete Hamiltonian formulation. The same framework is subsequently applied to the two Lagrangian nonlocal nonlinear Schrödinger equations introduced by Velasco-Juan and Fujioka, yielding consistent Hamiltonian formulations that reproduce the corresponding generalized Euler-Lagrange equations. These results establish a unified Hamiltonian framework for a broad class of spatially nonlocal nonlinear field theories.

</details>
