### [137] [Interpolation of Gaussian Free Fields via Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20707)
*Gabriel Raposo*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类双参数广义高斯场（generalized Gaussian fields），其插值于高斯自由场（Gaussian Free Field）、条件高斯自由场以及带独立噪声的高斯自由场之间；该场族亦刻画为某双参数 Wigner 随机矩阵变体的角过程（corner process）高度函数的涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅呈现构造与刻画目标。可推断其意图在于统一理解若干已有高斯场（含条件场与带噪场）之间的连续过渡关系，并建立其与随机矩阵角过程的联系。

Method: 构造双参数族广义高斯场，使其在参数取值变化时插值于高斯自由场、条件高斯自由场及带独立噪声的高斯自由场。依据参数取值，将该广义高斯场刻画为高斯自由场前两个傅里叶模式（Fourier modes）受扰动后的部分条件化（partial conditionings）。

Result: 利用参数取值给出了该广义高斯场作为带扰动的自由场部分条件化的刻画；并证明该场族可作为双参数 Wigner 随机矩阵变体的角过程所对应高度函数的涨落（fluctuations）而出现。

Conclusion: 该双参数广义高斯场族在自由场、条件自由场与带噪自由场之间建立统一插值，并通过扰动前两个傅里叶模式的部分条件化加以刻画，同时与双参数 Wigner 随机矩阵变体的角过程涨落相联系。

Abstract: In this paper we construct a two parameter family of generalized Gaussian fields which interpolates between the Gaussian Free Field, conditioned Gaussian Free Fields, and Gaussian Free Fields with independent noise. Depending on the values of the parameters, we characterize these generalized Gaussian fields as partial conditionings of a Gaussian Free Field with perturbations of its first two Fourier modes. We show that this family arises as the fluctuations of the height function associated with the corner process of a two parameter variant of Wigner random matrices.

</details>


### [138] [Independence Threshold for Collision Times of Many Planar Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21937)
*Ziyang Liu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbb Z^2$ 上多个独立简单随机游走（simple random walk）的碰撞时间（collision time），通过两两碰撞局部时（collision local time）的联合矩母函数（joint moment generating function）刻画其依赖性，并识别出从独立到依赖转变的阈值 $\asymp (\log N)^{1/3}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果指出，当游走数目固定时，经适当对数归一化（logarithmic normalisation）后碰撞时间渐近独立。本文关注这一独立性在游走数目随游走长度 $N$ 增长时能维持到何种程度，即确定独立性失效的临界规模。

Method: 证明结合混沌展开（chaos expansion）技术与一个相关性不等式（correlation inequality）；该相关性不等式是局部极限定理（local limit theorem）的推论结果。

Result: 以 $N$ 表示游走长度，结果确定了一个阈值 $\asymp (\log N)^{1/3}$，独立性向依赖性的转变即发生于该阈值处。

Conclusion: 多随机游走碰撞时间的渐近独立性仅在游走数目低于约 $(\log N)^{1/3}$ 的规模下成立，超过该阈值则出现依赖性；阈值的精确刻画由混沌展开与相关性不等式共同给出。

Abstract: We study collision times of many independent simple random walks on $\mathbb Z^2$ through the joint moment generating function of their pairwise collision local times. For a fixed number of walks, these collision times are known to be asymptotically independent after a suitable logarithmic normalisation. We investigate the extent to which this independence persists when the number of walks grows. For $N$ being the walk length, our results identify a threshold $\asymp (\log N)^{\frac{1}{3}}$, on which the transition from independence to dependence happens. The proofs combine chaos expansion techniques and a correlation inequality, which is the result of a local limit theorem.

</details>


### [139] [A variational approach for the 2D Abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21972)
*Nikolay Barashkov,Ajay Chandra,Ilya Chevyrev,Andreas Koller,Abdulwahab Mohamed*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文利用 Boué-Dupuis 变分法（variational method）在二维环面上构造了具有多项式希格斯势的 Abel 型 Yang-Mills-Higgs 测度，据作者所知是首次以该方法严格构造相互作用规范理论。同时证明了联合规范-希格斯场的指数可积性估计、规范协变性，以及正则化测度 Laplace 变换的收敛性和半经典极限下规范不变可观测量的大偏差原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为相互作用规范理论（interacting gauge theory）提供一种基于变分方法的新型严格构造途径，弥补该方法此前未被用于此类构造的空白。选取二维环面与多项式希格斯势（polynomial Higgs potentials）作为可处理的设定。

Method: 采用 Boué-Dupuis 变分方法，分两步进行：先在给定规范场条件下构造希格斯场（Higgs field），参考测度取协变高斯自由场（covariant Gaussian free field）；再将希格斯场积分掉，得到规范场上的权重，并通过第二次变分方法加以控制。

Result: 成功构造出 Abel 型 Yang-Mills-Higgs 测度（Abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs measure），并证明关于联合规范-希格斯场的新指数可积性估计（exponential integrability estimates）及规范协变性（gauge covariance）；此外证明了正则化测度 Laplace 变换的收敛性，并建立了半经典极限（semiclassical limit）下规范不变可观测量的新大偏差原理（large-deviation principle）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 Boué-Dupuis 变分方法可有效用于严格构造相互作用规范理论，并通过两步变分与协变参考测度的组合实现了对规范场权重的控制。所建立的指数可积性、Laplace 变换收敛与大偏差原理为后续分析提供了工具。

Abstract: We use the Boué-Dupuis variational method to construct the Abelian Yang-Mills-Higgs measure on the two-dimensional torus with polynomial Higgs potentials, giving, to our knowledge, the first rigorous construction of an interacting gauge theory by this approach. We first use the variational method to construct the Higgs field conditioned on the gauge field, with a covariant Gaussian free field as the reference measure. Integrating out the Higgs field produces a weight on the gauge field, which we control through a second application of the variational method. We also prove new exponential integrability estimates for the joint gauge-Higgs field and establish gauge covariance. Finally, we prove convergence of the Laplace transforms of the regularised measures and establish a new large-deviation principle for gauge-invariant observables in the semiclassical limit.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Bootstrapping Disordered Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20916)
*Yaprak Önder,Michael G. Scheer,Minjae Cho,Eslam Khalaf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种自举（bootstrap）框架，对含淬火无序（quenched disorder）量子系统的基态可观测量给出严格双侧界，无需采样无序实现，并可推广至多副本算符以约束无序涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需对无序实现进行采样（sampling），因而难以利用仅在平均意义上成立的对称性，也无法直接给出严格界。该工作旨在建立不依赖采样的严格方法，用于无序量子系统的基态观测。

Method: 将算符代数（operator algebra）扩展至包含经典无序变量，并通过一组矩（moments）编码无序分布；直接约束无序平均期望值，适用于有限或无限格点系统及任意无序分布，并可利用多副本算符（multi-replica operators）获取无序涨落（如方差）。

Result: 应用于一维随机横场伊辛模型（random transverse-field Ising model, RTFIM），涵盖离散、均匀与高斯无序，得到无序平均基态能量密度与短程自旋-自旋关联函数（spin-spin correlators）的紧致界，以及能量密度无序方差的界；并以上下界之差（自举间隙，bootstrap gap）刻画相图。

Conclusion: 自举间隙在弱无序的顺磁相与铁磁相内小且平，进入 Griffiths 区域后增大，并在临界线上达到峰值，表明该间隙可作为识别 RTFIM 相图及其相变结构的有效诊断量。

Abstract: We present a bootstrap framework that yields rigorous two-sided bounds on ground-state observables of quantum systems with quenched disorder. The key idea is to extend the operator algebra to include classical disorder variables and to encode the disorder distribution through a set of moments. The method directly bounds disorder-averaged expectation values for finite or infinite lattice systems and for arbitrary disorder distributions. In contrast to conventional approaches, we do not need to sample disorder realizations, which enables us to make thorough use of symmetries that hold only on average rather than for each disorder realization. Additionally, we show that multi-replica operators can be used to access disorder fluctuations such as the variance of an observable. We apply the method to the one-dimensional random transverse-field Ising model (RTFIM) with discrete, uniform, and Gaussian disorder, obtaining tight bounds on the disorder-averaged ground-state energy density and short-range spin-spin correlators, as well as bounds on the disorder variance of the energy density. Additionally, we show that the difference between the upper and lower bounds on the disorder-averaged ground-state energy density, which we term the bootstrap gap, can be used to map out the phase diagram of the RTFIM. The bootstrap gap is small and flat within the paramagnetic and ferromagnetic phases at weak disorder, grows upon entering the Griffiths regions, and peaks along the critical line.

</details>


### [141] [Shape Deformation and Braid Statistics of Fractional Quantum Hall Quasiparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20985)
*Mytraya Gattu,J. K. Jain*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文分析分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall, FQH）准粒子绕行另一准粒子时贝里相位（Berry phase）的构成，指出其包含阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆相位、形状依赖项与编织统计项三部分，后者可能被前两者淹没，从而给编织统计的精确测定带来困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想任意子（ideal anyons）的贝里相位与闭合回路的大小和形状无关，可直接给出编织统计（braid statistics）。但FQH准粒子（quasiparticles, QPs）带电且具有有限尺寸，其形状并非拓扑性质并随局域势变化，因此需要重新审视贝里相位的组成及其对编织统计提取的影响。

Method: 通过分析分数带电准粒子绕另一准粒子回路的贝里相位，将其分解为三个不同贡献：阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆相位（Aharonov-Bohm phase）$Θ_{\rm AB}$、形状依赖项 $Θ_{\rm shape}$ 与编织统计项 $Θ_{\rm braid}$，并比较各项的相对大小。随后在干涉实验（interference experiments）的语境中讨论这些结果。

Result: 贝里相位满足 $Θ=Θ_{\rm AB}+Θ_{\rm shape}+Θ_{\rm braid}$。其中 $Θ_{\rm AB}$ 占主导，与回路面积成正比；本文识别出的形状依赖项 $Θ_{\rm shape}$ 可能大于编织统计的序一（order-one）贡献 $Θ_{\rm braid}$。由于轨迹与形状存在实际难以探测的不确定性，编织统计的精确测定具有挑战性，可能被这些不确定性淹没。

Conclusion: 编织统计的精确测量因形状与轨迹的不确定性而困难。同时指出，实验中观察到的分数相位跳变（fractional phase jumps）无需假设FQH体系边缘存在分数电荷严格量子化的准粒子即可理解；这些相位跳变直接度量了FQH态体内准粒子的分数量子化涡度（fractionally quantized vorticity）。

Abstract: For ``ideal anyons,'' the Berry phase associated with a closed loop of an anyon around another is robust, that is, independent of the size or the shape of the loop, and directly yields the braid statistics. That is not the case for the fractional quantum Hall (FQH) quasiparticles (QPs), which are charged and have finite size. We consider here how the Berry phase depends on the shape of the QP, which, unlike its charge, is not a topological property and varies along the path in response to the local potential. We show that the Berry phase $Θ$ associated with the loop of a fractionally charged QP around another contains three distinct contributions: $Θ=Θ_{\rm AB}+Θ_{\rm shape}+Θ_{\rm braid}$. The Aharonov-Bohm phase $Θ_{\rm AB}$ is dominant, being proportional to the area of the loop, and the shape-dependent term $Θ_{\rm shape}$, identified in this work, can be larger than the order-one contribution from the braid statistics $Θ_{\rm braid}$. A precise determination of the braid statistics is challenging because it can be swamped by practically undetectable uncertainties in its trajectory and shape. We discuss these results in the context of the interference experiments. We also note that the fractional phase jumps in these experiments can be understood without assuming the existence of QPs with sharply quantized fractional charges at the edges of the FQH system, wherein these phase jumps are a direct measure of the fractionally quantized vorticity of the QPs in the bulk of the fractional quantum Hall state.

</details>


### [142] [Resonant spin mode from retarded relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21623)
*J. Beater,G. S. Uhrig*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文提出用延迟林德布拉德弛豫（retarded Lindbladian relaxation）解释超快自旋动力学（ultrafast spin dynamics）中常规朗道-利夫希茨方程（Landau-Lifshitz equations）无法描述的太赫兹（THz）附加模式，并与实验数据取得良好吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快自旋动力学中出现的太赫兹频段附加模式无法由常规朗道-利夫希茨方程捕捉，而通过引入更高阶时间导数（惯性朗道-利夫希茨-吉尔伯特方程，inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation）进行扩展仅能部分解决问题，因此需要新的理论解释框架。

Method: 采用现象学方法，以延迟林德布拉德弛豫（retarded Lindbladian relaxation）作为解释机制，用于描述这些附加模式的来源。

Result: 尽管该现象学方法较为简洁，其与实验数据的一致性具有前景：物理上合理的参数能够描述这些共振的位置及其较大的线宽，且多条近乎等间距共振峰这一显著特征与实验证据相符。

Conclusion: 延迟林德布拉德弛豫可作为解释超快自旋动力学中太赫兹附加共振模式的有效现象学途径，其预测的共振位置、大线宽及近等间距峰结构与实验观测一致。

Abstract: In ultrafast spin dynamics additional modes in the THz range appear which cannot be captured by conventional Landau-Lifshitz equations. Extending these equations by further time derivatives (inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation) solves the issue only partly. Here we suggest to explain these modes by retarded Lindbladian relaxation. In spite of the simplicity of this phenomenological approach the agreement with the experimental data is promising: physically plausible parameters describe the position and the large width of these resonances. The salient feature of several almost equidistant resonance peaks is in-line with experimental evidence as well.

</details>


### [143] [Reformulating Pfaffian Quantum Monte Carlo with the Hybrid Monte Carlo formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21631)
*Thomas Hauschild,Lin Wang,Finn L. Temmen,Thomas Luu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将 Pfaffian 量子蒙特卡洛（Pfaffian quantum Monte Carlo）与混合蒙特卡洛（Hybrid Monte Carlo, HMC）的全局更新相结合，提出复杂度为 O(N_τ L^3) 的算法，并将其应用于相互作用 Kitaev 链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pfaffian 量子蒙特卡洛将辅助场方法推广到反常费米子哈密顿量，可处理行列式方法无法描述的配对体系及相应 Hubbard-Stratonovich 分解。作者希望将这种灵活性同 HMC 的全局更新结合，以提升采样效率。

Method: 核心观察是分子动力学力（molecular dynamics force）仅依赖 Pfaffian 权重的大小，而该权重可写为行列式表示，从而得到简化的力表达式；对无便利解析指数导数的相互作用核，采用截断力展开（truncated force expansion），其误差由精确的 Metropolis 接受-拒绝步骤消除。

Result: 算法复杂度为 O(N_τ L^3)，与不含伪费米子（pseudofermions）的行列式量子蒙特卡洛（DQMC）相当；该构造还能以几乎零额外开销给出不等时 Majorana 关联函数（unequal-time Majorana correlators）。在相互作用 Kitaev 链上，结果与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）一致，并分辨出局域于两端边界的低能模及其在拓扑相变附近逐渐增大的重叠。

Conclusion: 结果确立了 HMC 作为 Pfaffian 辅助场模拟的高效采样框架。

Abstract: Pfaffian quantum Monte Carlo extends auxiliary field methods to anomalous fermionic Hamiltonians, opening access to pairing systems and Hubbard-Stratonovich decompositions that are unavailable in determinant formulations. We show that this flexibility can be combined with the global updates of Hybrid Monte Carlo (HMC), leading to an algorithm scaling as $\mathcal{O}(N_τL^3)$, similar to its determinant quantum Monte Carlo (DQMC) counterpart without pseudofermions. The central observation is that the molecular dynamics force depends only on the magnitude of the Pfaffian weight, which admits a determinant representation and leads to a simplified force expression. This construction also yields unequal-time Majorana correlators at essentially no additional cost. For interaction kernels with no convenient analytic exponential derivative, we employ a truncated force expansion whose errors are removed by the exact Metropolis accept-reject step. Applied to the interacting Kitaev chain, the method agrees with exact diagonalization and resolves low-energy modes localized at opposite boundaries, together with their increasing overlap near the topological transition. These results establish HMC as an efficient sampling framework for Pfaffian auxiliary-field simulations.

</details>


### [144] [Orbital character and photoemission signatures of the A-pocket in infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21665)
*Leonard M. Verhoff,Michael Seidl,Liang Si,Karsten Held*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合密度泛函理论与动力学平均场理论，研究无限层镍酸盐 NdNiO₂ 中 A 口袋（A-pocket）的轨道特征，指出该口袋是间隙 s 轨道、Nd-d_xy 与 Ni-d_xz/yz 强杂化的结果，并强调光电子矩阵元与轨道杂化须同时考虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐中 A 口袋的轨道来源（orbital character）仍存在争议，其微观归属尚未明确，因此需要更系统的理论与实验对照研究。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）与动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）相结合的方法研究体相 NdNiO₂，并进行角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）模拟，配合矩阵元（matrix element）逐项调控分析。

Result: A 口袋被识别为强杂化态，包含显著的间隙 s（interstitial-s）、Nd-d_xy 和 Ni-d_xz/yz 贡献；模拟再现了实验测量的主要偏振依赖特征。移除间隙 s 矩阵元后与实测费米面（Fermi surface）图明显不符，而抑制 Nd-d_xy 或 Ni-d_xz/yz 矩阵元几乎不改变图谱，因这些矩阵元在布里渊区角（zone corner）处很小。

Conclusion: 在无限层镍酸盐中判定谱特征（spectral features）的微观起源，必须将轨道杂化（orbital hybridization）与光电子矩阵元一并处理，二者缺一不可。

Abstract: The orbital character of the so-called $A$-pocket in infinite-layer nickelates remains under debate. We investigate bulk NdNiO$_2$ using density-functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory and identify the $A$-pocket as a strongly hybridized state with substantial interstitial-$s$, Nd-$d_{xy}$ and Ni-d_${xz/yz}$ contributions. Our angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy simulations reproduce the main polarization-dependent features measured experimentally. Removing the interstitial-$s$ matrix element leads to a clear disagreement with the measured Fermi surface maps, even though the interstitial-$s$ contribution is not dominant. Suppressing instead the Nd-d$_{xy}$ or Ni-$_{dxz/yz}$ matrix elements leaves the maps almost unchanged, because these are small at the zone corner. More generally, our results demonstrate that assigning the microscopic origin of spectral features in infinite-layer nickelates requires treating orbital hybridizations and photoemission matrix elements together.

</details>


### [145] [Correlation enhanced magnetism at a superconducting cuprate/manganite interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22051)
*Vitor A. M. Lima,Marcello Civelli,Walber Hugo de Brito*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于铜氧化物-锰氧化物异质结实验，采用团簇动力学平均场理论研究界面交换场下的关联双层哈伯德模型，发现超导与界面磁性可共存，且强关联下超导反而增强界面磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由铜氧化物-锰氧化物异质结（cuprate-manganite heterostructures）的实验研究驱动，探讨界面交换场与超导、磁性的相互作用。

Method: 在欠掺杂铜氧化物（underdoped cuprate）区，采用团簇动力学平均场理论（cluster dynamical mean-field theory）研究受局域界面交换场作用的关联双层哈伯德模型（correlated bilayer Hubbard model）。

Result: 超导与锰氧化物诱导的Cu自旋极化（Cu spin polarization）共存；在整个共存区，超导解比相应约束正常态（constrained normal state）具有更大磁矩，该反转由关联驱动；更强交换场下超导突然坍缩，呈一级相变（first-order transition）特征的滞后，并出现强自旋极化赝能隙（pseudogap）。

Conclusion: 在强电子关联（strong electronic correlations）下，超导可以增强而非抑制界面磁性（interfacial magnetism）。

Abstract: Motivated by experimental studies on cuprate-manganite heterostructures, we study a correlated bilayer Hubbard model subject to a local interfacial exchange field using cluster dynamical mean-field theory in the underdoped cuprate regime. We find that superconductivity coexists with a manganite-induced Cu spin polarization. Remarkably, throughout this coexistence regime, the superconducting solution carries a larger magnetic moment than the corresponding constrained normal state. Analysis of the magnetic response shows that this reversal is driven by correlations, which enhance magnetism already in the normal state and even more strongly in the superconducting solution. At stronger exchange fields, superconductivity collapses abruptly, displaying hysteresis consistent with a first-order transition, and a strongly spin-polarized pseudogap emerges. These results show that, under strong electronic correlations, superconductivity can enhance rather than suppress interfacial magnetism.

</details>

Wresilo,W. Wu,E. Yandel,T. Yang,L. E. Yates,H. W. Yu,G. P. Zeller,J. Zennamo,C. Zhang,Y. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次报道电子中微子（electron neutrino）与氩（argon）相互作用中，通量积分截面（flux-integrated cross section）随运动学失衡变量（kinematic imbalance variables）变化的测量结果，并将数据与多个中微子事件发生器（neutrino event generator）配置进行比较，发现模型与数据在预期不易受末态相互作用（final-state interactions）主导的区域存在张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动学失衡变量对费米运动（Fermi motion）、多核子过程（multinucleon processes）与末态相互作用等因素具有敏感性，可作为研究中微子-核（neutrino-nucleus）相互作用的细致探针；此类变量此前尚未应用于电子中微子相互作用，故需开展首次测量。

Method: 使用费米国家加速器实验室（Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory）助推器中微子束（Booster Neutrino Beam）下 MicroBooNE 的完整数据集，对应 1.3×10²¹ 质子打靶数（protons on target），对电子中微子在氩上的相互作用进行通量积分截面测量，并将结果与多种中微子事件发生器配置作对比。

Result: 获得了电子中微子-氩相互作用中通量积分截面随运动学失衡变量变化的测量结果，且在与多种事件发生器配置比较时，在预期末态相互作用主导程度较低的区域观测到模型与数据之间的张力。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将运动学失衡变量应用于电子中微子相互作用，所提供的截面测量为检验和改进中微子-核相互作用模型提供了新约束，尤其揭示了现有事件发生器在末态相互作用较弱区域的不足。

Abstract: We report the first measurement of flux-integrated cross sections as a function of kinematic imbalance variables for electron neutrino interactions on argon. Kinematic imbalance variables, applied here to electron neutrino interactions for the first time, are particularly interesting due to their sensitivity to effects such as Fermi motion, multinucleon processes, and final-state interactions, providing a detailed lens to study neutrino-nucleus interactions. These measurements are performed using MicroBooNE's full dataset of $1.3 \times 10^{21}$ protons on target from the Booster Neutrino Beam at the Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory. The measured cross sections are compared to several neutrino event generator configurations, revealing tensions between these models and data in the regions that are expected to be less dominated by final-state interactions.

</details>


### [147] [Dark photons in exotic Higgs boson decays at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21648)
*Axel Gallén,Sarah Ben Abdesselem,Giulia Ripellino,Rebeca Gonzalez Suarez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 针对未来环形对撞机电子-正电子阶段（FCC-ee），提出暗光子（dark photon）衰变为准直、位移双缪子（displaced muon pairs）的前瞻性搜寻，基于240 GeV希格斯运行的全部亮度，通过运动学选择实现零本底并给出希格斯玻色子衰变分支比上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光子作为暗扇区（dark sector）的可能媒介粒子，其探测是超出标准模型物理的重要方向；FCC-ee的高亮度与希格斯运行提供了独特探针条件，而现有对撞机对此类位移、准直末态的覆盖有限。

Method: 假设FCC-ee在240 GeV希格斯运行的全部亮度，考察Z玻色子衰变产生的两个喷流（jets）与希格斯衰变产生的两个暗光子各自衰变为缪子对所形成的特征信号；采用基于运动学（kinematics-based）的选择判据进行事件筛选。

Result: 基于运动学的选择在保持高信号效率的同时实现零本底（zero background），并在较宽的暗光子质量与耦合范围内给出预期灵敏度，得到希格斯玻色子衰变为暗光子的分支比（branching ratio）随暗光子质量与耦合变化的预期上限。

Conclusion: 该前瞻性研究表明FCC-ee希格斯运行可用于系统探测暗光子参数空间，零本底与高效率的结合使分支比上限具有较强约束力，凸显FCC-ee在暗扇区搜寻中的潜力。

Abstract: A prospective search for dark photons decaying into collimated, displaced muon pairs is presented for the electron-positron stage of the Future Circular Collider, FCC-ee. Assuming the full luminosity of the Higgs run at 240 GeV, the study probes a broad range of dark photon masses and couplings. The signature comprises two jets from a Z boson decay and two muon pairs from dark photons produced in Higgs decays. A kinematics-based selection achieves zero background while retaining high signal efficiency. Expected limits are set on the Higgs boson branching ratio to dark photons as a function of the dark photon mass and coupling.

</details>


### [148] [Search for resonant and nonresonant production of pairs of dijet resonances with b jets in the final state in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21850)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS 利用 136 fb⁻¹ 的 13 TeV 质子-质子碰撞数据，首次搜寻两个同质量、各自衰变为 b 夸克与轻味夸克的全分辨双喷注共振对，并同时给出非共振与经中间共振态产生情形的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该分析针对末态含 b 喷注的四喷注与双喷注共振产生，填补此前 LHC 未搜寻过“成对全分辨双喷注共振（其中所有共振均为非标准模型态）”的空白，并扩展 R 宇称破坏（R-parity-violating）衰变至奇异夸克与 b 夸克的搜寻。

Method: 使用 CMS 探测器在质心能 √s = 13 TeV 下采集的、积分亮度为 136 fb⁻¹ 的质子-质子碰撞数据，对成对产生、等质量且各自衰变为 b 夸克与轻味夸克的双喷注共振进行搜寻，结果分别按非共振产生与经中间共振态产生两种情形给出。

Result: 非共振产生情形中最显著超出出现在平均双喷注质量 1.0 TeV 处，局域显著性为 2.1 个标准差；共振产生情形中最显著超出出现在四喷注共振质量 3.1 TeV、双喷注共振质量 1.3 TeV 处，局域显著性为 2.6 个标准差。结果排除了质量在 0.5 至 0.8 TeV 之间的 top squark 成对产生，并在 95% 置信水平下对 diquark-to-diquarks 模型设限，排除 2 至 7 TeV 之间较重的 diquark 质量。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在 LHC 上针对末态含 b 喷注、成对全分辨双喷注共振（均为非标准模型态）的共振产生进行搜寻，报告了四喷注与双喷注共振产生的上限，并对 top squark 成对产生及 diquark-to-diquarks 模型给出排除区间。

Abstract: A search is presented for pair-produced dijet resonances of identical mass, each decaying into a bottom (b) quark and a light-flavor quark. The search uses a data sample corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 136 fb$^{-1}$ collected with the CMS detector in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV. Results are presented separately for nonresonant production and for the case where the four-jet production proceeds via an intermediate resonant state. Upper limits are reported on the production of four-jet and dijet resonances with b jets in the final state. This is the first LHC search for resonant production of pairs of fully resolved dijet resonances with b jets in the final state, in which all resonances considered correspond to non-standard-model states. For nonresonant production, the most significant excess occurs at an average dijet mass of 1.0 TeV, with a local significance of 2.1 standard deviations. Additionally, the results exclude pair production of top squarks with masses between 0.5 and 0.8 TeV, extending previous searches for $R$-parity-violating decays to strange and b quarks. In the search for resonant production, the most significant excess occurs at a four-jet resonance mass of 3.1 TeV and a dijet resonance mass of 1.3 TeV, with a local significance of 2.6 standard deviations. The results are also used to set limits at 95% confidence level on the diquark-to-diquarks model and exclude the heavier diquark masses between 2 and 7 TeV.

</details>

TL;DR: 利用 LHCb 升级探测器 2024 年采集的 6.3 fb⁻¹ 质子-质子对撞数据，在 Ω⁰_cπ⁺ 质量谱中观测到全局显著性 8.7σ 的峰结构，并将其确认为双粲重子 Ω⁺_cc，测得质量为 3725.9 ± 1.0 (stat) ± 0.2 (syst) ± 0.4 (lifetime) ± 0.6 (ext) MeV/c²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双粲重子（doubly charmed baryon）Ω⁺_cc 的寻找是重味物理（heavy-flavour physics）的重要课题，此前尚未在 Ω⁰_cπ⁺ 衰变道中被确认。该搜索旨在拓展对双粲重子谱的认识。

Method: 使用升级后的 LHCb 探测器于 2024 年在 13.6 TeV 质心能量（center-of-mass energy）下采集的、积分亮度（integrated luminosity）为 6.3 fb⁻¹ 的质子-质子对撞数据，在 Ω⁰_cπ⁺ 衰变道中搜索 Ω⁺_cc，其中 Ω⁰_c 通过 pK⁻K⁻π⁺ 末态重建。

Result: 在 Ω⁰_cπ⁺ 质量谱中观测到全局显著性（global significance）为 8.7σ 的峰结构，该结构符合来自弱衰变（weakly decaying）粒子的特征，被识别为双粲重子 Ω⁺_cc。其质量测定为 3725.9 ± 1.0 (stat) ± 0.2 (syst) ± 0.4 (lifetime) ± 0.6 (ext) MeV/c²，其中第三项不确定度源于选择偏置对未知 Ω⁺_cc 寿命的依赖，第四项源于 Ω⁰_c、Ξ⁺_c 与 Ξ⁺⁺_cc 重子质量的不确定度。

Conclusion: 在 Ω⁰_cπ⁺ 衰变道中确认了双粲重子 Ω⁺_cc 的存在，并给出了精确的质量测量值，同时明确区分了统计、系统、寿命相关及外部质量输入等四类不确定度来源。

Abstract: A search for the doubly charmed baryon $\varOmega^+_{cc}$ in the $\varOmega^0_cπ^+$ decay channel is performed using proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6.3\text{fb}^{-1}$, collected with the upgraded LHCb detector in 2024 at a center-of-mass energy of 13.6$\text{TeV}$. A peaking structure with a global significance of $8.7σ$ is observed in the $\varOmega^0_cπ^+$ mass spectrum, where the $\varOmega^0_c$ baryon is reconstructed in the $pK^-K^-π^+$ final state. The structure is consistent with originating from a weakly decaying particle and is identified as the doubly charmed baryon $\varOmega^+_{cc}$. Its mass is determined to be $3725.9 \pm 1.0 \,(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.2 \,(\mathrm{syst}) \pm 0.4 \,(\mathrm{lifetime}) \pm 0.6 \,(\mathrm{ext})\,\text{MeV/}c^2$, where the third uncertainty arises from the dependence of the selection-induced bias on the unknown $\varOmega^+_{cc}$ lifetime, and the fourth is due to the uncertainties on the masses of the $\varOmega^0_c$, $\varXi^+_c$, and $\varXi^{++}_{cc}$ baryons.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于Belle II探测器收集的$387 \times 10^6$个$\Upsilon(4S)$衰变事例，采用强子标记方法测量分支比比值$R(D^{(*)})$，结果为$R(D^{*})=0.242\pm0.019(\mathrm{stat})\pm0.016(\mathrm{syst})$、$R(D)=0.439\pm0.055(\mathrm{stat})\pm0.046(\mathrm{syst})$，与标准模型预言及先前测量一致，是目前强子标记下最精确的$R(D^{(*)})$测定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在检验轻子味普适性（lepton flavor universality），通过测量$\bar{B}\rightarrow D^{(*)}\tau^{-}\bar{\nu}_{\tau}$与$\bar{B}\rightarrow D^{(*)}\ell^{-}\bar{\nu}_{\ell}$分支比的比值$R(D^{(*)})$，检验标准模型（standard model）在这一过程中的预言是否成立。

Method: 分析使用Belle II探测器在能量非对称$e^+e^-$对撞中采集的$387\times 10^6$个$\Upsilon(4S)$衰变。一个$B$介子通过强子衰变模式被完全重建（hadronic tagging），另一个则重建为$\bar{B}\rightarrow D^{(*)}\tau^{-}\bar{\nu}_{\tau}$（其中$\tau^-\rightarrow \ell^-\bar{\nu}_{\ell}\nu_{\tau}$）或$\bar{B}\rightarrow D^{(*)}\ell^{-}\bar{\nu}_{\ell}$。信号通过剩余量热器能量（residual calorimeter energy）与未探测粒子的质量平方分布提取。

Result: 测得$R(D^{*}) = 0.242 \pm 0.019(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.016(\mathrm{syst})$以及$R(D) = 0.439 \pm 0.055(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.046(\mathrm{syst})$。该结果与标准模型预言和先前测量均保持一致。

Conclusion: 该测量构成了采用强子标记（hadronic tagging）方法得到的$R(D^{(*)})$最精确测定，并支持轻子味普适性在标准模型框架下的表现。

Abstract: We test lepton flavor universality with a measurement of the branching-fraction ratios $R(D^{(*)}) \equiv \mathcal{B}(\bar{B} \rightarrow D^{(*)} τ^{-} \barν_τ)/\mathcal{B}(\bar{B} \rightarrow D^{(*)} \ell^{-} \barν_{\ell})$, where $\ell$ denotes an electron or muon. The analysis uses $387\times 10^6$ $Υ(\mathrm{4S})$ decays collected with the Belle II detector in energy-asymmetric $e^+e^-$ collisions. One $B$ meson is fully reconstructed in a hadronic decay mode, while the other is reconstructed either in $\bar{B}\rightarrow D^{(*)}τ^{-}\barν_τ$, with $τ^- \rightarrow \ell^- \barν_{\ell}ν_τ$, or in $\bar{B}\rightarrow D^{(*)}\ell^{-}\barν_{\ell}$. We extract the signal from the distributions of the residual calorimeter energy and the squared mass of the undetected particles, obtaining $R(D^{*}) = 0.242 \pm0.019(\mathrm{stat}) \pm0.016(\mathrm{syst})$ and $R(D) = 0.439 \pm 0.055(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.046(\mathrm{syst})$. These results are consistent with both the standard model predictions and previous measurements, and constitute the most precise determination of $R(D^{(*)})$ with hadronic tagging.

</details>
